Archie Comics Style（阿奇漫画风格）：一种干净、wholesome 的卡通插画风格，以自信均匀重量的轮廓定义简化、吸引人的比例，除基本形体定义阴影外平涂明亮的纯色填充，通过圆润友好面部设计承载中叶世纪报纸 strips 的易接近魅力，优先瞬时可读性而非渲染复杂度的欢快、不复杂构图；永恒、全美 wholesome 的 timeless、毫不费力友好能量。受影响于：Dan DeCarlo、中叶世纪美式漫画 strips 传统、平涂漫画墨线技法、报纸连载卡通实践。

Bande Dessinée (Hergé/Goscinny Tradition): A clean, confident ligne-claire illustration style built on uniform, unmodulated contour lines holding clearly bounded, evenly flat-colored shapes, minimal internal shading that lets crisp linework and bold color carry the entire compositional weight, meticulous, precise architectural and environmental detail rendered with the same graphic clarity as the figures themselves, a measured, orderly compositional rhythm favoring readability and charm over painterly nuance, and a timeless, wholesome European adventure-comic sincerity that feels meticulous, warm, and effortlessly classic. Influenced by: Hergé, René Goscinny, ligne-claire illustration technique, Franco-Belgian bande dessinée tradition.
Bande Dessinée (Hergé/Goscinny Tradition)（清晰线条/丁丁传统）：一种干净、自信的 ligne-claire（清晰线条）插画风格，以均匀、无变化的轮廓线固定边界清晰、均匀平涂的形状，最少内部阴影让 crisp 线条与 bold 色彩承载整个构图重量，以与人物本身同等的图形清晰度刻画的精细、精确建筑与环境细节，优先可读性与魅力而非绘画微妙度的 measured、有序构图节奏；精细、温暖、毫不费力经典的永恒、wholesome 欧洲冒险漫画真诚感。受影响于：Hergé、René Goscinny、ligne-claire 插画技法、法比 bande dessinée 传统。

Mark Brooks / Manapul Style: A refined modern comic illustration style merging crisp, glossy semi-realistic linework with loose, atmospheric painterly washes, confident anatomical precision balanced against fluid, watercolor-like softness bleeding through backgrounds and costume, delicate cross-hatched shading giving skin and fabric a tactile, hand-finished quality, dynamic yet quietly cinematic posing that favors mood over bombast, and a polished, contemporary mainstream-comic sensibility that bridges tight illustrative control with expressive brushwork. Influenced by: Mark Brooks, Francis Manapul, modern mainstream comic illustration, watercolor comic-art technique.
Mark Brooks / Manapul Style（马克·布鲁克斯/马纳普尔风格）：一种精炼的现代漫画插画风格，将 crisp、glossy 的半写实线条与松散、氛围的绘画性水洗相融合，自信的解剖精度与透过背景与服装 bleed 的流体、水彩般柔软相平衡，细腻交叉排线阴影赋予皮肤与织物 tactile、手工完成的质感，优先氛围而非浮夸的动态却 quietly 电影感的姿态；桥接紧密插画控制与表现性笔触的抛光、当代主流漫画气质。受影响于：Mark Brooks、Francis Manapul、现代主流漫画插画、水彩漫画艺术技法。

Marvel Comics Style: A bold, dynamic mainstream superhero comic illustration style built on confident heavy inking over energetic, muscular figure work, punchy primary color palettes laid in flat-to-cel-shaded color with sharp graphic contrast, dramatic action-driven poses and foreshortening designed for kinetic panel-to-panel storytelling, crisp halftone-inspired texture and bold speed lines heightening momentum, and an iconic, larger-than-life heroic energy instantly recognizable as classic American superhero art. Influenced by: Jack Kirby, John Romita Jr., Marvel Comics house style, Silver Age comic inking technique.
Marvel Comics Style（漫威漫画风格）：一种 bold、动态的超英主流漫画插画风格，以自信重墨线覆盖充满能量的肌肉人物，以 sharp 图形对比平涂到赛璐珞的 punchy 原色配色，为动能分镜叙事设计的戏剧性动作姿态与 foreshortening，强化动量的 crisp 半调纹理与 bold 速度线；instantly 可辨识为经典美式超英艺术的 iconic、超越现实英雄能量。受影响于：Jack Kirby、John Romita Jr.、漫威漫画 house style、白银时代漫画墨线技法。

Marvel Cover Art: A striking, poster-worthy superhero illustration style built for maximum newsstand impact, dramatic hero-forward composition with bold dynamic posing thrust toward the viewer, richly rendered digital painting or ink-and-color hybrid technique giving weight and polish beyond interior panel art, saturated high-contrast color palettes anchored by an iconic focal color, dramatic rim lighting and glowing effects framing the central figure against a moody or explosive backdrop, and a bold, hyper-marketable heroic grandeur designed to sell a single unforgettable image. Influenced by: Alex Ross, J. Scott Campbell, Marvel Comics cover art direction, comic-cover painting technique.
Marvel Cover Art（漫威封面艺术）：一种为最大报摊冲击而生的、poster 级的超英插画风格，以 bold 动态姿态 thrust 向观众的戏剧性英雄前向构图，超越内页分镜权重的丰富数字绘画或墨线-色彩混合技法，以 iconic 焦点色锚定的饱和高对比配色，在阴郁或爆炸背景前框定中心人物的戏剧性边缘光与发光效果；为卖出单一难忘图像而设计的 bold、hyper-marketable 英雄宏伟感。受影响于：Alex Ross、J. Scott Campbell、漫威漫画封面艺术指导、漫画封面绘画技法。

Milo Manara Style: An elegant, sensuous comic-illustration style built on fluid, confident linework that traces the female form with graceful anatomical precision, delicate fine crosshatching used sparingly to suggest shadow and volume rather than heavy rendering, warm sun-bleached Mediterranean color palettes applied in soft watercolor-like washes, playful, flirtatious poses charged with lighthearted erotic tension, and a refined, painterly European comic-book sensuality that feels classic yet unmistakably illustrated. Influenced by: Milo Manara, Italian fumetti comic art, Mediterranean watercolor illustration, erotic graphic-novel linework.
Milo Manara Style（米洛·马纳拉风格）：一种优雅、性感的漫画插画风格，以流畅自信的线条以优雅解剖精度追踪女性形体，稀少使用以暗示阴影与体积而非厚重渲染的细腻细交叉排线，以柔和类水彩水洗应用的温暖阳光暴晒地中海配色，充满轻松情色张力的俏皮、flirtatious 姿态；经典却毋庸置疑插画的精炼、绘画性欧洲漫画感官感。受影响于：Milo Manara、意大利 fumetti 漫画艺术、地中海水彩插画、情色图像小说线条。

Psychological Thriller Pulp Digital Painting: A moody, high-contrast digital painting style built on pulp-novel cover sensibilities, with dramatic chiaroscuro lighting slicing across the face and figure, saturated noir color grading in deep colors, painterly brushwork that keeps edges slightly loose and expressive rather than photoreal, unsettling tilted or claustrophobic composition hinting at hidden threat or obsession, and a tense, voyeuristic atmosphere charged with paranoia and simmering danger. Influenced by: vintage pulp paperback cover art, Drew Struzan, film-noir movie posters, Gregory Manchess-style narrative illustration.
Psychological Thriller Pulp Digital Painting（心理惊悚通俗小说数字绘画）：一种阴郁、高对比的数字绘画风格，以通俗小说封面气质，将戏剧性 chiaroscuro 灯光切过面孔与身形，深色的饱和 noir 色彩分级，保持边缘略松、富有表现力而非照片写实的绘画性笔触，暗示隐藏威胁或执念的 unsettling 倾斜或 claustrophobic 构图，以及充满偏执与 simmering 危险的紧张、偷窥氛围。受影响于：复古通俗平装书封面艺术、Drew Struzan、黑色电影海报、Gregory Manchess 式叙事插画。

DC Comics Style: A bold, dynamic mainstream superhero comic-illustration style built on confident heavy inking over powerful, heroic figure work, punchy saturated color-block application with sharp graphic contrast, dramatic action-driven posing and foreshortening designed for kinetic panel-to-panel storytelling, crisp linework balancing classic heroic silhouette with modern rendering polish, and an iconic, larger-than-life heroic gravitas instantly recognizable as classic mainstream American superhero comics. Influenced by: Jim Lee, Neal Adams, DC Comics house style, mainstream superhero-illustration tradition.
DC Comics Style（DC 漫画风格）：一种 bold、动态的超英主流漫画插画风格，以自信重墨线覆盖有力、英雄的人物，以 sharp 图形对比的 punchy 饱和色块应用，为动能分镜叙事设计的戏剧性动作姿态与 foreshortening，平衡经典英雄剪影与现代渲染抛光的 crisp 线条；instantly 可辨识为经典主流美式超英漫画的 iconic、超越现实英雄庄严感。受影响于：Jim Lee、Neal Adams、DC 漫画 house style、主流超英插画传统。

Zenescope Illustration: polished fantasy-comic aesthetics characterized by premium character-focused rendering, elegant anatomy, contemporary fantasy glamour, intricate ornamental detailing, cinematic presentation, and highly refined digital craftsmanship. The style emphasizes visual impact, clarity, collector-edition quality, and a seamless balance between realism and stylization through sophisticated lighting, luxurious material treatment, dynamic composition, and meticulously rendered textures. Rather than relying on gritty realism or abstraction, it prioritizes beauty, readability, and high-end commercial appeal, producing imagery that feels dramatic, immersive, and immediately marketable. The visual language merges modern comic-book aesthetics with fantasy art sensibilities, creating artwork that is vibrant, aspirational, and visually striking, reminiscent of J. Scott Campbell and Elias Chatzoudis, inspired by Grimm Fairy Tales and Wonderland.
Zenescope Illustration（Zenescope 插画）：抛光奇幻漫画美学，以 premium 以角色为中心的渲染、优雅解剖、当代奇幻 glamour、繁复装饰细节、电影感呈现与高度精炼数字工艺为特征。该风格强调视觉冲击、清晰度、收藏版质量，以及通过成熟灯光、奢华材质处理、动态构图与精细刻画纹理实现的写实与风格化无缝平衡；不依赖粗粝写实或抽象，而优先美、可读性与高端商业吸引力，产出戏剧性、沉浸、即刻可售的图像，其视觉语言将现代漫画美学与奇幻艺术气质融合，创造出 vibrant、aspirational、视觉醒目的作品；令人联想到 J. Scott Campbell 与 Elias Chatzoudis，灵感来自《格林童话》与《爱丽丝梦游仙境》。

Ryan Benjamin Style: A dynamic, high-detail comic illustration style combining gritty painterly digital rendering with powerful, dramatically foreshortened action poses, richly textured musculature and costume detail rendered with sharp directional lighting and deep contrast shadow, moody desaturated backgrounds punctuated by bold accent lighting, confident heavy inking blended into painted color for a weighty, tactile finish, and an intense, larger-than-life comic-book gravitas built for splash-page impact. Influenced by: Ryan Benjamin, comic art, digital comic painting technique, modern cover illustration.
Ryan Benjamin Style（瑞恩·本杰明风格）：一种动态、高细节的漫画插画风格，将粗粝绘画性数字渲染与有力、戏剧性 foreshortening 的动作姿态相结合，以锐利方向光与深对比阴影刻画的丰富纹理肌肉与服装细节，以 bold 强调光点缀的去饱和阴郁背景，自信重墨线融入绘画色彩带来有重量、tactile 的完成度；为 splash-page 冲击而生的 intense、超越现实漫画庄严感。受影响于：Ryan Benjamin、漫画艺术、数字漫画绘画技法、现代封面插画。

Travis Charest Style: A hyper-clean, glossy comic-illustration style built on immaculately smooth gradient rendering that eliminates visible brushwork in favor of glassy precision, confident directional lighting carving sharp, polished highlight across every contour, meticulously controlled reflective sheen giving every form a slick, high-production finish, dynamic yet effortlessly composed posing charged with athletic grace, and a sleek, technically masterful comic-illustration intensity built for instantly striking visual impact. Influenced by: Travis Charest, painted comic-cover illustration tradition, hyper-glossy digital-rendering technique, athletic character-illustration practice.
Travis Charest Style（特拉维斯·查雷斯特风格）：一种超干净、glossy 的漫画插画风格，以完美平滑的渐变渲染消除可见笔触、换取玻璃般精度，自信方向光在每个轮廓刻出锐利抛光的亮部，精细控制的反射光泽赋予每个形态顺滑、高制作的完成度，充满运动优雅、动态却毫不费力地 composed 的姿态；为瞬时醒目视觉冲击而生的利落、技术 masterful 漫画插画强度。受影响于：Travis Charest、绘画性漫画封面传统、超光泽数字渲染技法、运动角色插画实践。

Adi Granov Style: A sleek, hyper-glossy painted comic-illustration style built on immaculately smooth gradient rendering that eliminates visible brushwork in favor of glassy precision, dramatic directional lighting carving crisp, confident highlight across every contour, meticulously controlled reflective sheen giving every form a polished, high-production finish, a restrained, technically disciplined realism balancing painterly depth with graphic clarity, and a sleek, cinematic comic-cover intensity built for instantly recognizable high-gloss impact. Influenced by: Adi Granov, painted comic-cover illustration tradition, hyper-glossy digital-rendering technique, cinematic character-illustration practice.
Adi Granov Style（阿迪·格拉诺夫风格）：一种利落、超光泽的绘画性漫画插画风格，以完美平滑渐变渲染消除可见笔触、换取玻璃般精度，戏剧性方向光在每个轮廓刻出 crisp、自信的亮部，精细控制的反射光泽赋予每个形态抛光、高制作的完成度，平衡绘画性纵深与图形清晰度的克制、技术写实，以及为瞬时可辨识高光泽冲击而生的利落、电影感漫画封面强度。受影响于：Adi Granov、绘画性漫画封面传统、超光泽数字渲染技法、电影感角色插画实践。

Bold Contour Style: graphic illustration aesthetics defined by strong outline emphasis, confident shape definition, clear visual hierarchy, impactful silhouette design, and highly readable forms, prioritizing visual clarity, structural strength, and compositional presence through disciplined line control, simplified volume construction, and deliberate graphic contrast, reminiscent of Mike Mignola and Frank Miller, inspired by Hellboy and Sin City.
Bold Contour Style（粗轮廓风格）：以强轮廓强调、自信造型定义、清晰视觉层级、有冲击的剪影设计，以及高度可读形态为定义的图形插画美学，通过自律线条控制、简化体积构建与刻意图形对比，优先视觉清晰度、结构强度与构图存在感；令人联想到 Mike Mignola 与 Frank Miller，灵感来自《地狱男爵》与《罪恶之城》。

Clean Line Art: refined line-focused aesthetics characterized by crisp contours, exceptional visual clarity, disciplined draftsmanship, controlled line weight, and highly readable form construction, emphasizing precision, elegance, and structural coherence through polished execution, simplified visual noise, and a timeless graphic sensibility that balances sophistication with effortless readability, reminiscent of Hergé and Yves Chaland, inspired by Tintin and Blake et Mortimer.
Clean Line Art（干净线条艺术）：以 crisp 轮廓、卓越视觉清晰度、自律造型、受控线条粗细，以及高度可读形态构建为特征的精炼线条中心美学，通过抛光执行、简化视觉噪点与平衡成熟与毫不费力可读性的永恒图形感，强调精度、优雅与结构 coherence；令人联想到 Hergé 与 Yves Chaland，灵感来自《丁丁历险记》与《布莱克与莫蒂默》。

Moebius-Inspired Illustration: A meticulously linear illustration style built on impossibly clean, confident linework that defines every form with fluid precision rather than heavy shading, expansive contemplative compositions that give equal weight to vast open space and intricate surface detail, delicate stippling and fine cross-hatching used sparingly to suggest volume and atmosphere, a serene, otherworldly stillness pervading even the most fantastical scenes, and a meditative, dreamlike clarity where technical mastery and imaginative wonder feel perfectly balanced. Influenced by: Jean "Mœbius" Giraud, French bande dessinée illustration, ligne claire technique, science-fantasy comic art.,
Moebius-Inspired Illustration（墨比斯启发插画）：一种精细线性的插画风格，以 impossibly 干净自信的线条而非厚重阴影以流体精度定义每个形态，给予广阔开放空间与繁复表面细节同等权重的 expansive 沉思构图，稀少使用细腻点刻与细交叉排线以暗示体积与氛围，即使最奇幻场景也弥漫宁静、超凡的静谧，以及技术 mastery 与想象奇迹完美平衡的冥想、梦境般清晰度。受影响于：Jean "Mœbius" Giraud、法国 bande dessinée 插画、ligne claire 技法、科幻漫画艺术。

Vintage Comics: A bold, graphic illustration style built on confident inked linework holding clearly defined, dynamic proportions, coarse halftone-dot patterning standing in for smooth shading, a slightly imperfect, offset-print texture giving the whole composition a worn, mass-produced charm, dynamic, energetic posing designed for instant visual impact within a small panel, and a nostalgic, unmistakably golden-age graphic-storytelling sincerity that feels handcrafted, punchy, and timeworn. Influenced by: Golden Age American comic-illustration tradition, halftone offset-printing technique, mid-century pulp-comic practice, classic newsstand comic-book method.
Vintage Comics（复古漫画）：一种 bold、图形的插画风格，以自信墨线固定清晰定义、动态的比例，以粗半调点图案替代平滑阴影，略不完美、胶印质感赋予整体 worn、量产的魅力，为小分镜内瞬时视觉冲击设计的动态充满能量姿态；手工感、punchy、饱经岁月的怀旧黄金时代图形叙事真诚感。受影响于：黄金时代美式漫画插画传统、半调胶印技法、中叶世纪通俗漫画实践、经典报摊漫画书方法。

Jim Lee Style: A dynamic, high-detail mainstream comic-illustration style built on confident, powerful anatomy rendered with meticulous cross-hatched inking, dramatic musculature and dense linework carrying real sculptural weight, sharp, dynamic foreshortening charged with kinetic action-movie energy, richly detailed costume and texture rewarding close inspection, and a bold, blockbuster comic-cover intensity that feels polished, powerful, and instantly iconic. Influenced by: Jim Lee, WildStorm/DC/Marvel mainstream comic-illustration tradition, dynamic cross-hatched inking technique, action-driven cover-illustration practice.
Jim Lee Style（吉姆·李风格）：一种动态、高细节的主流漫画插画风格，以自信有力的解剖经精细交叉排线墨线刻画，承载真实雕塑重量的戏剧性肌肉与密集线条，充满动能动作电影能量的锐利动态 foreshortening，值得近观的精细服装与纹理，以及抛光、有力、瞬时 iconic 的 bold、大片漫画封面强度。受影响于：Jim Lee、WildStorm/DC/Marvel 主流漫画插画传统、动态交叉排线墨线技法、动作驱动封面插画实践。

Joe Quesada Style: A sleek, hyper-glossy painted comic-illustration style built on immaculately smooth gradient rendering that trades traditional inking for glassy, airbrushed precision, dramatic directional lighting carving sharp, confident highlight across muscular, dynamic anatomy, meticulously controlled reflective sheen giving every form a polished, high-production finish, a stylized, exaggerated silhouette balancing painterly depth with graphic mainstream-comic clarity, and a sleek, cinematic comic-cover intensity built for instantly striking newsstand impact. Influenced by: Joe Quesada, painted comic-cover illustration tradition, hyper-glossy digital-rendering technique, mainstream superhero-illustration practice.
Joe Quesada Style（乔·奎萨达风格）：一种利落、超光泽的绘画性漫画插画风格，以完美平滑渐变渲染以玻璃喷枪精度取代传统墨线，在肌肉、动态解剖上刻出锐利自信亮部的戏剧性方向光，精细控制的反射光泽赋予每个形态抛光、高制作的完成度，平衡绘画性纵深与图形主流漫画清晰度的风格化、夸张剪影，以及为瞬时醒目报摊冲击而生的利落、电影感漫画封面强度。受影响于：Joe Quesada、绘画性漫画封面传统、超光泽数字渲染技法、主流超英插画实践。

glossy Comic-digital art: A vibrant, glossy comic-digital art style built on confident, clean design merged with extremely smooth airbrushed shading, luminous semi-realistic skin rendering with sharp specular highlights , a playful, curve-forward sensibility charged with confident vibes, a backdrop providing energetic contrast to the crisp central figure, and a polished, vibrant character-illustration intensity that feels contemporary, glossy, and effortlessly eye-catching. outlines are smoothed into invisibility,  Influenced by: contemporary digital illustration tradition, semi-realistic airbrush-rendering technique, centric compositional practice, glossy character-illustration method.
glossy Comic-digital art（光泽漫画数字艺术）：一种充满活力的 glossy 漫画数字艺术风格，以自信干净设计与极平滑喷枪阴影相融合，带锐利高光的发光半写实皮肤渲染，充满自信感、曲线前置的俏皮气质，为 crisp 中心人物提供能量对比的背景，以及当代、glossy、毫不费力吸睛的抛光角色插画强度；轮廓被平滑至不可见。受影响于：当代数字插画传统、半写实喷枪渲染技法、居中构图实践、glossy 角色插画方法。

Photorealistic Comic Ink-and-Paint Hybrid: A meticulously detailed comic-illustration style built on crisp, confident inked structure providing the underlying graphic clarity, layered painterly rendering fills that structure with convincing photoreal depth and tonal nuance, dramatic directional lighting reinforcing form through both linework and rendered shadow simultaneously, a technically disciplined balance between graphic boldness and painterly subtlety, and a polished, high-production comic-illustration intensity that bridges traditional inking craft with photoreal rendering ambition. Influenced by: photorealistic comic-illustration tradition, ink-and-paint hybrid rendering technique, contemporary graphic-novel practice, painted-cover comic-art method.
Photorealistic Comic Ink-and-Paint Hybrid（照片写实漫画墨线-上色混合）：一种精细的漫画插画风格，以提供底层图形清晰度的 crisp、自信墨线结构，填充该结构以令人信服照片纵深的层叠绘画性渲染，通过线条与渲染阴影同时强化形态的戏剧性方向光，图形 boldness 与绘画性微妙之间的技术平衡，以及桥接传统墨线工艺与照片写实渲染雄心的抛光、高制作漫画插画强度。受影响于：照片写实漫画插画传统、墨线-上色混合渲染技法、当代图像小说实践、绘画性封面漫画艺术方法。

Drawing: A direct, hand-crafted illustration style built on confident linework capturing form through economical, considered mark-making, restrained tonal shading applied through simple cross-hatching or light shading rather than elaborate rendering, an honest, unembellished clarity that favors observation over stylization, unhurried, deliberate strokes carrying a personal, tactile sensibility, and a timeless, accessible sincerity rooted in foundational drawing tradition. Influenced by: traditional pencil-and-ink drawing tradition, life-drawing practice, classical illustration fundamentals, observational sketching method.
Drawing（手绘）：一种直接、手工的插画风格，以自信线条经经济、考究的 mark-making 捕捉形态，经简单交叉排线或轻阴影而非繁复渲染应用的克制色调阴影，优先观察而非风格化的诚实、无修饰清晰度，携带个人、tactile 气质的从容、deliberate 笔触，以及根植基础绘画传统的永恒、易接近真诚感。受影响于：传统铅笔墨线绘画传统、人体写生实践、经典插画基础、观察式速写方法。

Caricature: A playful exaggeration-driven illustration style built on bold distortion of proportion that amplifies distinctive features for comedic and expressive impact, loose, confident linework capturing likeness through economical, energetic mark-making rather than exhaustive detail, light, quick shading applied to suggest form without slowing the gestural energy, an exuberant, larger-than-life comic timing embedded in every exaggerated feature, and a witty, high-energy sketch-illustration charm that celebrates personality over precision. Influenced by: classic caricature-illustration tradition, quick-sketch portraiture technique, satirical editorial illustration, gestural ink-and-wash caricature.
Caricature（漫画肖像/夸张像）：一种俏皮、夸张驱动的插画风格，以放大 distinctive 特征以获喜剧与表现力冲击的 bold 比例扭曲，经经济、充满能量 mark-making 而非 exhaustive 细节捕捉肖像的松散自信线条，为暗示形态而不拖慢 gestural 能量应用的轻快、迅速阴影，嵌入每个夸张特征的 exuberant、超越现实喜剧 timing，以及庆祝个性而非精度的 witty、高能量速写插画魅力。受影响于：经典漫画肖像插画传统、快速速写肖像技法、satirical 社论插画、gestural 墨线水洗漫画肖像。

Charcoal Rendering: traditional drawing aesthetics characterized by rich tonal gradation, expressive mark-making, tactile surface texture, atmospheric softness, and powerful value-based form construction, emphasizing depth, mood, and craftsmanship through layered shading, subtle edge control, and a handcrafted visual presence that balances realism with artistic interpretation, reminiscent of Käthe Kollwitz and Robert Longo, inspired by The Weavers and Men in the Cities.
Charcoal Rendering（炭笔渲染）：以丰富色调渐变、富有表现力的 mark-making、tactile 表面纹理、氛围柔软感，以及基于明度的强力形态构建为特征的传统绘画美学，通过层叠阴影、微妙边缘控制，以及平衡写实与艺术诠释的手工视觉存在，强调纵深、情绪与工艺；令人联想到 Käthe Kollwitz 与 Robert Longo，灵感来自《纺织工人》与《城市中的男人》。

Hyperrealistic Colored-Pen Sketch: A meticulously rendered drawing style built on dense, layered pen strokes that build tonal depth and material realism entirely through cross-hatching, stippling, and controlled ink-layering rather than blended shading, painstaking precision in capturing texture and light behavior with a fine-tipped, linear medium, an obsessive patience carried through countless overlapping passes to achieve photographic depth, a technically virtuosic tension between the rigidity of the pen medium and the softness of the rendered subject, and an intensely disciplined, prolonged-focus rendering intensity. Influenced by: hyperrealist pen-and-ink illustration technique, fine-liner cross-hatching method, contemporary colored-pen portrait artists, meticulous layered-ink rendering practice.
Hyperrealistic Colored-Pen Sketch（超写实彩色钢笔速写）：一种精细刻画的绘画风格，以密集层叠钢笔笔触完全通过交叉排线、点刻与受控墨层构建色调纵深与材质写实，以细尖线性媒介捕捉纹理与光行为的 painstaking 精度，经无数重叠 pass 达成照片纵深的偏执耐心，钢笔媒介的 rigid 与渲染对象的柔软之间的技术 virtuoso 张力，以及极度自律、长时间专注的渲染强度。受影响于：超写实钢笔插画技法、细头笔交叉排线方法、当代彩色钢笔肖像艺术家、精细层叠墨渲染实践。

Jarek Kubicki Style: A darkly whimsical illustration style built on confident, sketch-like linework carrying gothic-adjacent playfulness, a muted, atmospheric tonal palette balanced against bold, graphic character silhouettes, a slightly unsettling yet endearing character-design sensibility blending humor with quiet unease, meticulous textural mark-making giving surfaces a hand-drawn, tactile roughness, and a distinctive, contemporary illustration voice that feels equally macabre and playful. Influenced by: Jarek Kubicki, contemporary character-illustration tradition, gothic-whimsical illustration technique, hand-drawn textural rendering practice.
Jarek Kubicki Style（亚雷克·库比茨基风格）：一种黑暗异想天开的插画风格，以携带类哥特俏皮感的自信、速写般线条，与 bold、图形角色剪影相平衡的 muted、氛围色调 palette，融合幽默与安静不安、略 unsettling 却 endearing 的角色设计感，赋予表面手工、tactile 粗糙感的精细纹理 mark-making，以及兼具 macabre 与俏皮的独特、当代插画声音。受影响于：Jarek Kubicki、当代角色插画传统、哥特异想插画技法、手绘纹理渲染实践。

Playful Hand-Drawn Illustration: A loose, charming illustration style with visibly hand-inked or hand-painted linework that keeps a warm, imperfect human touch, bouncy exaggerated proportions and expressive gesture-driven poses, bright cheerful color palettes applied in flat or lightly textured washes, whimsical small details and doodle-like embellishments scattered through the composition, and a lighthearted, storybook charm that feels immediate, joyful, and unpolished in the best way. Influenced by: Quentin Blake, children's picture-book illustration, gouache and watercolor sketching, modern editorial doodle art.
Playful Hand-Drawn Illustration（俏皮手绘插画）：一种松散、迷人的插画风格，保留温暖、不完美人手触感的可见手绘墨线/手绘线条，弹性夸张的比例与 gestural 驱动姿态，平涂或轻纹理水洗应用的明亮欢快配色，散布构图的异想小细节与涂鸦式装饰，以及即时、欢乐、以最佳方式未打磨的轻快绘本魅力。受影响于：Quentin Blake、童书插画、水粉与水彩速写、现代社论涂鸦艺术。

Chinese Ink Drawing: A meditative, gestural illustration style built on fluid, calligraphic brushwork that captures form through economical, confident strokes rather than exhaustive detail, deliberate negative space treated as an active compositional element carrying as much weight as rendered form, varying ink density achieved through brush pressure and water dilution rather than layered shading, a contemplative, breath-driven rhythm carried through unhurried, deliberate mark-making, and a timeless, philosophical restraint that values suggestion and empty space over exhaustive representation. Influenced by: traditional Chinese shuǐmò (ink-wash) painting tradition, literati brush-painting practice, Xu Beihong ink technique, classical Chinese calligraphy discipline.
Chinese Ink Drawing（中国水墨画）：一种冥想、gestural 的插画风格，以流体、书法性笔触经经济、自信 strokes 而非 exhaustive 细节捕捉形态，作为主动构图元素、与渲染形态同等重量的刻意负空间，通过笔压与水稀释而非层叠阴影实现变化的墨色浓度，通过从容、deliberate mark-making 承载的沉思、呼吸驱动节奏，以及重暗示与空而非 exhaustive 再现的永恒、哲学性克制。受影响于：传统中国水墨（shuǐmò）绘画传统、文人笔墨实践、徐悲鸿墨法、经典中国书法纪律。

Sketch: A raw, immediate illustration style built on loose, gestural linework that captures form through confident, economical mark-making rather than exhaustive rendering, visible construction lines and searching strokes left intentionally unresolved, minimal tonal shading achieved through quick cross-hatching or light smudging rather than smooth gradation, an energetic sense of spontaneity and unfinished momentum carried through every line, and a raw, honest immediacy that values gesture and observation over polish. Influenced by: life-drawing sketch tradition, charcoal and graphite illustration technique, quick-study figure drawing, academic atelier sketching method.
Sketch（速写）：一种 raw、即时的插画风格，以松散、gestural 线条经自信、经济 mark-making 而非 exhaustive 渲染捕捉形态，刻意保留未解决的可见构造线与探索性 strokes，经快速交叉排线或轻涂抹而非平滑渐变实现的极少色调阴影，每根线条携带的能量感自发与未完成 momentum，以及重 gesture 与观察而非抛光的 raw、诚实即时感。受影响于：人体写生速写传统、炭笔与石墨插画技法、快速研究人物画、学院派 atelier 速写方法。

Children's Book Drawing: A warm, gentle illustration style built on soft, simplified linework carrying a sincere, approachable charm, delicate flat-to-lightly-shaded color application applied with cheerful, unhurried restraint, rounded, endearing proportions favoring emotional warmth over anatomical precision, generous, uncluttered composition that gives each small detail room to breathe, and a tender, timeless storybook sincerity that feels handmade, comforting, and quietly imaginative. Influenced by: golden-age children's-book illustration tradition, gouache-and-ink storybook technique, gentle character-design practice, whimsical picture-book method.
Children's Book Drawing（童书插画）：一种温暖、温柔的插画风格，以携带真诚、易接近魅力的柔软、简化线条，以欢快、从容克制应用的精致平涂到轻度阴影色彩，优先情感温暖而非解剖精度的圆润 endearing 比例，给每个小细节呼吸空间的 generous、不杂乱构图，以及手工感、舒适、quietly 富有想象力的温柔、永恒绘本真诚感。受影响于：黄金时代童书插画传统、水粉墨线绘本技法、温柔角色设计实践、异想绘本方法。

Vintage Children's Book Drawing: A gentle, nostalgic illustration style built on soft, hand-inked linework carrying old-world sincerity and charm, delicate watercolor-adjacent washes applied with unhurried, muted restraint, rounded, endearing proportions favoring warmth and whimsy over anatomical precision, a hushed, timeworn tenderness carried through generous, uncluttered composition, and a wistful, storybook nostalgia that feels handmade, cherished, and quietly magical. Influenced by: mid-century golden-age children's-book illustration tradition, gouache-and-watercolor storybook technique, classic picture-book character-design practice, vintage print-illustration method.
Vintage Children's Book Drawing（复古童书插画）：一种温柔、怀旧的插画风格，以携带旧世界真诚与魅力的柔软手绘墨线，以从容、muted 克制的细腻类水彩水洗，优先温暖与异想而非解剖精度的圆润 endearing 比例，generous、不杂乱构图承载的轻声、饱经岁月温柔感，以及手工感、被珍视、quietly 魔法的怅惘绘本怀旧感。受影响于：中叶世纪黄金时代童书插画传统、水粉水彩绘本技法、经典绘本角色设计实践、复古印刷插画方法。

Gris Grimly Style: A whimsically macabre illustration style built on gnarled, spindly linework that twists proportion into gothic, storybook exaggeration, scratchy, textured crosshatching layered with unsettling nervous energy, elongated, angular form carrying a darkly comedic fragility, a hushed, eerie theatricality pervading every composition, and a darkly whimsical, gothic-fairytale charm that feels equal parts sinister and playful. Influenced by: Gris Grimly, gothic children's-book illustration tradition, macabre hand-drawn illustration technique, whimsical dark-fantasy character-design practice.
Gris Grimly Style（格里斯·格里姆利风格）：一种异想天开且 macabre 的插画风格，以将比例扭成哥特、绘本式夸张的 gnarled、spindly 线条，携带 unsettling 紧张能量的 scratchy、纹理化交叉排线层叠，承载黑暗喜剧脆弱感的拉长棱角形态，弥漫每处构图的轻声、诡异戏剧性，以及兼具 sinister 与俏皮的黑暗异想哥特童话魅力。受影响于：Gris Grimly、哥特童书插画传统、macabre 手绘插画技法、异想黑暗奇幻角色设计实践。

Tim Burton Style: A whimsically macabre illustration style built on elongated, spindly proportions and exaggerated angular gauntness, high-contrast graphic shading that carves gothic drama from stark light-and-dark simplicity, swirling, hand-drawn linework carrying a nervous, sketchy energy, wide hollow eyes rendered with melancholic, storybook expressiveness, and a darkly whimsical, gothic-fairytale charm that feels equal parts eerie and endearing. Influenced by: Tim Burton, gothic stop-motion animation design, whimsical macabre illustration, hand-drawn concept-art sketch technique.
Tim Burton Style（蒂姆·伯顿风格）：一种异想天开且 macabre 的插画风格，以拉长、spindly 比例与夸张棱角憔悴感，从 stark 明暗简化切刻哥特戏剧的高对比图形阴影，携带紧张、速写能量的 swirling 手绘线条，以忧郁、绘本式表现力刻画的空洞大眼，以及兼具 eerie 与 endearing 的黑暗异想哥特童话魅力。受影响于：Tim Burton、哥特定格动画设计、异想 macabre 插画、手绘概念艺术速写技法。

Fashion Sketch: An elegant, elongated illustration style built on fluid, confident linework stretching proportion into graceful, statuesque exaggeration, minimal, loose shading applied with quick, economical strokes rather than full rendering, a light, airy sense of movement carried through unhurried, gestural mark-making, deliberate emphasis on flowing silhouette and pose over anatomical exactness, and a chic, effortless sketch-illustration elegance that feels immediate, stylish, and quietly sophisticated. Influenced by: fashion-illustration tradition, croquis sketching technique, editorial fashion-sketch practice, elongated figure-drawing method.
Fashion Sketch（时装速写）：一种优雅、拉长的插画风格，以流体、自信线条将比例拉伸为优雅、statuesque 夸张，以快速、经济 strokes 而非完整渲染应用的极少、松散阴影，通过从容、gestural mark-making 携带的轻盈、airy 运动感，刻意强调流动剪影与姿态而非解剖精确，以及即时、时髦、quietly 成熟、毫不费力的 chic 速写插画优雅感。受影响于：时装插画传统、croquis 速写技法、社论时装速写实践、拉长人物画方法。

Loose Crosshatched Character Sketch: A lively, unfinished-feeling character-illustration style built on confident, visibly searching linework left intentionally raw rather than cleaned up, dense parallel crosshatching used sparingly to suggest volume and shadow without committing to full rendering, expressive, exaggerated facial modeling that prioritizes emotional intensity over anatomical polish, a loose, energetic looseness at the edges that keeps the whole piece feeling immediate and process-driven, and a spirited, illustrative rawness that reads as equal parts concept sketch and finished character study. Influenced by: contemporary character-design sketch practice, marker-and-pencil illustration technique, expressive digital-sketch rendering method, gesture-driven character-study tradition.
Loose Crosshatched Character Sketch（松散排线角色速写）：一种 lively、未完结感的角色插画风格，以刻意保留 raw 而非清理的自信、可见探索性线条，稀少使用以暗示体积与阴影而不 commit 完整渲染的密集平行交叉排线，优先情感强度而非解剖抛光的夸张、富有表现力面部塑造，边缘松散、充满能量的 looseness 让整体感到即时与过程驱动，以及既像概念速写又像完成角色研究的 spirited、插画性 raw 感。受影响于：当代角色设计速写实践、马克笔与铅笔插画技法、表现性数字速写渲染方法、gesture 驱动角色研究传统。

Wojak/Meme-Sketch Style: A crude, minimal-effort illustration style built on loose, deliberately unrefined ballpoint-pen linework that abandons anatomical precision for rapid expressive shorthand, flat, mostly absent shading beyond the barest suggestion of form, a blunt, economical mark-making approach that prioritizes instant emotional legibility over any technical polish, an intentionally amateur roughness that reads as sincere rather than sloppy, and a deadpan, internet-native rawness that turns crudeness itself into the entire expressive language. Influenced by: internet meme-illustration tradition, minimalist ballpoint-sketch technique, imageboard-culture drawing practice, deliberately crude digital-doodle method.
Wojak/Meme-Sketch Style（Wojak/梗图速写风格）：一种粗陋、最低 effort 的插画风格，以松散、刻意未精炼的圆珠笔线条放弃解剖精度、换取快速表现性速记，除最基础形态暗示外基本缺失的平涂阴影，优先瞬时情感可读性而非任何技术抛光的 blunt、经济 mark-making，读作真诚而非草率的刻意业余粗糙感，以及将粗陋本身变成整个表现语言的 deadpan、互联网原生 raw 感。受影响于：互联网梗图插画传统、极简圆珠笔速写技法、图像板文化绘画实践、刻意粗陋数字涂鸦方法。

Ink Pen Drawing: A precise, linear illustration style built on confident, controlled strokes that construct every form entirely through line rather than blended tone, dense cross-hatching and stippling layered to achieve tonal depth purely through line density, a crisp, deliberate discipline carried through unhurried, considered mark-making, meticulous attention to line-weight variation suggesting weight and dimensionality, and a timeless, meticulous craftsmanship rooted in centuries of traditional pen-and-ink illustration practice. Influenced by: classical pen-and-ink illustration tradition, fine cross-hatching technique, engraving-adjacent linework practice, traditional academic drawing method.
Ink Pen Drawing（钢笔画）：一种精确、线性的插画风格，以自信、受控 strokes 完全通过线条而非混合色调构建每个形态，以纯线密度实现色调纵深的密集交叉排线与点刻层叠，通过从容、考究 mark-making 携带的 crisp、deliberate 纪律，对暗示重量与维度的线重变化精细考究，以及根植数世纪传统钢笔插画实践的永恒、精细工艺感。受影响于：经典钢笔插画传统、精细交叉排线技法、类版画线条实践、传统学院派绘画方法。

Etching: A meticulous printmaking-inspired illustration style built on dense, fine crosshatched linework that constructs every tone and volume purely through line density rather than flat shading, crisp needle-precise detail rendered with old-world engraving discipline, restrained tonal range achieved through layered hatching passes rather than smooth gradients, a quiet, antique gravitas carried through the deliberate, unhurried linework, and a refined, scholarly print-craft polish that feels historical, meticulous, and timeless. Influenced by: classical etching and engraving technique, Albrecht Dürer, printmaking illustration tradition, Gustave Doré linework.
Etching（蚀刻版画）：一种精细、受版画启发的插画风格，以构建每个色调与体积完全通过线密度而非平涂阴影的密集、细交叉排线线条，以旧世界版画纪律刻画的 crisp 针尖精度细节，通过层叠排线 pass 而非平滑渐变实现的克制色调范围，通过 deliberate、从容线条携带的轻声、古董庄严感，以及历史、精细、永恒的精炼、学术性版画工艺抛光感。受影响于：经典蚀刻与版画技法、Albrecht Dürer、版画插画传统、Gustave Doré 线条。

Technical Drawing: A precise, disciplined illustration style built on exacting linework constructed with mechanical accuracy and consistent line-weight logic, minimal or entirely absent shading that relies on line alone to convey form and structure, a rigorous, measured clarity that favors exact proportion and dimensional accuracy over expressive interpretation, meticulous attention to structural detail rendered with drafting-level precision, and a clean, methodical exactness rooted in centuries of engineering and scientific illustration discipline. Influenced by: technical/engineering-drawing tradition, drafting-illustration technique, scientific-diagram illustration practice, orthographic-projection drawing method.
Technical Drawing（技术制图）：一种精确、自律的插画风格，以机械精度与一致线重逻辑构建的严谨线条，依赖线条 alone 传达形态与结构、极少或完全无阴影，优先精确比例与尺寸精度而非表现性诠释的严谨、measured 清晰度，以制图级精度刻画的构造细节精细考究，以及根植数世纪工程与科学插画纪律的干净、methodical 精确感。受影响于：技术/工程制图传统、制图插画技法、科学图解插画实践、正投影画法。

Conté Pastel: A rich, tactile drawing style built on dense, velvety pigment applied with confident, blendable pressure, a soft, smudgeable quality allowing form to be built through both layered mark-making and controlled blending, a warm, granular texture distinct from smoother chalk or charcoal media, deliberate tonal transition achieved through pressure variation rather than wet blending, and a timeless, painterly-adjacent intensity rooted in classical academic drawing tradition. Influenced by: Conté crayon drawing tradition, classical academic tonal-drawing practice, life-drawing atelier technique, fine-art pastel-and-chalk method.
Conté Pastel（孔戴粉笔）：一种丰富、tactile 的绘画风格，以自信、可混合压力的密集、天鹅绒般颜料应用，通过层叠 mark-making 与受控混合皆可构建形态的柔软、可涂抹质感，区别于更平滑粉笔或炭笔的温暖、颗粒纹理，通过压力变化而非湿混合实现的 deliberate 色调过渡，以及根植经典学院派绘画传统的永恒、类绘画性强度。受影响于：孔戴蜡笔绘画传统、经典学院派色调绘画实践、人体写生 atelier 技法、fine-art 粉彩与粉笔方法。

Crayola Colored-Book Style: A cheerful, deliberately simplified illustration style built on bold, uneven waxy strokes that fill flat shapes with visible, layered texture rather than smooth coverage, thick, confident outline holding basic, uncomplicated forms, minimal shading achieved through pressure variation and layered strokes rather than blending, a charming, imperfect childlike sincerity carried through slightly uneven coloring-within-the-lines application, and a nostalgic, playful simplicity that feels handmade, joyful, and unmistakably crafted by hand. Influenced by: children's coloring-book tradition, wax-crayon illustration technique, elementary art-class practice, simplified line-and-fill illustration method.
Crayola Colored-Book Style（绘儿乐涂色书风格）：一种欢快、刻意简化的插画风格，以填充平涂形状、可见层叠纹理而非平滑覆盖的 bold、不均匀蜡笔 strokes，固定基础、不复杂形态的厚实自信轮廓，通过压力变化与层叠 strokes 而非混合实现的极少阴影，略不均匀的"不出线"上色应用携带的迷人、不完美童真真诚感，以及手工感、欢乐、毋庸置疑手作的怀旧、俏皮简化感。受影响于：儿童涂色书传统、蜡笔插画技法、小学美术课实践、简化线条填充插画方法。

Bookplate Illustration: refined decorative illustration aesthetics characterized by meticulous linework, engraved-inspired detailing, ornamental sophistication, literary craftsmanship, balanced composition, and timeless artisanal quality, blending classical printmaking traditions with elegant visual storytelling through intricate textures, disciplined draftsmanship, and exceptionally polished design sensibilities, reminiscent of Arthur Rackham and Aubrey Beardsley, inspired by Golden Age Bookplates and The Fairy Tales of the Brothers Grimm.
Bookplate Illustration（藏书票插画）：以精细线条、受版画启发的细节、装饰性精致、文学工艺、平衡构图，以及永恒工匠品质为特征的精炼装饰插画美学，通过繁复纹理、自律造型与极度抛光的设感，将经典版画传统与优雅视觉叙事相融合；令人联想到 Arthur Rackham 与 Aubrey Beardsley，灵感来自黄金时代藏书票与《格林兄弟童话》。

Boulet Graphic Illustration: contemporary graphic storytelling aesthetics characterized by expressive linework, fluid visual rhythm, intelligent simplification, dynamic composition, and a balance between humor, atmosphere, and observational detail, blending sketchbook spontaneity with polished narrative craftsmanship through confident draftsmanship, readable forms, and highly engaging visual communication, reminiscent of Boulet and Lewis Trondheim, inspired by Notes and Donjon.
Boulet Graphic Illustration（布勒/Boulet 图像叙事）：以表现性线条、流体视觉节奏、聪明简化、动态构图，以及幽默、氛围与观察细节之间平衡为特征的当代图形叙事美学，通过自信造型、可读形态与高度 engaging 视觉传达，将速写本自发感与抛光叙事工艺相融合；令人联想到 Boulet 与 Lewis Trondheim，灵感来自《Notes》与《Donjon》。

Poe-Inspired Illustration: A haunting gothic-macabre illustration style steeped in melancholy and dread, rendered in desaturated colors, ash-gray, and deep black tones pierced by isolated light, decaying Victorian vibes, symbolic motifs woven through the composition, delicately cross-hatched or ink-washed texture recalling antique engraving, and a brooding, poetic atmosphere of obsession, grief, and quiet horror. Influenced by: Harry Clarke, gothic engraving illustration, Victorian mourning art, Gustave Doré.
Poe-Inspired Illustration（爱伦·坡启发插画）：一种沉浸忧郁与恐惧的 haunting 哥特-macabre 插画风格，以去饱和色彩、灰烬灰与深黑调被孤立光刺穿渲染，衰败的维多利亚氛围，编织进构图的象征母题，回忆古董版画的细腻交叉排线或墨线水洗纹理，以及执念、悲伤与安静恐怖的 brooding、诗意氛围。受影响于：Harry Clarke、哥特版画插画、维多利亚哀悼艺术、Gustave Doré。

Collector Storybook Illustration: premium narrative illustration aesthetics characterized by refined draftsmanship, elegant visual storytelling, intricate decorative detail, polished painterly rendering, and a timeless sense of wonder, blending classic storybook craftsmanship with modern production-quality execution through sophisticated composition, atmospheric depth, and meticulously curated visual richness, reminiscent of Arthur Rackham and Kinuko Y. Craft, inspired by The Fairy Tales of the Brothers Grimm and The Chronicles of Narnia.
Collector Storybook Illustration（典藏童书插画）：以精炼造型、优雅视觉叙事、繁复装饰细节、抛光绘画性渲染，以及永恒奇迹感为特征的 premium 叙事插画美学，通过成熟构图、氛围纵深与精心策划的视觉丰富，将经典绘本工艺与现代制作质量执行相融合；令人联想到 Arthur Rackham 与 Kinuko Y. Craft，灵感来自《格林兄弟童话》与《纳尼亚传奇》。

Saul Steinbeck Art: A refined, gently satirical illustration style built on confident, economical linework capturing wit and observation with restrained precision, muted, sophisticated tonal application applied with understated elegance rather than bold contrast, a quiet, literary charm carried through clever, observational composition, meticulous attention to subtle visual storytelling over exhaustive detail, and a timeless, urbane sophistication that feels intelligent, witty, and effortlessly refined. Influenced by: Saul Steinbeck (Saul Steinberg), contemporary New Yorker illustration tradition, gouache-and-ink editorial technique, sophisticated satirical-illustration practice.
Saul Steinbeck Art（索尔·斯坦伯格风格）：一种精炼、轻度 satirical 的插画风格，以自信、经济线条以克制精度捕捉机智与观察，以低调优雅而非 bold 对比应用的 muted、成熟色调，通过聪明、观察性构图携带的轻声、文学魅力，对微妙视觉叙事而非 exhaustive 细节精细考究，以及智能、witty、毫不费力精炼的永恒、urbane 成熟感。受影响于：Saul Steinberg、当代《纽约客》插画传统、水粉墨线社论技法、成熟 satirical 插画实践。

Whimsical Illustration: A lighthearted, charming illustration style built on soft, bouncy proportion that stretches form into playful, exaggerated shapes, gentle, evenly balanced shading applied with cheerful simplicity rather than dramatic contrast, a storybook sincerity carried through rounded, approachable linework, delicate small details scattered throughout the composition to reward closer looking, and a joyful, effortless charm that feels handmade, tender, and universally endearing. Influenced by: contemporary children's-book illustration tradition, gouache-and-ink whimsical technique, gentle character-design practice, storybook illustration method.
Whimsical Illustration（异想天开插画）：一种轻快、迷人的插画风格，以将形态拉伸为俏皮夸张形状的柔软、弹跳比例，以欢快简单而非戏剧对比应用的温柔、均匀平衡阴影，通过圆润、易接近线条携带的绘本真诚，散布构图以奖励细看的小巧精致细节，以及手工感、温柔、普适 endearing 的欢乐、毫不费力魅力。受影响于：当代童书插画传统、水粉墨线异想技法、温柔角色设计实践、绘本插画方法。

Doodle: A loose, casual illustration style built on quick, unplanned linework that meanders with playful spontaneity rather than deliberate structure, minimal to no shading beyond simple scribbled fill, an unselfconscious, low-stakes energy carried through impulsive, exploratory mark-making, small whimsical details scattered organically without formal composition, and a lighthearted, effortless charm that feels immediate, personal, and free of any polish. Influenced by: margin-doodle sketching tradition, casual ballpoint-pen illustration technique, unstructured gesture-drawing practice, personal notebook-sketch method.
Doodle（涂鸦）：一种松散、随意的插画风格，以随意、未计划线条以俏皮自发而非 deliberate 结构漫游，除简单涂鸦填充外极少或无阴影，通过冲动、探索性 mark-making 携带的 unselfconscious、low-stakes 能量，无正式构图、有机散布的小巧异想细节，以及即时、个人、无抛光的轻快、毫不费力魅力。受影响于：页边涂鸦速写传统、随意圆珠笔插画技法、非结构 gesture 绘画实践、个人笔记本速写方法。

Very Simplistic Doodle: A radically minimal illustration style built on the barest possible linework needed to suggest form, no shading whatsoever beyond an occasional flat scribble, a childlike, unpracticed economy of gesture that favors instant recognizability over any accuracy, loose, wobbly strokes drawn with no correction or refinement, and a charmingly crude, effortless simplicity that feels spontaneous, playful, and completely unpolished. Influenced by: margin-doodle sketching tradition, casual ballpoint-pen technique, unstructured gesture-drawing practice, minimalist notebook-sketch method.
Very Simplistic Doodle（极简涂鸦）：一种激进极简的插画风格，以暗示形态所需的最少线条，除偶尔平涂涂鸦外完全无阴影，优先瞬时可辨识而非任何精度的童真、无练习 gesture 经济，无修正或精炼绘制的松散、wobbly strokes，以及自发、俏皮、完全未打磨的迷人粗陋、毫不费力简化感。受影响于：页边涂鸦速写传统、随意圆珠笔技法、非结构 gesture 绘画实践、极简笔记本速写方法。

Beatrix Potter Style: A gentle, meticulously observed watercolor illustration style built on delicate, softly blended washes carrying quiet naturalist precision, fine detailed linework reserved for texture and form rather than bold outline, a hushed pastoral tenderness radiating through soft diffused lighting, restrained old-world charm achieved through understated, unhurried brushwork, and a tender, storybook intimacy that feels both scientifically observant and gently whimsical. Influenced by: Beatrix Potter, English watercolor illustration, naturalist botanical illustration, Golden Age children's book art.
Beatrix Potter Style（碧雅翠丝·波特风格）：一种温柔、精细观察的水彩插画风格，以携带安静自然主义精度的细腻、柔和混合水洗，为纹理与形态而非 bold 轮廓保留的精细细节线条，通过柔和漫射光辐射的轻声田园温柔，以低调、从容笔触实现的克制旧世界魅力，以及既科学观察又 gently 异想天开的温柔、绘本亲密感。受影响于：Beatrix Potter、英国水彩插画、自然主义植物插画、黄金时代童书艺术。

Lo-Fi Art: A relaxed, low-fidelity illustration style built on soft, slightly muted rendering that intentionally avoids sharp precision, a gentle, unhurried simplicity in linework and shading applied with casual, low-effort ease, a hazy, slightly grainy or textured quality replacing crisp digital cleanliness, an easygoing, mellow atmosphere that favors comfort over technical ambition, and a nostalgic, chill sincerity that feels handmade, cozy, and effortlessly unpolished. Influenced by: lo-fi aesthetic movement, casual sketch-illustration technique, chillwave visual-culture practice, contemporary relaxed-digital-art method.
Lo-Fi Art（低保真艺术）：一种放松、低保真的插画风格，以刻意避免锐利精度的柔软、略 muted 渲染，以随意、低 effort 轻松应用的线条与阴影的温柔、从容简化，以朦胧、略带颗粒或纹理质感取代 crisp 数字洁净，优先舒适而非技术雄心的轻松、mellow 氛围，以及手工感、cozy、毫不费力未打磨的怀旧、chill 真诚感。受影响于：lo-fi 美学运动、随意速写插画技法、chillwave 视觉文化实践、当代放松数字艺术方法。

Pastel-Toned Cute Illustration (Western-Korean Hybrid): A gentle, softly rounded illustration style built on smooth, evenly diffused shading applied with cheerful, uncomplicated clarity, delicate blush-and-glow accents settling softly across rendered features, a hushed, sweet stillness carried through clean, uncluttered composition, an appealing blend of Western cartoon roundness and Korean kawaii-adjacent daintiness in proportion and expression, and a wholesome, gently affectionate charm that feels bright, sincere, and effortlessly endearing. Influenced by: contemporary Korean kawaii-illustration tradition, Western flat-cartoon character-design technique, cute-culture digital-illustration practice, pastel-focused character-art method.
Pastel-Toned Cute Illustration (Western-Korean Hybrid)（粉彩可爱插画·西方-韩国混合）：一种温柔、柔和圆润的插画风格，以欢快、不复杂清晰度应用的平滑、均匀漫射阴影，轻柔 settling 于渲染特征的精致泛红与发光点缀，干净、不杂乱构图携带的轻声、甜美静谧，比例与表情中西方卡通圆润与韩国 kawaii 邻接精致的可人融合，以及明亮、真诚、毫不费力 endearing 的 wholesome、温柔亲近魅力。受影响于：当代韩国 kawaii 插画传统、西方平涂卡通角色设计技法、可爱文化数字插画实践、粉彩聚焦角色艺术方法。

Glen Keane Style: A fluid, expressive hand-drawn animation illustration style built on confident, sweeping linework that carries real weight and momentum through every gesture, richly modeled form built with sculptural, almost three-dimensional volume despite being fundamentally linear, exaggerated yet anatomically grounded proportion emphasizing emotional expressiveness over rigidity, a warm, painterly sense of light suggested through soft graphite-like shading beneath confident contour, and a masterful, deeply emotive hand-drawn intensity that feels alive, weighty, and effortlessly graceful. Influenced by: Glen Keane, golden-age Disney animation tradition, expressive hand-drawn character-animation technique, sculptural gesture-drawing practice.
Glen Keane Style（葛连·基恩风格）：一种流体、富有表现力的手绘动画插画风格，以通过每个 gesture 携带真实重量与 momentum 的自信、sweeping 线条，尽管本质线性却以雕塑般、近三维体积构建的丰富建模形态，优先情感表现力而非 rigid 的夸张却解剖扎实的比例，自信轮廓下柔软类石墨阴影暗示的温暖、绘画性光感，以及 alive、有重量、毫不费力优雅的 masterful、深沉情感手绘强度。受影响于：Glen Keane、黄金时代迪士尼动画传统、富有表现力手绘角色动画技法、雕塑性 gesture 绘画实践。

Calligraphic Linework: refined line-centric aesthetics characterized by fluid strokes, graceful visual rhythm, expressive contour variation, elegant mark-making, and exceptional draftsmanship, emphasizing movement, balance, and artistic confidence through controlled line weight, organic transitions, and a harmonious interplay between precision and spontaneity, reminiscent of Egon Schiele and Katsuya Terada, inspired by The Book of Kells and Ukiyo-e brush drawing traditions.0
Calligraphic Linework（书法性线条）：以流体 strokes、优雅视觉节奏、表现性轮廓变化、优雅 mark-making，以及卓越造型为特征的精炼线条中心美学，通过受控线重、有机过渡，以及精度与自发之间的和谐互动，强调运动、平衡与艺术自信；令人联想到 Egon Schiele 与 Katsuya Terada，灵感来自《凯尔经》与浮世绘笔绘传统。

Traditional Marker Illustration: handcrafted illustration aesthetics characterized by visible marker strokes, layered pigment buildup, rich tonal layering, controlled color blending, and confident sketch-based construction. The style emphasizes spontaneity, design clarity, and tactile artistic process, combining expressive linework with deliberate rendering decisions that preserve the character of traditional media. Rather than pursuing flawless digital smoothness, it celebrates material texture, artist hand presence, and dynamic visual energy through overlapping strokes, selective detail, and efficient visual communication. The resulting imagery feels immediate, vibrant, and authentically crafted, balancing presentation-quality finish with the warmth and personality of traditional illustration techniques, reminiscent of Mike Mignola's marker studies and Scott Robertson, inspired by The Skillful Huntsman and Design Studio Press marker rendering tutorials.
Traditional Marker Illustration（传统马克笔插画）：以可见马克笔 strokes、层叠颜料堆积、丰富色调层叠、受控色彩混合，以及自信基于速写的构建为特征的手工插画美学。该风格强调自发、设计清晰度与 tactile 艺术过程，将表现性线条与保留传统媒介特性的 deliberate 渲染决策相结合；不追求无瑕数字平滑，而通过重叠 strokes、选择性细节与高效视觉传达，赞美材质纹理、艺术家手迹与动态视觉能量，结果即时、vibrant、真实考究，平衡展示级完成度与传统插画技法的温暖个性；令人联想到 Mike Mignola 的马克笔研究与 Scott Robertson，灵感来自《熟练的猎人》与 Design Studio Press 马克笔渲染教程。

Western Cel-Shaded Illustration: A bright, cheerful cartoon-illustration style built on thick, confident discontinuous outline holding rounded, super-deformed proportion, flat, evenly applied color fill with minimal shading beyond soft contact shadow, sparkling highlight accents for maximum emotional charm, energetic frame, and a joyful, polish that feels playful, festive, and effortlessly appealing. Influenced by: Nintendo Kirby promotional art,Western indie-cartoon design tradition, flat-color illustration technique, celebratory merchandise-illustration practice, contemporary Western adjacent cartoon method.
Western Cel-Shaded Illustration（西方赛璐珞上色插画）：一种明亮、欢快的卡通插画风格，以固定圆润、super-deformed 比例的厚实、自信不连续轮廓，除柔和接触阴影外极少阴影的均匀平涂纯色填充，为最大情感魅力服务的 sparkling 高光点缀，充满能量的画幅，以及俏皮、festive、毫不费力吸引人的欢乐、抛光感。受影响于：任天堂星之卡比宣传艺术、西方独立卡通设计传统、平涂插画技法、庆祝性周边插画实践、当代西方邻接卡通方法。

Mad Magazine Cover Art: satirical caricature composition anchored by a central caricatured subject rendered with exaggerated proportions, grotesque distorted vibes, meticulously crosshatched shading and stippled textures building a grotesque yet highly detailed surface,overly-exaggerated grimacing subject, the linework loose but masterfully controlled with varying pen weights, the atmosphere relentlessly irreverent, mischievous, and subversive, parody. Influenced by: Mort Drucker's cinematic caricature mastery, Jack Davis's frantic crosshatched scene, Don Martin's rubbery distortions, classic EC Comics, propaganda-poster parody of early Mad magazine,
Mad Magazine Cover Art（《疯杂志》封面艺术）：以夸张比例、grotesque 扭曲气质、构建 grotesque 却高细节表面的精细交叉排线阴影与点刻纹理、过度夸张做鬼脸主体渲染的 satirical 漫画肖像构图；线条松散却 masterfully 受控、带变化的笔压，氛围 relentlessly irreverent、mischievous、subversive，parody。受影响于：Mort Drucker 的电影感漫画肖像 mastery、Jack Davis 的 frantic 交叉排线场景、Don Martin 的橡皮扭曲、经典 EC 漫画、早期《Mad》杂志的宣传海报 parody。

Stick-Figure Child Drawing: A radically minimal illustration style built on the barest possible linework needed to suggest form, simple circular head and straight-line limbs replacing any anatomical detail entirely, a wobbly, unpracticed economy of gesture , and a charmingly crude, innocent simplicity that feels spontaneous, playful, and completely unpolished. Influenced by: child-art tradition, crayon drawing technique, kindergarten art, naive-art,
Stick-Figure Child Drawing（火柴人儿童画）：一种激进极简的插画风格，以暗示形态所需的最少线条，以简单圆形头与直筒四肢完全取代任何解剖细节，wobbly、无练习的 gesture 经济，以及自发、俏皮、完全未打磨的迷人粗陋、天真简化感。受影响于：儿童艺术传统、蜡笔绘画技法、幼儿园艺术、naive-art。

Photography: A naturalistic image-capture style built on realistic light behavior and tonal rendering true to how a camera records a scene, balanced exposure calibrated for believable, accurate representation, honest, unmanipulated framing that favors authentic likeness over stylization, subtle, natural depth-of-field separation distinguishing subject from surroundings, and a straightforward, credible visual clarity rooted in standard photographic convention. Influenced by: standard photographic-capture tradition, professional camera-technique practice, naturalistic exposure method, conventional portrait-photography convention.
Photography（摄影）：一种基于真实光行为与色调渲染、忠于相机记录场景的自然主义图像捕捉风格，为可信、准确再现校准的平衡曝光，优先真实肖像而非风格化的诚实、未操纵取景，区分主体与环境的微妙自然景深分离，以及根植标准摄影惯例的直接、可信视觉清晰度。受影响于：标准摄影捕捉传统、专业相机技法实践、自然主义曝光方法、常规肖像摄影惯例。

35mm Photography: A classic film-photography style built on natural grain structure inherent to the smaller film format, a slightly compressed yet intimate depth-of-field character distinct from larger formats, honest, unmanipulated tonal rendering shaped by the chemistry of the film stock itself, unhurried, documentary-adjacent framing suited to spontaneous, handheld capture, and a timeless, tactile authenticity rooted in decades of photographic tradition. Influenced by: 35mm film photography tradition, photojournalism practice, Henri Cartier-Bresson candid-capture method, analog darkroom-development technique.
35mm Photography（35mm 摄影）：一种经典胶片摄影风格，以较小画幅固有的自然颗粒结构，区别于大画幅的略压缩却亲密的景深特征，由胶片乳剂化学本身塑造的诚实、未操纵色调渲染，适合自发、手持捕捉的从容、类纪实取景，以及根植数十年摄影传统的永恒、tactile 真实性。受影响于：35mm 胶片摄影传统、新闻摄影实践、Henri Cartier-Bresson 决定性瞬间方法、模拟暗房冲洗技法。

Adobe Lightroom Masterpiece: A meticulously graded photographic finishing style built on precise tonal curve control that balances highlight roll-off and shadow depth with editorial exactness, subtle clarity and texture enhancement that sharpens fine detail without tipping into artificiality, carefully calibrated dynamic range that preserves nuance across the full tonal spectrum, refined selective dodging and burning that guides the eye with quiet confidence, and a polished, professional-grade finishing intensity that elevates a raw capture into a considered, gallery-ready photographic statement. Influenced by: professional photo-retouching workflow, Adobe Lightroom color-grading technique, editorial photography post-production, fine-art print finishing.
Adobe Lightroom Masterpiece（Adobe Lightroom 大师级调色）：一种精细分级的摄影后期风格，以平衡高光滚降与阴影纵深的编辑级精确色调曲线控制，在不落入 artificiality 前提下锐化精细细节的微妙清晰度与纹理增强，保留全色调谱细微差别的精心校准动态范围，以安静自信引导视线的精炼选择性减淡与加深，以及将 raw 捕捉提升为考究、画廊级摄影陈述的抛光、专业级后期强度。受影响于：专业照片润色工作流、Adobe Lightroom 调色技法、编辑摄影后期、fine-art 印刷后期。

Analog Photography: A tactile, organic photographic style built on natural grain structure that gives every surface a subtle, unpolished texture rather than digital smoothness, gentle imperfections in tonal transition and mild halation around bright highlights that soften harsh contrast, a slightly muted, imperfect dynamic range that feels handled and physical rather than clinically corrected, unhurried natural or available light rendered with honest, unmanipulated character, and a nostalgic, authentic materiality that carries the quiet imperfection of chemistry and light captured on film. Influenced by: 35mm film photography tradition, darkroom development technique, analog photojournalism practice, medium-format film camera aesthetic.
Analog Photography（胶片摄影）：一种 tactile、有机的摄影风格，以赋予每个表面微妙、未抛光纹理而非数字平滑的自然颗粒结构，柔化 harsh 对比的色调过渡温和瑕疵与亮部周围轻度光晕，感觉被处理、物理而非临床修正的略 muted、不完美动态范围，以诚实、未操纵特性渲染的从容自然或可用光，以及携带化学与光定格于胶片之安静不完美的怀旧、真实材质感。受影响于：35mm 胶片摄影传统、暗房冲洗技法、模拟新闻摄影实践、中画幅胶片相机美学。

Annie Leibovitz Portraiture: cinematic portrait photography distinguished by sophisticated storytelling, meticulously crafted lighting, rich tonal depth, editorial elegance, and a strong sense of personality conveyed through refined composition and emotional presence, balancing realism with carefully directed visual narrative to achieve iconic and memorable imagery, reminiscent of Annie Leibovitz and Richard Avedon, inspired by Vanity Fair Portraits and Rolling Stone Cover Photography.
Annie Leibovitz Portraiture（安妮·莱博维茨肖像摄影）：以成熟叙事、精心打造灯光、丰富色调纵深、编辑优雅，以及通过精炼构图与情感存在传达的强烈个性为特征的电影感肖像摄影，平衡写实与精心导演的视觉叙事，达成 iconic、难忘的图像；令人联想到 Annie Leibovitz 与 Richard Avedon，灵感来自《Vanity Fair》肖像与《Rolling Stone》封面摄影。

Atmospheric Matte Painting: cinematic worldbuilding aesthetics emphasizing expansive visual depth, seamless environmental integration, refined lighting gradients, realistic atmospheric perspective, meticulously crafted detail distribution, and a strong sense of scale, creating immersive and believable imagery through polished rendering, subtle mood construction, and production-quality visual storytelling, reminiscent of Dylan Cole and Craig Mullins, inspired by The Lord of the Rings film matte paintings and Avatar.
Atmospheric Matte Painting（氛围接景绘画）：强调 expansive 视觉纵深、无缝环境融合、精炼灯光渐变、真实大气透视、精心细节分布，以及强烈规模感的电影感世界观构建美学，通过抛光渲染、微妙情绪构建与制作级视觉叙事，创作沉浸、可信的图像；令人联想到 Dylan Cole 与 Craig Mullins，灵感来自《指环王》电影接景与《阿凡达》。

Backlit Portraiture: luminous photographic aesthetics defined by strong backlighting, radiant edge illumination, subtle translucency effects, atmospheric depth, graceful tonal transitions, and a heightened sense of visual separation, creating elegant and emotionally evocative imagery through carefully controlled exposure, naturalistic light diffusion, and refined portrait craftsmanship, reminiscent of Peter Lindbergh and Paolo Roversi, inspired by Vogue editorials and In the Mood for Love.
Backlit Portraiture（逆光肖像）：以强逆光、发光边缘照明、微妙半透明效果、氛围纵深、优雅色调过渡，以及强化视觉分离为定义的发光摄影美学，通过受控曝光、自然主义光扩散与精炼肖像工艺，创作优雅、情感 evocative 的图像；令人联想到 Peter Lindbergh 与 Paolo Roversi，灵感来自《Vogue》编辑大片与《花样年华》。

Black And White Documentary: timeless documentary photography aesthetics emphasizing strong tonal separation, rich grayscale rendering, authentic visual storytelling, observational realism, natural light behavior, subtle film grain, and emotional immediacy, capturing people and moments with honesty, depth, and human presence through restrained composition and enduring photographic craftsmanship, reminiscent of Sebastião Salgado and Henri Cartier-Bresson, inspired by Workers and The Decisive Moment.
Black And White Documentary（黑白纪实）：强调强色调分离、丰富灰阶渲染、真实视觉叙事、观察式写实、自然光行为、微妙胶片颗粒与情感即时的永恒纪实摄影美学，通过克制构图与持久摄影工艺，以诚实、纵深与人的存在捕捉人与瞬间；令人联想到 Sebastião Salgado 与 Henri Cartier-Bresson，灵感来自《Workers》与《决定性瞬间》。

Bleach Bypass Photography: cinematic photographic aesthetics characterized by desaturated tonal richness, elevated contrast, metallic surface response, dense shadow structure, preserved highlight detail, and a gritty yet highly controlled visual atmosphere, creating imagery with dramatic emotional weight and production-grade realism through sophisticated tonal compression and refined filmic rendering, reminiscent of Roger Deakins and Janusz Kamiński, inspired by Saving Private Ryan and Minority Report.
Bleach Bypass Photography（省略漂白摄影）：以去饱和色调丰富度、提升对比、金属表面响应、密集阴影结构、保留高光细节，以及粗粝却高度受控视觉氛围为特征的电影感摄影美学，通过成熟色调压缩与精炼胶片式渲染，创作具戏剧情感重量与制作级写实的图像；令人联想到 Roger Deakins 与 Janusz Kamiński，灵感来自《拯救大兵瑞恩》与《少数派报告》。

Blue Hour Cinema: cinematic visual aesthetics defined by soft transitional illumination, subtle atmospheric depth, refined tonal gradients, naturalistic mood, and elegant color relationships, creating a sense of quiet emotional intensity and immersive realism through sophisticated lighting design, polished rendering, and carefully balanced visual storytelling, reminiscent of Roger Deakins and Greig Fraser, inspired by Skyfall and Blade Runner 2049.
Blue Hour Cinema（蓝调时刻电影感）：以柔和过渡照明、微妙氛围纵深、精炼色调渐变、自然主义情绪，以及优雅色彩关系为定义的电影感视觉美学，通过成熟灯光设计、抛光渲染与精心平衡视觉叙事，创作安静情感强度与沉浸写实的感受；令人联想到 Roger Deakins 与 Greig Fraser，灵感来自《007：大破天幕杀机》与《银翼杀手2049》。

Butterfly Lighting: classic portrait photography aesthetics characterized by symmetrical facial illumination, refined shadow placement, sculptural volume, elegant tonal transitions, and timeless studio craftsmanship, creating a polished and flattering visual presentation through carefully controlled frontal lighting, balanced contrast, and sophisticated portrait execution, reminiscent of George Hurrell and Yousuf Karsh, inspired by Hollywood Glamour Photography and Vanity Fair Portraiture.
Butterfly Lighting（蝴蝶光）：以对称面部照明、精炼阴影放置、雕塑性体积、优雅色调过渡，以及永恒影棚工艺为特征的经典肖像摄影美学，通过受控正面光、平衡对比与成熟肖像执行，创作抛光、讨好的视觉呈现；令人联想到 George Hurrell 与 Yousuf Karsh，灵感来自好莱坞魅力摄影与《Vanity Fair》肖像。

Candid Photography: A natural, unposed photographic style built on available or ambient light captured as it falls rather than controlled or arranged, spontaneous, in-motion framing that favors authentic gesture over composed stillness, a slightly imperfect, unrefined tonal balance that preserves the honesty of the captured moment, subtle grain and soft focus falloff typical of quick, reactive capture rather than deliberate setup, and an intimate, documentary-style authenticity that feels observed rather than performed. Influenced by: street photography tradition, photojournalism practice, documentary candid-capture technique, available-light shooting method.
Candid Photography（抓拍摄影）：一种自然、无摆姿的摄影风格，以捕捉落下而非控制或安排的可用/环境光，优先真实 gesture 而非 composed 静止的自发、运动中取景，保留捕捉瞬间诚实的略不完美、未精炼色调平衡，典型快速反应捕捉而非 deliberate 设置的中速颗粒与柔焦衰减，以及观察而非表演的亲密、纪录片式真实性。受影响于：街头摄影传统、新闻摄影实践、纪实抓拍技法、可用光拍摄方法。

Cinematic Photography: A dramatically composed photographic style built on deliberate, film-like framing that favors wide aspect ratios and purposeful negative space, controlled color-grading logic that unifies highlight and shadow into a single cohesive mood, soft, motivated lighting sources that feel narratively justified rather than purely technical, a patient, unhurried compositional stillness charged with implied story beyond the frame, and a polished, narrative-driven visual gravitas rooted in feature-film visual-language tradition. Influenced by: Roger Deakins cinematography tradition, anamorphic-lens visual technique, narrative film-lighting practice, color-grading post-production method.
Cinematic Photography（电影感摄影）：一种戏剧性构图的摄影风格，以优先宽画幅与 purposeful 负空间的 deliberate、类电影取景，将高光与阴影统一为单一 cohesive 情绪的受控调色逻辑，感觉叙事合理而非纯技术的柔软、motivated 光源，承载画框外暗示故事的从容、unhurried 构图静谧，以及根植院线电影视觉语言传统的抛光、叙事驱动视觉庄严感。受影响于：Roger Deakins 摄影传统、变形宽银幕镜头视觉技法、叙事电影灯光实践、调色后期方法。

Commercial Beauty Photography: luxury commercial photography aesthetics characterized by flawless presentation, sophisticated studio lighting, immaculate detail reproduction, refined tonal transitions, premium surface rendering, and high-end editorial polish, emphasizing visual perfection, elegance, and market-ready image quality through meticulous craftsmanship, controlled composition, and contemporary beauty-industry standards, reminiscent of Solve Sundsbo and Mario Testino, inspired by Vogue Beauty Covers and Dior Beauty Campaigns.
Commercial Beauty Photography（商业美容摄影）：以无瑕呈现、成熟影棚灯光、immaculate 细节再现、精炼色调过渡、premium 表面渲染，以及高端编辑抛光为特征的奢华商业摄影美学，通过精细工艺、受控构图与当代美容行业标准，强调视觉完美、优雅与市售图像质量；令人联想到 Solve Sundsbø 与 Mario Testino，灵感来自《Vogue》美容封面与 Dior 美容 campaign。

Editorial Photography: A considered, narrative-driven photographic style built on deliberate, purposeful lighting calibrated to support a story rather than purely flatter, confident, composed framing designed for immediate publication-ready clarity, a polished, magazine-grade retouching finish that idealizes without erasing character, meticulous attention to context and mood woven into every compositional choice, and a sharp, professional editorial authority that feels timely, considered, and unmistakably publication-ready. Influenced by: contemporary editorial-photography tradition, magazine-photography technique, narrative-driven photojournalism practice, publication-photography method.
Editorial Photography（编辑摄影）：一种考究、叙事驱动的摄影风格，以经校准为支持故事而非纯讨好的 deliberate、purposeful 灯光，为即时出版级清晰度设计的自信、composed 取景，理想化而不抹除个性的抛光、杂志级润色完成度，编织进每个构图选择的上下文与情绪精细考究，以及及时、考究、毋庸置疑出版就绪的锐利、专业编辑权威感。受影响于：当代编辑摄影传统、杂志摄影技法、叙事驱动新闻摄影实践、出版摄影方法。

Cross Process Photography: experimental photographic aesthetics characterized by unconventional tonal relationships, heightened contrast, distinctive film-derived color shifts, graphic visual impact, and an unpredictable analog character, blending technical imperfection with artistic intent through expressive processing artifacts, atmospheric depth, and a bold editorial sensibility, reminiscent of Nick Knight and Sølve Sundsbø, inspired by Dazed & Confused editorials and i-D Magazine photography.
Cross Process Photography（交叉冲印摄影）：以非常规色调关系、提升对比、独特胶片衍生色彩偏移、图形视觉冲击，以及不可预测模拟特性为特征的实验摄影美学，通过表现性处理 artifacts、氛围纵深与 bold 编辑感，将技术不完美与艺术意图相融合；令人联想到 Nick Knight 与 Sølve Sundsbø，灵感来自《Dazed & Confused》编辑大片与《i-D》杂志摄影。

Cross-Processed Film Photography: A chemically unpredictable photographic technique built on deliberately mismatched development chemistry that shifts tonal balance into unexpected, unnatural territory, a bold, punchy contrast that pushes highlights and shadows into exaggerated separation, an unstable, shifting cast that colors the entire frame with an unpredictable tonal signature, a gritty, slightly degraded texture inherent to the chemical mismatch, and a raw, experimental authenticity that turns process error into deliberate aesthetic signature. Influenced by: cross-processing darkroom technique, experimental analog-photography tradition, fashion-editorial film practice, alternative-process photographic method.
Cross-Processed Film Photography（胶片交叉冲印摄影）：一种化学上不可预测的摄影技法，以刻意错配的显影化学将色调平衡推入意外、不自然领域，将高光与阴影推入夸张分离的 bold、punchy 对比，为整个画幅染上不可预测色调签名的 unstable、shifting 色偏，化学错配固有的粗粝、略降级纹理，以及将过程错误转为 deliberate 美学签名的 raw、实验真实性。受影响于：交叉冲印暗房技法、实验模拟摄影传统、时尚编辑胶片实践、替代工艺摄影方法。

Cyberpunk Photography: A high-contrast, neon-drenched photographic style built on intense directional gel lighting that crosses the frame in sharp, saturated bands, wet-look reflective skin and atmosphere achieved through deliberate humid haze and moisture-catching highlight, dramatic low-angle framing that dwarfs the subject within looming urban density, deep engulfing shadow broken only by isolated glowing accents, and a cold, futuristic tension charged with dystopian nocturnal energy. Influenced by: Blade Runner cinematography tradition, neon-noir photography technique, cyberpunk urban-photography practice, gel-lighting cinematic method.
Cyberpunk Photography（赛博朋克摄影）：一种高对比、霓虹浸透的摄影风格，以横跨画幅、呈锐利饱和带的强烈方向性 gel 灯光，通过刻意湿润雾气与捕光高光的湿面反射皮肤与氛围，以压倒性城市密度 dwarf 主体的戏剧性低角度取景，仅被孤立发光点缀打破的深吞噬阴影，以及充满反乌托邦夜间能量的寒冷、未来主义张力。受影响于：《银翼杀手》摄影传统、霓虹 noir 摄影技法、赛博朋克城市摄影实践、gel 灯光电影方法。

Fish-Eye Photography: A dramatically distorted photographic technique built on an ultra-wide spherical field of view that curves straight lines into pronounced convex bowing, an exaggerated sense of depth that pushes the center forward while compressing the periphery into curved recession, a heightened, immersive perspective that feels expansive and slightly disorienting, meticulous edge-to-edge sharpness typical of the lens's extreme depth of field, and a playful, surreal spatial distortion that transforms ordinary framing into something vividly exaggerated. Influenced by: fisheye-lens photographic tradition, ultra-wide-angle technique, skateboard and action-sports photography practice, experimental distortion-lens method.
Fish-Eye Photography（鱼眼摄影）：一种戏剧性扭曲的摄影技法，以将直线弯成明显凸形弓的超大球形视场，将中心推前、同时压缩边缘进入弯曲后退的夸张纵深感，感到 expansive、略 disorienting 的强化沉浸透视，典型镜头超大景深的边缘到边缘锐利，以及将普通取景变为 vividly 夸张的俏皮、超现实空间扭曲。受影响于：鱼眼镜头摄影传统、超广角技法、滑板与动作运动摄影实践、实验扭曲镜头方法。

Surveillance Camera Photo: A detached, utilitarian photographic style built on flat, unflattering illumination typical of fixed automated capture rather than deliberate lighting design, a grainy, low-resolution degradation inherent to compressed continuous recording, a wide, slightly distorted angle characteristic of fixed elevated positioning, a clinical, indifferent framing that feels observed rather than composed, and a cold, detached authenticity that carries an unmistakably impersonal, institutional quality. Influenced by: closed-circuit surveillance-camera technique, low-resolution CCTV footage aesthetic, fixed-angle security-camera practice, found-footage documentary method.
Surveillance Camera Photo（监控摄像头照片）：一种 detached、实用的摄影风格，以固定自动捕捉而非 deliberate 灯光设计的 flat、不讨好的照明，压缩连续录制固有的颗粒、低分辨率降级，固定高位特征的典型 wide、略扭曲角度，感觉被观察而非 composed 的临床、indifferent 取景，以及携带毋庸置疑非个人、机构特质的寒冷、detached 真实性。受影响于：闭路监控摄像头技法、低分辨率 CCTV 画面美学、固定角度安防摄像头实践、found-footage 纪录方法。

Direct-Flash Photography: A raw, unflinching photographic style built on a single harsh, on-axis burst of light that flattens form and eliminates subtlety, stark, high-contrast shadow falling immediately and abruptly behind the subject, an unretouched, unglamorous rendering quality that captures every texture with blunt honesty, a candid, confrontational immediacy carried through the flash's split-second harshness, and a gritty, documentary-style rawness that feels unfiltered, direct, and unmistakably real. Influenced by: Bruce Gilden street-photography tradition, direct on-camera-flash technique, raw documentary-photography practice, unretouched candid-capture method.
Direct-Flash Photography（直闪摄影）：一种 raw、不回避的摄影风格，以单一 harsh、同轴爆发光 flatten 形态、消除微妙，立即、突兀落在主体后的 stark、高对比阴影，以 blunt 诚实捕捉每个纹理的未润色、不 glamour 渲染质量，通过闪光 split-second harshness 携带的 candid、对抗性即时感，以及未过滤、直接、毋庸置疑真实的粗粝、纪录片式 raw 感。受影响于：Bruce Gilden 街头摄影传统、机载直闪技法、raw 纪实摄影实践、未润色抓拍方法。

Dutch Angle Photography: dynamic photographic aesthetics characterized by intentional camera tilt, visual tension, dramatic perspective, heightened energy, and cinematic composition, using unconventional framing to create psychological intensity and narrative momentum through controlled imbalance, atmospheric depth, refined lighting, and striking visual storytelling, reminiscent of Orson Welles and Gregg Toland, inspired by The Third Man and Citizen Kane.
Dutch Angle Photography（荷兰角摄影）：以刻意相机倾斜、视觉张力、戏剧性透视、强化能量，以及电影感构图为特征的动态摄影美学，通过受控不平衡、氛围纵深、精炼灯光与惊人视觉叙事，用非传统取景创作心理强度与叙事 momentum；令人联想到 Orson Welles 与 Gregg Toland，灵感来自《第三人》与《公民凯恩》。

Full Body Photo: A comprehensive figure-photography style built on balanced, even lighting that renders the entire form with consistent clarity from head to foot, deliberate framing calibrated to capture full proportion and posture without cropping, a natural depth-of-field logic that keeps the whole figure sharply resolved, confident, considered posing designed to showcase silhouette and stance in equal measure, and a polished, complete photographic clarity that feels deliberate, well-composed, and fully contextualized. Influenced by: full-length portrait-photography tradition, fashion-lookbook photographic technique, studio full-figure practice, editorial fashion-photography method.
Full Body Photo（全身照）：一种全面人物摄影风格，以从头部到脚部一致清晰度渲染整个形态的均衡、均匀灯光，为捕捉完整比例与姿态而不裁切的 deliberate 取景，保持整个身形锐利解析的自然景深逻辑，为同等展示剪影与站姿设计的自信、考究姿态，以及 deliberate、构图良好、完全语境化的抛光、完整摄影清晰度。受影响于：全身肖像摄影传统、时尚 lookbook 摄影技法、影棚全身实践、编辑时尚摄影方法。

GQ Photoshoot: A polished, confident editorial photography style built on crisp, controlled lighting that renders tailored form and texture with sharp, masculine clarity, bold, assured framing calibrated for magazine-cover authority and instant visual presence, a refined, understated retouching finish that emphasizes strength and composure without artificiality, striking directional key light carving confident definition across features and fabric alike, and a commanding, aspirational editorial polish that feels contemporary, assured, and effortlessly stylish. Influenced by: GQ magazine editorial tradition, high-fashion menswear photography technique, luxury glossy-print retouching practice, contemporary lifestyle campaign photography.
GQ Photoshoot（GQ 风格拍摄）：一种波兰、自信的编辑摄影风格，以 crisp、受控灯光以锐利、男性化清晰度渲染剪裁形态与纹理，为杂志封面权威与瞬时视觉存在校准的 bold、笃定取景，强调力量与镇定而不 artificiality 的精炼、低调润色完成度，在特征与织物上同样刻出自信定义的高冲击方向主光，以及当代、笃定、毫不费力时髦的命令式、aspirational 编辑抛光感。受影响于：GQ 杂志编辑传统、高端男装摄影技法、奢华 glossy 印刷润色实践、当代生活方式 campaign 摄影。

HDR Photography: A richly detailed photographic technique built on merging multiple exposures to capture an expanded tonal range beyond a single capture's limitation, meticulously balanced highlight and shadow detail that reveals nuance across the entire dynamic spectrum simultaneously, a hyper-clarified, almost otherworldly evenness in illumination that flattens typical contrast extremes, dense, saturated micro-contrast enhancing texture across every plane, and a technically ambitious, heightened-reality intensity that pushes photographic capture toward a hyper-vivid, almost painterly clarity. Influenced by: high-dynamic-range exposure-blending technique, tone-mapping photographic practice, landscape HDR-photography tradition, technically enhanced digital-photography method.
HDR Photography（HDR 摄影）：一种丰富细节的摄影技法，以合并多曝光捕捉超出单次捕捉限制的扩展色调范围，同时揭示整个动态谱细微差别的精心平衡高光与阴影细节，flatten 典型对比极端的 hyper-clarified、近乎超凡 evenness，增强每个平面纹理的密集、饱和微对比，以及将摄影捕捉推向 hyper-vivid、近乎绘画清晰的技术雄心、强化现实强度。受影响于：高动态范围曝光混合技法、色调映射摄影实践、风景 HDR 摄影传统、技术增强数字摄影方法。

High-Key Lighting Photography: A bright, evenly luminous photographic style built on soft, diffused illumination from multiple balanced sources that minimizes shadow and contrast almost entirely, a clean, airy tonal range concentrated in the upper end of the exposure scale, gentle wraparound light that flatters every plane of the subject without harsh falloff, a crisp, optimistic clarity free of moody depth, and a polished, commercial-grade brightness that reads as open, cheerful, and immaculately controlled. Influenced by: high-key studio photography technique, commercial beauty lighting tradition, fashion catalog photography, softbox lighting method.
High-Key Lighting Photography（高调光摄影）：一种明亮、均匀发光的摄影风格，以最小化阴影与对比、几乎完全的多源柔和漫射照明，集中于曝光尺度上端的干净、airy 色调范围，无 harsh 衰减 flattering 每个平面的温柔包裹光，无 moody 纵深的 crisp、乐观清晰度，以及开放、欢快、immaculately 受控的抛光、商业级亮度。受影响于：高调影棚摄影技法、商业美容灯光传统、时尚目录摄影、柔光箱布光方法。

Hitchcockian Photo: A meticulously composed suspense-driven photographic style built on deliberate, controlled framing that withholds and reveals information with calculated precision, dramatic, high-contrast lighting that isolates the subject within tense, shadow-heavy surroundings, a voyeuristic, slightly elevated or through-window vantage that implies watching rather than direct confrontation, an unhurried, deliberate stillness charged with mounting psychological tension, and a masterful, cinematic suspense built entirely through composition and restraint rather than overt spectacle. Influenced by: Alfred Hitchcock cinematography tradition, suspense-thriller visual technique, voyeuristic framing practice, classic Hollywood tension-building method.
Hitchcockian Photo（希区柯克式摄影）：一种精心构图、悬念驱动的摄影风格，以刻意、受控取景以计算精度 withholding 与 revealing 信息，将主体孤立于紧张、重阴影环境的戏剧性高对比灯光，暗示观看而非直接对抗的 voyeuristic、略高或透窗视角，承载 mounting 心理张力的从容、deliberate 静谧，以及完全通过构图与克制而非 overt spectacle 构建的 masterful、电影感悬念。受影响于：Alfred Hitchcock 摄影传统、悬念惊悚视觉技法、voyeuristic 取景实践、经典好莱坞张力构建方法。

Film Noir: A stark, high-contrast photographic and cinematic style built on dramatic slatted-shadow patterns cutting sharply across the frame, a single low, hard-angled light source carving deep, engulfing darkness against isolated highlight, a hardboiled, morally ambiguous tension conveyed entirely through shadow and withheld detail, a deliberate, unhurried stillness charged with suspicion and menace, and a moody, cinematic gravitas rooted in mid-century crime-thriller visual tradition. Influenced by: classic film-noir cinematography tradition, chiaroscuro lighting technique, hardboiled crime-thriller visual practice, high-contrast black-and-white photographic method.
Film Noir（黑色电影·其一）：一种 stark、高对比的摄影与电影风格，以戏剧性板条阴影图案横切画幅，单一低、硬角度光源在孤立高光旁刻出深、吞噬性黑暗，完全通过阴影与 withheld 细节传达的 hardboiled、道德模糊张力，承载怀疑与威胁的从容、deliberate 静谧，以及根植中叶世纪犯罪惊悚视觉传统的 moody、电影感庄严感。受影响于：经典黑色电影摄影传统、chiaroscuro 灯光技法、hardboiled 犯罪惊悚视觉实践、高对比黑白摄影方法。

Film Noir: A shadow-drenched visual language built on dramatic chiaroscuro lighting and stark high-contrast monochrome cinematography, where hard-edged pools of illumination carve space from surrounding darkness, a morally ambiguous tonal palette framed through dutch angles and disorienting compositions, persistent atmospheric haze diffusing harsh light sources, gritty urban spatial dynamics defined by reflective wet surfaces and sharp architectural silhouettes, and a haunting, claustrophobic mood where visual tension emerges through the interplay of extreme light and absolute darkness. Influenced by: classic Hollywood noir cinematography, German Expressionist lighting techniques, hardboiled visual aesthetic, 1940s-50s monochrome film stock, chiaroscuro painting tradition, urban night photography.
Film Noir（黑色电影·其二）：一种 shadow-drenched 视觉语言，以戏剧性 chiaroscuro 灯光与 stark 高对比单色电影摄影构建，硬边光池从周围黑暗雕刻空间，通过荷兰角与 disorienting 构图框定的道德模糊色调 palette，扩散 harsh 光源的持续氛围雾，由反射湿面与锐利建筑剪影定义的粗粝城市空间动态，以及通过极端光与绝对黑暗互动涌现视觉张力的 haunting、claustrophobic 情绪。受影响于：经典好莱坞 noir 摄影、德国表现主义灯光技法、hardboiled 视觉美学、1940–50 年代单色胶片、chiaroscuro 绘画传统、城市夜景摄影。

Instagram Model Glamour Photography: A polished, aspirational glamour-photography style built on soft, evenly diffused lighting that flatters skin with a warm, flawless glow, confident curve-forward posing shot with a slight low angle for a flattering elongated silhouette, glossy heavy retouching that smooths texture while keeping a believable, photographic sheen, gentle golden-hour warmth or clean bright-studio evenness depending on setting, and a polished, algorithm-friendly commercial appeal built for maximum aspirational impact. Influenced by: contemporary Instagram influencer photography, commercial glamour retouching technique, social-media beauty content, golden-hour portrait lighting.inspired by Demi Rose photoshoot, Amouranth Stream, Emily Ratajkowski glamour photo,
Instagram Model Glamour Photography（Instagram 模特魅态摄影）：一种抛光、aspirational 的魅态摄影风格，以柔软、均匀漫射光以温暖、无瑕 glow flattering 皮肤，为 flattering 拉长剪影以略低角度拍摄的自信曲线前置姿态，在保持可信照片光泽的同时平滑纹理的 glossy 重润色，依场景而定的温柔黄金时刻暖意或干净明亮影棚均匀感，以及为最大 aspirational 冲击而生的抛光、algorithm-friendly 商业吸引力。受影响于：当代 Instagram 网红摄影、商业魅态润色技法、社交媒体美容内容、黄金时刻肖像灯光；灵感来自 Demi Rose 拍摄、Amouranth 直播、Emily Ratajkowski 魅态照。

Art Photography: A refined, intentional photographic style built on carefully considered light and composition treated as ends in themselves rather than commercial necessity, a contemplative, unhurried approach to framing that favors conceptual depth over immediate legibility, meticulous attention to tonal nuance and print-quality fidelity across the full dynamic range, a restrained, gallery-conscious sensibility that rewards sustained, close viewing, and a timeless, contemplative photographic gravitas rooted in fine-art rather than commercial tradition. Influenced by: fine-art photography tradition, gallery-exhibition photographic practice, contemplative large-format technique, contemporary conceptual-photography method.
Art Photography（艺术摄影）：一种精炼、intentional 的摄影风格，以作为目的本身而非商业必要的精心考究光与构图，优先概念纵深而非即时可读性的沉思、从容取景，对全动态范围色调细微差别与印刷质量保真的精细考究，奖励持续细看的克制、画廊意识气质，以及根植 fine-art 而非商业传统的永恒、沉思摄影庄严感。受影响于：fine-art 摄影传统、画廊展览摄影实践、沉思大画幅技法、当代概念摄影方法。

Kodachrome: A richly saturated film-photography style built on deep, warm tonal density characteristic of the stock's distinctive chemistry, fine grain structure that renders detail with crisp, archival clarity, a slightly compressed dynamic range that favors bold contrast over subtle gradation, a nostalgic, mid-century documentary sincerity carried through unmanipulated natural light, and a timeless, richly saturated authenticity rooted in decades of iconic photojournalism. Influenced by: Kodachrome film-stock tradition, Steve McCurry photojournalism practice, mid-century color-photography method, archival slide-film technique.
Kodachrome（柯达克罗姆）：一种丰富饱和的胶片摄影风格，以乳剂独特化学特征的深、温暖色调密度，以 crisp、档案级清晰度渲染细节的细颗粒结构，优先 bold 对比而非微妙渐变的略压缩动态范围，以未操纵自然光携带的怀旧、中叶世纪纪实真诚感，以及根植数十年 iconic 新闻摄影的永恒、丰富饱和真实性。受影响于：Kodachrome 胶片传统、Steve McCurry 新闻摄影实践、中叶世纪彩色摄影方法、档案反转片技法。

National Geographic Photography: A richly detailed documentary photographic style built on patient, observational framing that captures authentic, unposed moments with narrative depth, natural available light rendered with honest, unmanipulated clarity across a wide dynamic range, meticulous attention to environmental context that grounds every subject within a believable, lived sense of place, an unhurried, respectful stillness carried through careful, considered composition, and a timeless, editorial-grade authenticity that feels both intimate and monumentally significant. Influenced by: National Geographic photojournalism tradition, documentary environmental-portrait technique, long-form editorial photography practice, observational field-photography method.
National Geographic Photography（国家地理摄影）：一种丰富细节的纪实摄影风格，以捕捉具叙事纵深、真实无摆姿瞬间的耐心、观察取景，以诚实、未操纵清晰度跨宽动态范围渲染的自然可用光，将每个主体扎根于可信、lived 在地感的环墶上下文精细考究，通过小心、考究构图携带的从容、respectful 静谧，以及既亲密又具纪念碑意义的永恒、编辑级真实性。受影响于：国家地理新闻摄影传统、纪实环境肖像技法、长形式编辑摄影实践、观察式田野摄影方法。
