Motion Blur Photography: A dynamic, energy-charged photographic style built on deliberate exposure duration that lets movement smear and streak across the frame, sharp, frozen focal elements contrasted against surrounding blurred trails of motion, a kinetic, directional smearing that visually encodes speed and momentum into the still image, unpredictable, organic streaking that varies with the pace and path of movement, and a visceral, immersive sense of velocity that makes stillness itself feel alive with implied motion. Influenced by: long-exposure motion-photography technique, panning-shot photographic practice, kinetic sports-photography method, experimental shutter-speed technique.
Motion Blur Photography（动态模糊摄影）：一种动态、能量满载的摄影风格，以让运动 smear、streak 画幅的 deliberate 曝光时长，与周围运动模糊轨迹对比的锐利、冻结焦点元素，将速度与 momentum 视觉编码进静照的动能、方向性 smearing，随运动节奏与路径变化的不可预测、有机 streaking，以及让静止本身感到 alive、隐含运动的 visceral、沉浸速度感。受影响于：长曝光运动摄影技法、追随拍摄实践、动能体育摄影方法、实验快门速度技法。

Natural Lighting Photography: A soft, organic photographic style built on ambient, unmanipulated illumination shaped entirely by the qualities of available light rather than artificial control, gentle, shifting tonal balance that changes with time and atmosphere, an honest, unforced rendering quality that preserves subtle imperfection and authentic dimensionality, a calm, unhurried stillness carried through patient, observational framing, and a timeless, believable authenticity that feels effortless, grounded, and quietly beautiful. Influenced by: available-light photography tradition, documentary natural-light technique, editorial location-photography practice, observational portrait method.
Natural Lighting Photography（自然光摄影）：一种柔软、有机的摄影风格，以完全由可用光特质塑造、而非人工控制的 ambient、未操纵照明，随时段与氛围变化的温柔、shifting 色调平衡，保留微妙不完美与真实维度的诚实、unforced 渲染质量，通过耐心、观察取景携带的平静、从容静谧，以及毫不费力、扎根、quietly 美丽的永恒、可信真实性。受影响于：可用光摄影传统、纪实自然光技法、编辑外景摄影实践、观察式肖像方法。

Neon Photography: A vivid, high-contrast photographic style built on intense directional glow radiating from concentrated luminous sources, deep, engulfing shadow surrounding pockets of saturated brilliance, sharp reflective bounce catching and refracting colored light across every plane, an electric, nocturnal energy carried through dramatic tonal extremes, and a moody, atmospheric vibrancy that feels charged, urban, and unmistakably after-dark. Influenced by: neon-noir photography tradition, urban night-photography technique, gel-lighting cinematic practice, contemporary nightlife-photography method.
Neon Photography（霓虹摄影）：一种 vivid、高对比的摄影风格，以集中发光源辐射的强烈方向性 glow，包围饱和辉煌光池的深、吞噬阴影，捕捉并折射每平面彩色光的锐利反射弹跳，通过戏剧性色调极端携带的 electric、夜间能量，以及充满 urban、毋庸置疑入夜感的 moody、氛围活力。受影响于：霓虹 noir 摄影传统、城市夜景技法、gel 灯光电影实践、当代夜生活摄影方法。

Low-Key Photography: A moody, dramatically shadowed photographic style built on a single dominant light source that carves the subject out of near-total darkness, a narrow, concentrated tonal range weighted heavily toward deep shadow with only selective highlight breaking through, sharp falloff that lets form dissolve quickly into blackness at the edges, a hushed, introspective intensity carried through restrained, deliberate exposure, and a polished, cinematic gravitas that feels intimate, mysterious, and controlled. Influenced by: low-key studio photography technique, film-noir lighting tradition, chiaroscuro photographic method, dramatic portrait lighting practice.
Low-Key Photography（低调光摄影）：一种 moody、戏剧性阴影的摄影风格，以将主体从近乎全黑中刻出的单一主导光源，向深阴影重度加权、仅选择性高光突破的窄、集中色调范围，让形态在边缘迅速 dissolve 入黑暗的锐利衰减，通过克制、deliberate 曝光携带的轻声、内省强度，以及亲密、神秘、受控的抛光、电影感庄严感。受影响于：低调影棚摄影技法、黑色电影灯光传统、chiaroscuro 摄影方法、戏剧性肖像灯光实践。

Night Photography: A moody, low-light photographic style built on deep, engulfing darkness broken only by isolated pockets of available illumination, long, patient exposure logic that gathers scarce light into soft, glowing pools, a hushed, quiet stillness carried through the extended time required to capture the scene, subtle grain and reduced dynamic range typical of low-light capture rather than artificial correction, and a contemplative, nocturnal atmosphere that feels intimate, mysterious, and quietly suspended in time. Influenced by: available-light night-photography tradition, long-exposure technique, urban nocturne photographic practice, low-light documentary method.
Night Photography（夜景摄影）：一种 moody、低光的摄影风格，以仅被孤立可用光照破的深、吞噬黑暗，将稀缺光收集为柔软发光池的漫长、耐心曝光逻辑，通过捕捉场景所需 extended 时间携带的轻声、quiet 静谧，典型低光捕捉而非人工修正的微妙颗粒与降低动态范围，以及亲密、神秘、quietly 悬停于时间的沉思、夜间氛围。受影响于：可用光夜景摄影传统、长曝光技法、城市夜曲摄影实践、低光纪实方法。

Peter Lindbergh–inspired photography style: timeless portraiture with a raw and humanist aesthetic, natural and available light emphasizing texture over polish, high-contrast yet soft tonal range with deep blacks and luminous skin highlights, minimal retouching preserving wrinkles, pores, and authenticity, straightforward compositions often shot at eye level or slightly low angle, emotional and introspective expressions prioritized over glamour, cinematic use of wind, movement, and imperfect environments, fashion imagery stripped of excess to reveal character and strength, inspired by Peter Lindbergh and Herb Ritts.
Peter Lindbergh–inspired photography style（彼得·林德伯格启发摄影）：具 raw、humanist 美学的永恒肖像，强调纹理而非抛光的天然与可用光，具深黑与发光皮肤高光的高对比却柔和色调范围，保留皱纹、毛孔与真实性的最少润色，常平视或略低角度拍摄的直接构图，优先魅力之上的情感与内省表达，风、运动与不完美环境的电影感运用，剥离多余的时尚图像以显露个性与力量；灵感来自 Peter Lindbergh 与 Herb Ritts。

Photoshoot: A polished, professionally composed photography style built on deliberate, controlled lighting calibrated to flatter and define form with confident clarity, considered, purposeful posing framed for maximum visual intention rather than candid spontaneity, a refined, consistent exposure balance that keeps every plane clearly and evenly rendered, a composed, unhurried stillness carried through careful, methodical setup, and a polished, commercial-grade photographic intensity built for deliberate, high-impact visual presentation. Influenced by: professional studio-photography tradition, commercial portrait-lighting technique, editorial photoshoot production practice, controlled-environment photographic method.
Photoshoot（棚拍）：一种抛光、专业构图的摄影风格，以经校准为以自信清晰度 flattering、定义形态的 deliberate、受控灯光，为最大视觉意图而非 candid 自发设计的考究、purposeful 姿态，保持每个平面清晰均匀渲染的精炼、一致曝光平衡，通过小心、methodical 设置携带的 composed、从容静谧，以及为 deliberate、高冲击视觉呈现而生的抛光、商业级摄影强度。受影响于：专业影棚摄影传统、商业肖像灯光技法、编辑棚拍制作实践、受控环境摄影方法。

Playboy / Maxim-Style Photoshoot: A glossy, confident glamour-photography style built on flattering studio or golden-hour lighting that sculpts skin with a warm, sun-kissed glow, playful, flirtatious posing that favors curve-forward silhouettes and direct eye contact with the camera, saturated yet naturalistic color grading, tasteful wardrobe styling ranging from lingerie to casual glamour wear, and a confident, aspirational lifestyle-magazine polish that balances sensuality with an approachable, editorial sheen. Influenced by: Playboy magazine photography, Maxim magazine editorial style, glamour studio lighting technique, lifestyle commercial photography.
Playboy / Maxim-Style Photoshoot（《花花公子》/《Maxim》风格拍摄）：一种 glossy、自信的魅态摄影风格，以 flattering 影棚或黄金时刻灯光以温暖、sun-kissed glow 雕塑皮肤，优先曲线前置剪影与直视镜头的俏皮、flirtatious 姿态，饱和却自然主义的调色，从内衣到休闲魅态装的得体服装造型，以及平衡感官与易接近编辑光泽的自信、aspirational 生活方式杂志抛光感。受影响于：《花花公子》杂志摄影、《Maxim》杂志编辑风格、魅态影棚灯光技法、生活方式商业摄影。

Hyper Synthwave: A vividly nostalgic digital-aesthetic style built on intense neon glow radiating in sharp, saturated bands, a retro-futuristic grid-logic sense of depth, dramatic backlit silhouette contrast separating form from an electric, pulsing ambiance, dense atmospheric haze scattering luminous color throughout the composition, and a hypnotic, electric nostalgia suspended between 1980s retro-futurism and glowing digital fantasy. Influenced by: synthwave digital-art movement, 1980s retro-futurist visual tradition, neon-grid aesthetic technique, glow-bloom digital-rendering practice.
Hyper Synthwave（超合成波）：一种 vividly 怀旧的数字美学风格，以呈锐利饱和带的强烈霓虹 glow，复古未来主义网格逻辑纵深感，将形态从 electric、pulsing 氛围分离的戏剧性逆光剪影对比，散播发光色彩贯穿构图的密集氛围雾，以及悬于 1980 年代复古未来主义与发光数字幻想之间的催眠、electric 怀旧感。受影响于：synthwave 数字艺术运动、1980 年代复古未来主义视觉传统、霓虹网格美学技法、辉光晕染数字渲染实践。

Selfie Photo: A casual, close-range photographic style built on a slightly distorted, wide-angle-adjacent perspective inherent to arm's-length framing, soft, often front-facing diffused illumination that flattens shadow across the face, an intimate, immediate closeness that favors candid spontaneity over composed distance, a natural, unpolished exposure balance shaped by ambient or handheld conditions rather than deliberate setup, and a familiar, personal authenticity that feels casual, present, and unmistakably first-person. Influenced by: contemporary smartphone-photography convention, front-camera portrait technique, candid self-portraiture practice, casual social-media photographic method.
Selfie Photo（自拍）：一种随意、近距离的摄影风格，以手臂长度取景固有的略扭曲、类广角透视，flatten 面部阴影的柔软、常前置漫射照明，优先 candid 自发而非 composed 距离的亲密、即时亲近，由环境或手持条件而非 deliberate 设置塑造的自然、未抛光曝光平衡，以及 casual、当下、毋庸置疑第一人称的熟悉、个人真实性。受影响于：当代智能手机摄影惯例、前置摄像头肖像技法、candid 自拍实践、随意社交媒体摄影方法。

Sin City Photography: A stark, high-contrast black-and-white photographic style with razor-sharp shadow-to-highlight extremes, deep inky blacks pooling around a single dramatically lit subject, one isolated element rendered in bold selective color against the otherwise monochrome frame, and a hardboiled, pulp-noir cinematic tension dripping with danger and seduction. Influenced by: Sin City (Robert Rodriguez/Frank Miller), film noir cinematography, Frank Miller graphic novel art, chiaroscuro photography lighting.
Sin City Photography（《罪恶之城》摄影）：一种 stark、高对比的黑白摄影风格，具 razor-sharp 阴影到高光的极端，围绕单一戏剧性打光主体汇聚的深墨黑，在否则单色画幅中渲染为 bold 选择性色彩的孤立元素，以及滴着危险与 seduction 的 hardboiled、通俗 noir 电影张力。受影响于：《罪恶之城》（Robert Rodriguez/Frank Miller）、黑色电影摄影、Frank Miller 图像小说艺术、chiaroscuro 摄影灯光。

Soft Beauty-Dish Portrait Rendering: A gently flattering portrait-lighting technique, softly diffused light that wraps the face in even, shadowless illumination, a smooth falloff that eliminates harsh contrast while preserving subtle dimensional modeling beneath the eyes and along the jaw, a glossy, luminous quality settling naturally into the eyes, refined tonal transition that keeps skin reading as soft and touchable rather than flat, and a polished, classically flattering studio-portrait clarity built for timeless, dependable beauty photography. Influenced by: beauty-dish studio-lighting technique, classical glamour-photography practice, softbox-adjacent portrait-lighting method, professional beauty-retouching tradition.
Soft Beauty-Dish Portrait Rendering（柔光美颜罩肖像渲染）：一种 gently flattering 的肖像灯光技法，以将脸包裹于均匀、无影照明的柔软漫射光，消除 harsh 对比同时保留眼下与下颌下微妙立体建模的平滑衰减，自然 settle 入眼睛的 glossy、发光质感，保持皮肤读作柔软可触而非 flat 的精炼色调过渡，以及为永恒、可靠美容摄影而生的抛光、经典 flattering 影棚肖像清晰度。受影响于：美颜罩影棚灯光技法、经典魅力摄影实践、类柔光箱肖像灯光方法、专业美容润色传统。

Streamer Glam Selfie Realism: contemporary social-media aesthetics characterized by premium beauty-focused realism, immaculate skin rendering, sophisticated lens behavior, polished image finishing, flattering facial illumination, and a strong sense of aspirational digital presence. The style emphasizes visual confidence, curated authenticity, lifestyle luxury, and camera-aware presentation through refined detail management, subtle optical effects, clean composition, and influencer-era image culture. Rather than editorial formality or cinematic drama, it prioritizes immediacy, relatability, and high-engagement visual appeal while maintaining a technically refined appearance. The resulting imagery feels modern, charismatic, and effortlessly polished, blending lifestyle branding, beauty photography, and creator-culture aesthetics into a visually compelling digital identity, reminiscent of Sam Dameshek and Brandon Woelfel, inspired by Instagram Creator Culture and Twitch Front Page Photography.
Streamer Glam Selfie Realism（主播魅态自拍写实）：以 premium 美容聚焦写实、immaculate 皮肤渲染、成熟镜头行为、抛光图像后期、flattering 面部照明，以及强 aspirational 数字存在为特征的当代社交媒体美学。该风格强调视觉自信、策划真实性、生活方式奢华，以及通过精炼细节管理、微妙光学效果、干净构图与网红时代图像文化的 camera-aware 呈现；不追求编辑正式或电影戏剧，而优先即时、 relatability 与高 engagement 视觉吸引力，同时保持技术精炼外观，结果现代、有魅力、毫不费力抛光，将生活方式品牌、美容摄影与创作者文化美学融合为视觉 compelling 的数字身份；令人联想到 Sam Dameshek 与 Brandon Woelfel，灵感来自 Instagram 创作者文化与 Twitch 首页摄影。

Supermodel Photoshoot: A polished, high-fashion editorial photography style built on immaculately controlled studio lighting calibrated for maximum sculptural precision and flawless clarity, confident, statuesque posing framed with deliberate, magazine-ready authority, a refined retouching finish that idealizes without erasing distinctive character, dramatic directional key light carving sharp definition across every plane, and a commanding, aspirational editorial polish that feels timeless, glamorous, and unmistakably world-class. Influenced by: high-fashion editorial-photography tradition, luxury studio-lighting technique, professional glamour-retouching practice, haute-couture campaign-photography method.
Supermodel Photoshoot（超模拍摄）：一种波兰、高定编辑摄影风格，以经校准为最大雕塑精度与无瑕清晰度的 immaculately 受控影棚灯光，以 deliberate、杂志级权威框定的自信、statuesque 姿态，理想化而不抹除 distinctive 个性的精炼润色完成度，在每个平面刻出锐利定义的高冲击方向主光，以及永恒、glamorous、毋庸置疑世界级的命令式、aspirational 编辑抛光感。受影响于：高定编辑摄影传统、奢华影棚灯光技法、专业魅态润色实践、高级定制 campaign 摄影方法。

LinkedIn Pro Portrait: A clean, approachable corporate-portrait photography style built on soft, evenly diffused lighting that flatters without dramatic contrast, confident, composed posing that reads as professional yet warm rather than stiff, a polished, subtly retouched finish that preserves natural authenticity over glossy artificiality, gentle, even exposure calibrated for consistent, trustworthy clarity, and a competent, approachable professional polish built for instant credibility at a glance. Influenced by: contemporary corporate-headshot photography, professional business-portrait practice, soft studio-lighting technique, LinkedIn/business-platform portrait convention.
LinkedIn Pro Portrait（LinkedIn 职业肖像）：一种干净、易接近的企业肖像摄影风格，以 flattering 而无戏剧对比的柔软、均匀漫射光，读作专业却温暖而非 stiff 的自信、composed 姿态，优先自然真实而非 glossy artificiality 的抛光、微妙润色完成度，为一致、可信清晰度校准的温柔、均匀曝光，以及一眼即获 instant 可信度的胜任、易接近专业抛光感。受影响于：当代企业头像摄影、专业商务肖像实践、柔和影棚灯光技法、LinkedIn/商业平台肖像惯例。

Sitcom Still: A bright, evenly lit multi-camera photographic style built on flat, shadow-minimizing illumination that keeps every plane clearly and consistently visible, a clean, high-key exposure logic prioritizing legibility over dramatic mood, static, proscenium-adjacent framing that favors clarity and coverage over cinematic composition, an unhurried, staged stillness carried through deliberate, evenly balanced lighting setups, and a familiar, comfortably lit television-studio polish rooted in decades of broadcast-comedy production tradition. Influenced by: multi-camera sitcom lighting tradition, television-studio production practice, broadcast three-point lighting technique, classic American TV-comedy visual convention.
Sitcom Still（情景喜剧静帧）：一种明亮、均匀打光的多机位摄影风格，以最小化阴影、保持每个平面清晰一致可见的 flat、shadow-minimizing 照明，优先可读性而非戏剧情绪的干净、高调曝光逻辑，优先清晰度与覆盖而非电影构图的静态、类 proscenium 取景，通过 deliberate、均匀平衡灯光设置携带的从容、staged 静谧，以及根植数十年广播喜剧制作传统的熟悉、舒适打光电视影棚抛光感。受影响于：多机位情景喜剧灯光传统、电视影棚制作实践、广播三点布光技法、经典美式 TV 喜剧视觉惯例。

Rembrandt-Inspired Photography: A masterfully lit classical portrait-photography style built on single-source "Rembrandt lighting" that carves a small triangular highlight beneath one eye against an otherwise shadowed face emphasizing beauty, deep warm chiaroscuro tones bathing the frame in umber, gold, and near-black, richly textured skin and fabric rendered with painterly tonal depth rather than flat exposure, minimal dark backgrounds that isolate the subject in contemplative stillness, and a quiet gravitas translated into modern photographic technique. Influenced by: Rembrandt van Rijn, chiaroscuro lighting technique, classical portrait painting, fine-art studio photography.,
Rembrandt-Inspired Photography（伦勃朗启发摄影）：一种 masterfully 打光的经典肖像摄影风格，以单一"伦勃朗光"在 otherwise 阴影脸上刻出一眼下小三角高光、强调美，以 umber、gold 与近黑沐浴画幅的深暖 chiaroscuro 调，以绘画性色调纵深而非 flat 曝光渲染的丰富纹理皮肤与织物，孤立主体于沉思静谧的极简暗背景，以及 translated 入现代摄影技法的安静庄严感。受影响于：Rembrandt van Rijn、chiaroscuro 灯光技法、经典肖像绘画、fine-art 影棚摄影。

Stranger Things-Inspired Art: A moody, atmospheric illustration style built on thick, engulfing haze that swallows peripheral detail while a single dramatic source pierces through with sharp, saturated intensity, a nostalgic, retro-cinematic grain layered over the entire composition, tense, low-angle framing that charges ordinary space with looming unease, a deliberate contrast between soft ambient glow and harsh directional flare, and a suspenseful, synth-scored nostalgia that feels equal parts childhood wonder and creeping dread. Influenced by: 1980s horror-cinema visual tradition, retro VHS film-grain technique, practical-effects horror lighting practice, synth-era genre-film aesthetic.
Stranger Things-Inspired Art（《怪奇物语》启发艺术）：一种 moody、氛围插画风格，以吞噬边缘细节、仅单一戏剧源以锐利饱和强度穿透的厚、吞噬雾，覆盖整个构图的怀旧、复古电影颗粒，以 looming 不安 charge 普通空间的紧张、低角度取景，柔和 ambient glow 与 harsh 方向性 flare 之间的 deliberate 对比，以及兼具童年奇迹与 creeping 恐惧的悬念、synth 配乐怀旧感。受影响于：1980 年代恐怖电影视觉传统、复古 VHS 颗粒技法、实用特效恐怖灯光实践、synth 时代类型电影美学。

Vogue Magazine: A high-fashion editorial photography style built on crisp, immaculately controlled studio lighting that renders every plane with flawless clarity and precision, bold, confident close-framed composition designed for instant cover-line impact, a polished, glossy retouching finish that idealizes without erasing character, striking directional key light carving sharp definition across features and fabric alike, and a commanding, aspirational editorial authority that feels simultaneously timeless and of-the-moment. Influenced by: Vogue magazine editorial tradition, high-fashion studio photography technique, luxury glossy-print retouching practice, haute couture campaign photography.
Vogue Magazine（《Vogue》杂志）：一种高定编辑摄影风格，以经 immaculately 受控影棚灯光以无瑕清晰度与精度渲染每个平面，为瞬时封面行冲击设计的 bold、自信近景构图，理想化而不抹除个性的抛光、glossy 润色完成度，在特征与织物上同样刻出锐利定义的高冲击方向主光，以及同时永恒与当下、命令式、aspirational 的编辑权威感。受影响于：《Vogue》杂志编辑传统、高定影棚摄影技法、奢华 glossy 印刷润色实践、高级定制 campaign 摄影。

Golden Hour Photography: A softly directional photographic style built on a low, raking angle of natural illumination that stretches long, gentle shadows across the frame, a diffused, glowing quality softened by increased atmospheric scattering at that time of day, a flattering, low-contrast luminosity that wraps gently around form rather than casting harsh definition, an unhurried, fleeting stillness carried through the brief window of ideal light, and a nostalgic, cinematic tenderness that feels naturally soft and effortlessly atmospheric. Influenced by: natural available-light photography tradition, magic-hour cinematography technique, outdoor portrait-lighting practice, documentary golden-hour shooting method.
Golden Hour Photography（黄金时刻摄影）：一种柔软方向性摄影风格，以 stretching 长、温柔阴影的低、raking 自然光角度，该时段增强大气散射 softened 的漫射、发光质感，wrapping 形态而非投下 harsh 定义的 flattering、低对比发光，通过理想光短暂窗口携带的从容、fleeting 静谧，以及自然柔软、毫不费力氛围的怀旧、电影感温柔感。受影响于：自然可用光摄影传统、magic-hour 电影摄影技法、户外肖像灯光实践、纪实黄金时刻拍摄方法。

Cinematic Tron: A sleek futuristic style built on glowing circuitry lines tracing form like living light against deep matte darkness, hard geometric paneling with a digitized, vector-precise silhouette, volumetric haze and lens bloom radiating off every glowing seam, dramatic wide-scale composition establishing cinematic grandeur within a single sweeping frame, and a cold, high-tech intensity that reads as being rendered from pure light and code. Influenced by: Tron: Legacy production design, Syd Mead, vector and vaporwave digital-art technique, cinematic concept-art lighting method.
Cinematic Tron（电影感《创：战纪》风格）：一种利落未来主义风格，以在深邃哑光黑暗上如活光般勾勒形态的发光电路线，具数字化、矢量精度的硬几何面与剪影，从每道发光接缝辐射的体积雾与镜头晕染，在单一 sweeping 画幅建立电影宏大的戏剧性宽幅构图，以及读作由纯光与代码渲染的寒冷、高科技强度。受影响于：《创：战纪》美术设计、Syd Mead、矢量与 vaporwave 数字艺术技法、电影概念艺术灯光方法。

Brett Weston Photography: A meticulously composed black-and-white photographic style built on sharp, exacting tonal contrast that renders every texture with crystalline precision, abstract, tightly cropped framing that isolates form and pattern from broader context, a deliberate, unhurried attention to graphic composition over narrative content, meticulous control over the full tonal range from deep black to luminous white, and a disciplined, modernist photographic clarity that treats abstraction and form as the entire compositional subject. Influenced by: Brett Weston, Edward Weston, modernist black-and-white photography tradition, straight-photography technique.
Brett Weston Photography（Brett Weston 摄影）：一种精心构图的黑白摄影风格，以渲染每个纹理 crystal 精度的锐利、exacting 色调对比，将形态与图案从更广上下文孤立的抽象、紧凑裁切取景，对图形构图而非叙事内容的 deliberate、从容关注，从深黑到发光白的全程色调范围精细控制，以及将抽象与形态视为整个构图主体的自律、现代主义摄影清晰度。受影响于：Brett Weston、Edward Weston、现代主义黑白摄影传统、straight-photography 技法。

Refined social-media  photography: natural-light–driven editorial imagery emphasizing authenticity and presence, minimalist compositions that center the subject with confident simplicity, soft yet high-contrast lighting that enhances textures without heavy stylization, fashion-forward framing blending casual intimacy with magazine polish, relaxed body language conveying self-possession and modern sensuality, clean color palettes with warm organic hues, contemporary lifestyle aesthetic balanced between candid and posed moments, social-media–era visual language refined for high-end fashion contexts, by Zoë Ghertner, Cass Bird,
Refined social-media photography（精致社交媒体摄影）：以自然光驱动的编辑影像，强调真实性与在场，以自信简洁居中主体的极简构图，在不过度风格化下增强纹理的柔软却高对比灯光，融合随意亲密与杂志抛光的时尚前沿取景，传达自我掌控与现代性感的放松肢体语言，带温暖有机色调的干净配色，介于 candid 与摆拍间的当代生活方式美学，为高端时尚语境精炼的社交媒体时代视觉语言；by Zoë Ghertner、Cass Bird。

Halation Style: A softly radiant photographic technique built on a gentle glow that spreads outward from bright highlight areas, bleeding warmth into surrounding tone rather than staying sharply contained, a hazy, diffused luminosity that softens edges and dissolves crisp contrast, an unhurried, dreamlike quality where light itself seems to overflow its boundaries, delicate atmospheric bloom carried through subtle overexposed brightness, and a nostalgic, cinematic tenderness that feels romantic, hazy, and quietly luminous. Influenced by: halation film-emulsion technique, vintage cinematography lighting practice, glow-diffusion photographic method, soft-focus lens-flare technique.
Halation Style（光晕风格）：一种柔软发光的摄影技法，以从明亮高光区向外扩散、将温暖 bleed 入周围色调而非 sharp 受限的 gentle glow，软化边缘、dissolve crisp 对比的朦胧、漫射发光，光本身似溢出边界的从容、梦境质感，经微妙过曝亮度的精致氛围 bloom，以及浪漫、hazy、quietly 发光的怀旧、电影感温柔感。受影响于：光晕胶片乳剂技法、复古电影灯光实践、辉光漫射摄影方法、柔焦镜头光晕技法。

Ethereal Memorial Portrait Photography: A hauntingly luminous photographic style built on soft, diffused illumination that seems to emanate around the subject rather than fall directly upon it, an otherworldly glow separating the figure from its surroundings with gentle, dreamlike radiance, a hushed, elegiac stillness carried through unhurried, reverent framing, delicate soft-focus rendering that blurs the boundary between memory and presence, and a tender, wistful gravitas that treats the subject as simultaneously present and already lost. Influenced by: cinematic flashback-lighting technique, soft-focus memorial-portrait photography, halation-glow diffusion practice, elegiac cinematography tradition.
Ethereal Memorial Portrait Photography（空灵纪念肖像摄影）：一种 hauntingly 发光的摄影风格，以似环绕而非直落主体的柔软、漫射照明，以温柔、梦境般光辉将人物从环境分离的超凡 glow，通过从容、reverent 取景携带的轻声、挽歌式静谧，模糊记忆与在场边界的精致柔焦渲染，以及将主体同时读作在场与已逝的温柔、怅惘庄严感。受影响于：电影闪回灯光技法、柔焦纪念肖像摄影、光晕辉光漫射实践、挽歌式电影传统。

High-Angle Shot Photography: A dramatically elevated photographic technique built on a steep, downward vantage that foreshortens and compresses the subject from above, an altered sense of scale that renders the subject smaller and more vulnerable within the frame, a wide, encompassing view of surrounding context made possible by the elevated position, deliberate, considered framing that shifts psychological weight through perspective alone, and a distinctive, compositionally assertive intensity that treats camera position itself as the primary storytelling tool. Influenced by: elevated-perspective cinematography tradition, dramatic camera-angle technique, psychological framing practice, classic high-angle photographic method.
High-Angle Shot Photography（高角度摄影）：一种戏剧性升高的摄影技法，以从上方 foreshorten、compress 主体的陡峭向下视角，将主体渲染得更小、更脆弱的尺度改变感，升高位置成就的周围上下文 wide、encompassing 视野，仅通过透视 shift 心理重量的 deliberate、考究取景，以及将相机位置本身视为首要叙事工具的 distinctive、构图性 assertive 强度。受影响于：升高视角电影传统、戏剧性相机角度技法、心理取景实践、经典高角度摄影方法。

Translucency-Focused Photography: A delicately luminous photographic technique built on soft, diffused illumination that seems to pass through rather than bounce off the subject, gentle internal glow settling just beneath the outermost extent, a hazy, ethereal softness replacing sharp, hard-edged contrast, meticulous attention to how light bends and scatters through varying depths, and a quiet, dreamlike intensity that treats the passage of light itself as the compositional subject. Influenced by: translucency-focused studio-lighting technique, backlit portrait-photography practice, soft-diffusion glow method, macro light-transport photographic tradition.
Translucency-Focused Photography（透光聚焦摄影）：一种精致发光的摄影技法，以似穿过而非弹离主体的柔软、漫射照明，在最外表层下 settle 的温柔内部 glow，取代 sharp、硬边对比的 hazy、ethereal 柔软，对光如何弯曲、穿过不同深度散射的精细考究，以及将光本身 passage 视为构图主体的安静、梦境强度。受影响于：透光聚焦影棚灯光技法、逆光肖像实践、柔漫辉光方法、微距光传输摄影传统。

Matrix-Grading Photography: A cold, high-contrast photographic technique built on a dominant monochromatic-green color cast washing over the entire tonal range, stark, hard-edged directional lighting that carves dramatic separation between illuminated form and deep, engulfing darkness, a slightly desaturated, clinical coldness replacing natural photographic warmth entirely, meticulous crushed-shadow contrast that flattens midtones into stark extremes, and a sleek, dystopian cinematic intensity that feels simulated, sterile, and quietly unsettling. Influenced by: The Matrix cinematography tradition, monochromatic color-grading technique, dystopian film-grading practice, cyberpunk cinematic-photography method.
Matrix-Grading Photography（《黑客帝国》调色摄影）：一种寒冷、高对比的摄影技法，以 wash 过整个色调范围的 dominant 单色绿色调，将照明形态与深、吞噬黑暗 carving 戏剧分离的 stark、硬边方向光，完全取代自然摄影温暖的略去饱和、临床寒冷，将中间调 flatten 为 stark 极端的精细 crushed-shadow 对比，以及模拟、sterile、quietly unsettling 的利落、反乌托邦电影强度。受影响于：《黑客帝国》摄影传统、单色调色技法、反乌托邦电影调色实践、赛博朋克电影摄影方法。

Cosplay Photography: A polished, character-driven photographic style built on flattering studio or location lighting that renders costume detail with crisp, believable clarity, confident, dynamic posing calibrated to embody a character's personality and energy, meticulous attention to prop and costume texture rendered with genuine photographic conviction, a bright, evenly balanced exposure that keeps every detail legible, and a devoted, high-production convention-photography polish that feels vibrant, expressive, and unmistakably fan-crafted. Influenced by: contemporary convention-photography tradition, character-portrait lighting technique, costume-craft documentation practice, fan-community photographic method.
Cosplay Photography（角色扮演摄影）：一种抛光、角色驱动的摄影风格，以 rendering 服装细节 crisp、可信清晰度的 flattering 影棚或外景灯光，经校准体现角色个性与能量的自信、动态姿态，以真实摄影说服力渲染道具与服装纹理的精细考究，保持每个细节可读的明亮、均匀平衡曝光，以及 vibrant、富有表现力、毋庸置疑粉丝手作的 devoted、高制作漫展摄影抛光感。受影响于：当代漫展摄影传统、角色肖像灯光技法、服装工艺记录实践、粉丝社群摄影方法。

Outer Form Inner Imagery Interplay: A conceptual double-exposure photomontage style where the outer silhouette of a figure functions as a container or window, its surface seamlessly blended with an entirely separate inner scene, glimpsed through translucent overlaps and layered blending, the boundary between subject and embedded imagery dissolving into soft gradient transitions and selective sharp detail, creating a poetic visual metaphor where the figure's interior world is made literally visible. Influenced by: double-exposure photography, Dan Mountford, surrealist collage art, digital photo-manipulation.
Outer Form Inner Imagery Interplay（外形-内像互文/双重曝光）：一种概念性双重曝光照片蒙太奇风格，人物外轮廓作为容器或窗口，其表面与完全独立的内部场景无缝融合，透过半透明重叠与层叠混合瞥见，主体与嵌入图像间的边界 dissolve 为柔软渐变过渡与选择性锐利细节，创作将人物内在世界 literal 可见的诗歌视觉隐喻。受影响于：双重曝光摄影、Dan Mountford、超现实主义拼贴艺术、数字照片处理。

Studio Photography: A controlled, immaculately lit photographic style built on precise lighting that sculpt form with deliberate, repeatable exactness, a clean, seamless separation between subject and surrounding space achieved through careful lighting, crisp, evenly balanced exposure that eliminates unwanted ambient interference, confident, deliberate composition within a controlled,environment, and a polished, professional-grade clarity that feels composed, exacting, and fully intentional. Influenced by: commercial studio-lighting technique, professional portrait-photography tradition, controlled-environment photographic method.
Studio Photography（影棚摄影）：一种受控、immaculately 打光的摄影风格，以 deliberate、可重复精确的精准灯光雕塑形态，通过小心灯光实现主体与周围空间干净、无缝分离，消除不想要环境干扰的 crisp、均匀平衡曝光，受控环境中的自信、deliberate 构图，以及 composed、exacting、完全 intentional 的抛光、专业级清晰度。受影响于：商业影棚灯光技法、专业肖像摄影传统、受控环境摄影方法。

Imperfections-Driven Photography: A candid, photographic style built on visible textural flaws — grain, soft focus, uneven exposure, subtle asymmetry  composition carrying genuine,  visible VHS film grain, natural noise artifacts, motion blur presence, imperfect focus, raw unpolished framing, uneven lighting conditions, faint hairline scratches and dust marks scattered across the frame, Influenced by: old photo, documentary snapshot-photography tradition, imperfection-embracing wabi-sabi photographic practice, unretouched candid-capture technique, analog authenticity method.
Imperfections-Driven Photography（瑕疵驱动摄影）：一种 candid 摄影风格，以可见纹理瑕疵——颗粒、柔焦、不均曝光、微妙不对称构图携带真实、可见 VHS 胶片颗粒、自然噪点 artifacts、动态模糊存在、不完美对焦、raw 未抛光取景、不均灯光条件、散布画幅的细微划痕与尘斑。受影响于：老照片、纪实快照摄影传统、拥抱瑕疵的 wabi-sabi 摄影实践、未润色 candid 捕捉技法、模拟真实性方法。

High-Contrast Photography: A striking photography technique built on a bright, blown-out backdrop that turns the surrounding area nearly pure white, a strong rim of illumination tracing the edge of the subject while the front stays comparatively underexposed and rich in shadow detail, sharp, decisive focus concentrated tightly on the figure against a softly overexposed, low-detail surround, deliberate exposure contrast pushing the tonal range toward extremes rather than an even middle, and a bold, graphic, high-impact clarity that isolates form through light alone. Influenced by: high-contrast lighting practice, controlled exposure-contrast photographic tradition.
High-Contrast Photography（高对比摄影）：一种 striking 摄影技法，以将周围区域转为近纯白的明亮、blown-out 背景，强边缘 illumination tracing 主体边缘而正面相对欠曝、富阴影细节，集中于人物对抗柔软过曝、低细节环绕的 sharp、decisive 对焦，将色调范围推向极端而非均匀中段的 deliberate 曝光对比，以及仅通过光孤立形态的 bold、图形、高冲击清晰度。受影响于：高对比灯光实践、受控曝光对比摄影传统。

John Wick-Inspired Neon Photography: A stylish, high-contrast cinematic photographic technique built on saturated neon-colored gel lighting crossing the frame in sharp, deliberate bands, deep, engulfing shadow surrounding pockets of intense colored illumination, glossy reflective surfaces catching and bouncing colored light throughout the composition, dramatic silhouette-driven framing charged with restrained, cool tension, and a sleek, stylish nocturnal intensity that feels moody, deliberate, and unmistakably cinematic. Influenced by: contemporary action-thriller cinematography tradition, neon-gel lighting technique, stylized nightclub-noir photographic practice, high-contrast cinematic color-grading method.
John Wick-Inspired Neon Photography（《疾速追杀》启发霓虹摄影）：一种时髦、高对比电影摄影技法，以呈锐利、deliberate 带的饱和霓虹 gel 灯光横切画幅，包围强烈彩色照明光池的深、吞噬阴影，捕捉并弹跳彩色光贯穿构图的 glossy 反射面，承载克制、cool 张力的戏剧性剪影驱动取景，以及 moody、deliberate、毋庸置疑电影感的利落、时髦夜间强度。受影响于：当代动作惊悚摄影传统、霓虹 gel 灯光技法、风格化夜店 noir 摄影实践、高对比电影调色方法。

Wes Anderson Photography: A meticulously symmetrical photographic style built on precise, centered compositional balance where every element is deliberately arranged within a controlled geometric frame, flat, evenly diffused lighting that minimizes dramatic shadow in favor of clean, storybook clarity, a deadpan, static stillness carried through careful, unhurried framing, a restrained, deliberate formality where visual order itself becomes the mood, and a whimsical, meticulously curated precision that feels theatrical yet perfectly controlled. Influenced by: Wes Anderson cinematography tradition, symmetrical composition technique, deadpan visual-comedy framing practice, meticulous production-design photography.
Wes Anderson Photography（韦斯·安德森摄影）：一种精心对称的摄影风格，以每个元素 deliberate 安排于受控几何框架内的精准、居中构图平衡，最小化戏剧阴影、优先干净绘本清晰度的 flat、均匀漫射光，通过小心、从容取景携带的 deadpan、静态静谧，视觉秩序本身成为情绪的克制、deliberate 正式感，以及戏剧性却完美受控的异想、精心策展精度。受影响于：Wes Anderson 摄影传统、对称构图技法、deadpan 视觉喜剧取景实践、精细制作设计摄影。

Underwater Photography: A soft, diffused photographic technique built on light scattering and bending as it passes through a dense medium, a gentle, hazy loss of sharpness increasing with distance , a cool, muted tonal cast that deepens and shifts the further the subject recedes, delicate suspended particulate drifting through the frame catching stray beams of light, and a weightless, dreamlike stillness that feels immersive, hushed, and quietly otherworldly. Influenced by: underwater-photography technique, natural-light diffusion, marine documentary photography, ambient light-scattering.
Underwater Photography（水下摄影）：一种柔软、漫射的摄影技法，以光穿过密集介质时的散射与弯曲，随距离增加的温柔、hazy 锐度损失，随主体后退加深、shift 的 cool、muted 色调 cast，捕捉散落光束、drifting 的精致悬浮颗粒，以及沉浸、轻声、quietly 超凡的失重、梦境静谧感。受影响于：水下摄影技法、自然光漫射、海洋纪实摄影、环境光散射。

Mexico Color Grading: A photographic image pushed through an aggressive digital color grade built on crushed yellows, desaturated ochres, and dusty amber warmth, the midtones shifted decisively toward a sepia-bronze spectrum while the shadows sink into deep, muddy brown-blacks , a heavy tungsten-leaning white balance simmering over every texture. Influenced by: Steven Soderbergh's digital color grading on Traffic and Che, the bleach-bypass warmth of the Mexican narco-cinema palette,
Mexico Color Grading（墨西哥调色）：以 crushed 黄、去饱和赭石与 dusty 琥珀暖意推进的激进数字调色摄影图像，中间调 decisive 转向 sepia-bronze 谱而阴影 sink 入深、muddy 棕黑，每条纹理上 simmer 的重 tungsten 偏白平衡。受影响于：Steven Soderbergh 在《交通》与《切》上的数字调色、墨西哥毒枭电影调色的省略漂白暖意。

Design: A clean, purposeful graphic-illustration style built on confident, simplified shape construction that prioritizes function and clarity over decorative detail, flat or minimally gradated color application applied with intentional restraint, a balanced, structured compositional logic guided by clear visual hierarchy, deliberate, uncluttered spacing that lets each element read instantly, and a versatile, contemporary graphic-design clarity rooted in modern commercial and digital design convention. Influenced by: modern graphic-design tradition, minimalist design practice, contemporary commercial-design technique, digital-design composition method.
Design（设计·图形插画）：一种干净、purposeful 的图形插画风格，以优先功能与清晰度而非装饰细节的自信、简化形态构建，以 intentional 克制应用的平涂或极少渐变色彩，由清晰视觉层级引导的平衡、结构化构图逻辑，让每个元素即时可读的 deliberate、不杂乱间距，以及根植现代商业与数字设计惯例的 versatile、当代平面设计清晰度。受影响于：现代平面设计传统、极简设计实践、当代商业设计技法、数字设计构图方法。

Butcher Billy Style: A bold, graphic retro-pop illustration style built on flat, punchy color-block construction separated by crisp confident outline, a deliberate mash-up sensibility blending mid-century advertising polish with pop-culture iconography, sharp geometric simplification replacing painterly nuance entirely, confident, graphic silhouette design calibrated for instant recognizability, and a witty, nostalgic commercial-art punch that feels both retro and unmistakably contemporary. Influenced by: Butcher Billy, retro-pop graphic-design tradition, mid-century advertising illustration technique, pop-culture mash-up illustration practice.
Butcher Billy Style（Butcher Billy 风格）：一种 bold、图形复古波普插画风格，以 crisp 自信轮廓分隔的 flat、punchy 色块构建，融合中叶世纪广告抛光与流行文化符号的 deliberate mash-up 感，完全取代绘画性微妙的锐利几何简化，为瞬时可辨识校准的自信、图形剪影设计，以及既复古又毋庸置疑当代的 witty、怀旧商业艺术 punch。受影响于：Butcher Billy、复古波普平面设计传统、中叶世纪广告插画技法、流行文化 mash-up 插画实践。

Corporate Memphis: A flat, rounded illustration style built on simplified, tube-like limbs and gently curved geometric shapes replacing naturalistic anatomy entirely, uniform flat-color fill with minimal to no shading beyond soft contact shadow, a deliberately generic, approachable proportion language designed for broad, inoffensive relatability, clean, uncluttered composition prioritizing instant digital-platform legibility, and a friendly, corporate-tech sincerity that feels streamlined, accessible, and unmistakably contemporary. Influenced by: contemporary flat-illustration movement, tech-industry design-system practice, geometric character-illustration technique, digital-platform brand-illustration method.
Corporate Memphis（企业孟菲斯风格）：一种 flat、圆润的插画风格，以 tube-like 四肢与温柔曲线几何形状完全取代自然解剖，除柔和接触阴影外极少或无阴影的均匀平涂，为广泛、无冒犯 relatability 设计的刻意通用、易接近比例语言，优先瞬时数字平台可读性的干净、不杂乱构图，以及 streamlined、易接近、毋庸置疑当代的友好、企业科技真诚感。受影响于：当代平涂插画运动、科技行业设计系统实践、几何角色插画技法、数字平台品牌插画方法。

8-Bit Style: A blocky, pixel-constrained illustration style built on hard, discrete square units standing in for all line and shading, an extremely limited tonal palette-logic forcing every gradient into stepped, visible bands, deliberate geometric simplification that reduces anatomy to its most essential recognizable silhouette, a rigid, grid-locked precision replacing any curved or organic mark-making, and a nostalgic, technically constrained charm that celebrates the aesthetic discipline of early digital-display limitation. Influenced by: early video-game sprite-art tradition, Nintendo Entertainment System graphics technique, pixel-art illustration practice, retro arcade visual-design method.
8-Bit Style（8位风格）：一种 blocky、像素受限的插画风格，以取代所有线条与阴影的硬、离散方块单元，迫使每个渐变进入 stepped、可见带的极有限色调 palette 逻辑，将解剖简化至最本质可辨识剪影的 deliberate 几何简化，取代任何曲线或有机 mark-making 的 rigid、网格锁定精度，以及赞美早期数字显示限制美学纪律的怀旧、技术受限魅力。受影响于：早期电子游戏 sprite 艺术传统、任天堂娱乐系统图形技法、像素艺术插画实践、复古街机视觉设计方法。

Kintsugi-Inspired Digital Art: A contemplative repair-as-beauty illustration style built on deliberate fracture lines traced with luminous, precious-metal seams that transform breakage into ornamental focal points, a quiet philosophical tension between damage and restoration rendered with meticulous, reverent craftsmanship, smooth surrounding surfaces contrasted against the raised, textural presence of the mended seams, a hushed, contemplative stillness that treats imperfection as the compositional centerpiece rather than something hidden, and a refined, wabi-sabi sincerity that finds quiet grandeur in visible history. Influenced by: traditional kintsugi ceramic-repair technique, wabi-sabi aesthetic philosophy, Japanese lacquer-craft tradition, contemporary repair-art practice.
Kintsugi-Inspired Digital Art（金缮启发数字艺术）：一种沉思、以修复为美的插画风格，以 luminous、贵金属接缝 tracing 的 deliberate 裂痕线，将破损转为装饰性焦点，以 meticulous、reverent 工艺渲染的损坏与修复间安静哲学张力，与隆起、有纹理的修补接缝对比的平滑周围表面，将不完美视为构图中心而非隐藏的轻声、沉思静谧，以及于可见历史中寻得安静宏大的精炼、wabi-sabi 真诚感。受影响于：传统金缮陶瓷修复技法、wabi-sabi 美学哲学、日本漆艺传统、当代修复艺术实践。

Soviet Propaganda Poster: A bold, graphic illustration style built on stark, high-contrast flat color fields separated by confident heavy outline, a monumental, low-angle compositional heroism that dramatically elevates the central figure, dynamic diagonal energy conveying collective momentum and forward-driving purpose, simplified, sculptural anatomy rendered with poster-ready graphic clarity over naturalistic nuance, and a bold, rousing propagandistic grandeur built for instant ideological impact at a distance. Influenced by: Constructivist poster-design tradition, Alexander Rodchenko, socialist-realist illustration technique, agitprop graphic-design practice.
Soviet Propaganda Poster（苏联宣传海报）：一种 bold、图形插画风格，以 confident 重轮廓分隔的 stark、高对比平涂色场，戏剧性 elevate 中心人物的纪念碑式、低角度构图英雄感，传达集体 momentum 与向前驱动的 dynamic 对角能量，优先自然主义微妙、具海报级图形清晰度的简化、雕塑性解剖，以及为远距离瞬时意识形态冲击而生的 bold、rousing 宣传宏伟感。受影响于：构成主义海报设计传统、Alexander Rodchenko、社会主义现实主义插画技法、agitprop 平面设计实践。

Minecraft Style: A blocky, voxel-constrained illustration style built on hard-edged cubic geometry standing in for all organic and structural form, a deliberately low-resolution texture logic that reduces surface detail to flat, pixelated approximation, rigid right-angle construction replacing curved or naturalistic silhouette entirely, a charmingly crude, procedurally consistent simplicity carried through uniform block-unit scale, and a nostalgic, technically constrained charm rooted in early sandbox-game visual limitation. Influenced by: voxel-based game-design tradition, Markus Persson procedural-generation practice, low-poly/blocky 3D-rendering technique, sandbox-game visual-design method.
Minecraft Style（我的世界风格）：一种 blocky、voxel 受限的插画风格，以取代所有有机与结构形态的硬边立方几何，将表面细节 reduce 为 flat、像素化近似的 deliberate 低分辨率纹理逻辑，完全取代曲线或自然剪影的 rigid 直角构建，以均匀方块单元尺度携带的迷人粗陋、程序一致简化，以及根植早期沙盒游戏视觉限制的怀旧、技术受限魅力。受影响于：voxel 游戏设计传统、Markus Persson 程序生成实践、低多边形/blocky 3D 渲染技法、沙盒游戏视觉设计方法。

Funko Pop Style: A stylized, toy-figure illustration style built on an oversized, dominant head-to-body proportion ratio replacing naturalistic anatomy entirely, a glossy, uniformly smooth vinyl-like finish applied across every plane with no visible textural variation, minimal facial detail reduced to simplified dot-eyes and near-featureless expression, a rigid, static posing logic that mimics fixed collectible-figure stance, and a charmingly reductive, mass-collectible commercial polish that feels instantly recognizable and endearingly toy-like. Influenced by: Funko Pop! product-design tradition, vinyl-collectible figure aesthetic, stylized toy-proportion design practice, mass-market collectible-merchandise rendering method.
Funko Pop Style（Funko Pop 风格）：一种风格化、玩具人物插画风格，以完全取代自然解剖的超大、主导头身比例，无任何可见纹理变化的 glossy、均匀光滑 vinyl 般 finish，reduce 为简化点眼、近乎无特征表情的极少面部细节，模仿固定收藏人物站姿的 rigid、静态姿态逻辑，以及即时可辨识、endearingly 玩具感的迷人 reductive、mass-collectible 商业抛光感。受影响于：Funko Pop! 产品设计传统、vinyl 收藏人物美学、风格化玩具比例设计实践、大众市场收藏周边渲染方法。

Hyper-Stylized Illustration: A boldly exaggerated illustration style built on confident distortion of proportion that pushes form far beyond naturalistic reference, sharp, graphic linework holding dramatically simplified or elongated silhouette with total confidence, flat-to-gradient shading applied with deliberate, controlled boldness rather than subtlety, an assertive, larger-than-life visual rhythm carried through exaggerated posing and composition, and a striking, unapologetically stylized intensity that treats distortion itself as the core creative statement. Influenced by: contemporary stylized character-design tradition, exaggerated-proportion illustration technique, bold graphic-design-adjacent rendering practice, confident stylization-driven concept-art method.
Hyper-Stylized Illustration（超风格化插画）：一种 boldly 夸张的插画风格，以将形态推远超出自然参考的自信比例扭曲，以 total 自信持有戏剧性简化或拉长剪影的 sharp、图形线条，以 deliberate、受控 boldness 而非微妙应用的平涂到渐变阴影，通过夸张姿态与构图携带的 assertive、超越现实视觉节奏，以及将扭曲本身视为核心创作陈述的 striking、毫不歉疚风格化强度。受影响于：当代风格化角色设计传统、夸张比例插画技法、bold 平面设计邻接渲染实践、自信风格化驱动概念艺术方法。

Graphic Flat-Vector Travel Poster Illustration: A bold, hand-cut illustration style built on faceted, angular shape construction that reduces form into confident planar segments rather than smooth gradation, sharp textural crosshatching and scratchy directional mark-making overlaid across otherwise flat color-block regions, a poster-ready graphic clarity balanced against loose, painterly energy at the edges, dramatic sunlit contrast carving crisp separation between illuminated and shadowed planes, and a stylish, contemporary travel-illustration polish that feels equally editorial and hand-crafted. Influenced by: contemporary vector-travel-poster illustration, faceted geometric-painting technique, mid-century advertising illustration tradition, editorial flat-color rendering practice.
Graphic Flat-Vector Travel Poster Illustration（图形平涂矢量旅行海报插画）：一种 bold、手切插画风格，以将形态 reduce 为自信平面段而非平滑渐变的 faceted、棱角形态构建，在否则 flat 色块区域上叠加的锐利纹理交叉排线与 scratchy 方向性 mark-making，平衡边缘松散、绘画性能量的海报级图形清晰度，carving 照明与阴影平面 crisp 分离的戏剧性阳光对比，以及既编辑又手工的时髦、当代旅行插画抛光感。受影响于：当代矢量旅行海报插画、faceted 几何绘画技法、中叶世纪广告插画传统、编辑平涂渲染实践。

Ornamental Patterns Hyperrealism Portrait: A meticulously rendered portrait-illustration style built on dense, intricately repeating decorative motifs woven directly into the rendering of the figure itself, each interlocking element executed with obsessive photographic precision and convincing dimensional depth, dramatic raking light that catches every raised contour and recessed groove against otherwise softly modeled skin, a hushed, exacting patience carried through the sheer density of repeated detail contrasted with quiet portrait stillness, and a technically virtuosic intensity that treats ornamental complexity and human presence as equally devotional subjects. Influenced by: hyperrealist digital-painting technique, decorative-arts portrait-illustration tradition, meticulous pattern-rendering practice, ornamental craft-illustration method.
Ornamental Patterns Hyperrealism Portrait（繁复纹样超写实肖像）：一种精细渲染的肖像插画风格，以直接 woven 进人物本身渲染的密集、 intricately 重复装饰母题，每个 interlocking 元素以偏执照片精度与可信维度纵深执行，在否则柔软建模皮肤上捕捉每个隆起轮廓与凹陷 groove 的戏剧性 raking 光，与安静肖像静谧对比的纯粹细节密度的轻声、exacting 耐心，以及将装饰复杂性与人的存在视为同等 devotional 主题的技术 virtuoso 强度。受影响于：超写实数字绘画技法、装饰艺术肖像插画传统、精细图案渲染实践、装饰工艺插画方法。

Flat Illustration: A clean, graphic illustration style built on evenly bounded shapes filled with uniform color rather than blended tone, minimal to no shading beyond deliberate, flat contact shadow, confident, simplified linework that reduces form to its most essential silhouette, a crisp, uncluttered compositional clarity favoring instant legibility over dimensional depth, and a contemporary, streamlined graphic sensibility that feels bold, tidy, and effortlessly modern. Influenced by: contemporary flat-design illustration tradition, vector-based graphic technique, minimalist digital-illustration practice, modern editorial flat-color method.
Flat Illustration（平涂插画）：一种干净、图形插画风格，以均匀色彩填充的 evenly bounded 形状而非混合色调，除 deliberate、flat 接触阴影外极少或无阴影，将形态 reduce 至最本质剪影的自信、简化线条，优先瞬时可读性而非维度纵深的 crisp、不杂乱构图清晰度，以及 bold、tidy、毫不费力现代的当代、streamlined 图形感。受影响于：当代平涂设计插画传统、矢量图形技法、极简数字插画实践、现代编辑平涂方法。

Vector Art: A crisp, scalable illustration style built on clean, mathematically precise linework and evenly bounded color regions, smooth gradient or flat-fill logic replacing any textural or painterly noise entirely, confident geometric simplification of form favoring graphic clarity over naturalistic nuance, razor-sharp edges that hold their precision at any size, and a polished, contemporary graphic-design sleekness rooted in digital illustration practice. Influenced by: contemporary vector-illustration tradition, Adobe Illustrator technique, flat-design graphic practice, digital scalable-artwork method.
Vector Art（矢量艺术）：一种 crisp、可缩放插画风格，以干净、数学精度线条与 evenly bounded 色彩区域，完全取代任何纹理或绘画性噪点的平滑渐变或平涂逻辑，优先图形清晰度而非自然微妙、自信几何简化形态，在任何尺寸保持精度的 razor-sharp 边缘，以及根植数字插画实践的抛光、当代平面设计 sleekness。受影响于：当代矢量插画传统、Adobe Illustrator 技法、平涂平面设计实践、数字可缩放 artwork 方法。

Bauhaus: A functionalist geometric illustration style built on stark, primary geometric forms — circles, squares, triangles — arranged with rigorous compositional logic, flat, uniform color regions applied with deliberate, unornamented restraint, a rational, grid-driven structure replacing any decorative or naturalistic impulse, confident economy of form where every element serves clear structural purpose, and a disciplined, timeless modernist clarity rooted in functionalist design philosophy. Influenced by: Wassily Kandinsky, Bauhaus design-movement tradition, functionalist geometric-design technique, modernist constructivist practice.
Bauhaus（包豪斯）：一种功能主义几何插画风格，以 rigorous 构图逻辑安排的 stark、primary 几何形——圆、方、三角，以 deliberate、无装饰克制的 flat、均匀色彩区域，取代任何装饰或自然冲动的理性、网格驱动结构，每个元素服务清晰结构目的的自信形态经济，以及根植功能主义设计哲学的自律、永恒现代主义清晰度。受影响于：Wassily Kandinsky、包豪斯设计运动传统、功能主义几何设计技法、现代主义构成主义实践。

Poster Art: A bold, graphic illustration style built on confident, simplified composition designed to communicate instantly at a distance, flat or minimally gradated color application applied with deliberate visual punch, strong compositional hierarchy that guides the eye directly to a single focal statement, meticulous economy of detail favoring immediate readability over exhaustive rendering, and a striking, commercial-grade graphic impact rooted in traditional advertising and print-design practice. Influenced by: mid-century poster-design tradition, commercial advertising-illustration technique, graphic-design composition practice, print-poster illustration method.
Poster Art（海报艺术）：一种 bold、图形插画风格，以经设计、为远距离瞬时传达的自信、简化构图，以 deliberate 视觉 punch 应用的平涂或极少渐变色彩，直接引导眼睛至单一焦点陈述的强构图层级，优先瞬时可读性而非 exhaustive 渲染的精细细节经济，以及根植传统广告与印刷设计实践的 striking、商业级图形冲击。受影响于：中叶世纪海报设计传统、商业广告插画技法、平面设计构图实践、印刷海报插画方法。

Minimalist Line Art: A pared-down illustration style built on a single confident, continuous line tracing form with extreme economy, near-total absence of shading or fill beyond the line itself, deliberate restraint that suggests rather than fully describes structure, meticulous control over line weight as the sole carrier of expression and rhythm, and a quiet, contemplative elegance that treats empty space as equally important as the mark itself. Influenced by: Pablo Picasso's single-line drawings, contemporary minimalist illustration tradition, continuous-line drawing technique, modern editorial line-art practice.
Minimalist Line Art（极简线条艺术）：一种 pared-down 插画风格，以极端经济、single 自信连续线 tracing 形态，除线本身外近乎完全无阴影或填充，suggest 而非 fully 描述结构的 deliberate 克制，作为表达与节奏唯一载体的线重精细控制，以及将空空间视为与标记同等重要的安静、沉思优雅。受影响于：毕加索单线素描、当代极简插画传统、连续线绘画技法、现代编辑线条艺术实践。

Minimalism: A pared-back illustration style built on radical simplification of form into its most essential shapes, sparse, uncluttered composition that treats empty space as an active compositional element, minimal to no shading beyond deliberate, restrained contrast, confident economy of mark-making that resists any decorative excess, and a quiet, contemplative clarity that finds impact through restraint rather than density. Influenced by: minimalist art-movement tradition, contemporary reductive-illustration technique, geometric-abstraction practice, modernist economy-of-form method.
Minimalism（极简主义）：一种 pared-back 插画风格，以形态 radical 简化至最本质形状，将空空间视为主动构图元素的 sparse、不杂乱构图，除 deliberate、克制对比外极少或无阴影，resist 任何装饰 excess 的自信 mark-making 经济，以及通过克制而非密度寻得冲击的安静、沉思清晰度。受影响于：极简艺术运动传统、当代 reductive 插画技法、几何抽象实践、现代主义形态经济方法。

Gradients: A smooth, continuous-tone illustration style built on gradual color transition replacing any hard-edged shading or flat fill, seamless blending across every plane that eliminates visible mark-making entirely, a soft, technically controlled precision governing exactly how one tone dissolves into the next, minimal linework that lets the transition itself define form and depth, and a polished, contemporary digital sleekness that feels smooth, atmospheric, and effortlessly refined.
Gradients（渐变）：一种平滑、连续色调插画风格，以取代任何硬边阴影或平涂的逐渐色彩过渡，完全消除可见 mark-making 的每个平面无缝混合，治理一个色调如何 dissolve 入下一个的柔软、技术受控精度，让过渡本身定义形态与纵深的极少线条，以及平滑、氛围、毫不费力精炼的抛光、当代数字 sleekness。

Origami: A geometric, faceted illustration style built on crisp, angular folds constructing form entirely through flat planar segments rather than curved or blended transition, sharp crease lines defining every structural boundary with mathematical precision, minimal shading reduced to simple contrast between adjoining folded segments, a confident, economical construction logic where each fold serves clear structural purpose, and a quietly elegant, meditative craftsmanship rooted in the discipline of paper-folding tradition. Influenced by: traditional Japanese origami tradition, geometric paper-folding technique, low-poly faceted-illustration practice, minimalist structural-design method.
Origami（折纸）：一种几何、faceted 插画风格，以完全通过 flat 平面段而非曲线或混合过渡构建形态的 crisp、棱角折痕，以数学精度定义每个结构边界的锐利 crease 线，reduce 为相邻折叠段间简单对比的极少阴影，每个折叠服务清晰结构目的的自信、经济构建逻辑，以及根植折纸纪律的安静优雅、沉思工艺感。受影响于：传统日本折纸传统、几何折纸技法、低多边形 faceted 插画实践、极简结构设计方法。

3D Render: A dimensionally precise digital-illustration style built on smooth, volumetric modeling that reads as fully three-dimensional through simulated geometry and light rather than drawn line, meticulously calculated illumination with soft ambient occlusion settling naturally into every contour, polished specular response giving forms convincing physical depth and weight, confident sculptural construction replacing traditional linework entirely, and a technically precise, computer-generated intensity built on simulated physical light behavior. Influenced by: real-time 3D-rendering technique, CGI modeling practice, physically based rendering (PBR) method, contemporary animated-render pipeline.
3D Render（3D 渲染）：一种维度精确的数字插画风格，以通过模拟几何与光而非绘制线读作完全三维的平滑、体积建模，以自然 settle 入每个轮廓的柔软环境光遮蔽的 meticulous 计算照明，赋予形态可信物理纵深与重量的抛光高光响应，完全取代传统线条的自信雕塑性构建，以及基于模拟物理光行为的技术精确、计算机生成强度。受影响于：实时 3D 渲染技法、CGI 建模实践、基于物理渲染（PBR）方法、当代动画渲染管线。

Pixar  Animation: A richly rendered 3D-illustration style built on soft, warm subsurface-scattered lighting that gives skin a believable inner glow, meticulously smooth volumetric modeling balancing appealing, rounded proportion with convincing dimensional weight, gentle, cinematic three-point lighting establishing wholesome, inviting mood within every frame, confident ambient occlusion and soft shadow settling naturally into every contour, and a polished, heartwarming studio-grade animation intensity built for broad, emotionally resonant appeal. Influenced by: Pixar, contemporary 3D-animation rendering technique, subsurface-scattering character-design practice, cinematic three-point lighting method, mainstream feature-animation studio pipeline.
Pixar Animation（皮克斯动画）：一种丰富渲染的 3D 插画风格，以赋予皮肤可信内在 glow 的柔软、温暖次表面散射光，平衡讨喜、圆润比例与可信维度重量的 meticulous 平滑体积建模，在每个画幅建立 wholesome、inviting 情绪的温柔、电影感三点布光，自然 settle 入每个轮廓的自信环境光遮蔽与柔影，以及为广泛、情感共鸣吸引力而生的抛光、暖心工作室级动画强度。受影响于：皮克斯、当代 3D 动画渲染技法、次表面散射角色设计实践、电影感三点布光方法、主流院线动画工作室管线。

Cinematic Final Fantasy 3D Render: A richly cinematic 3D-illustration style built on meticulously detailed volumetric modeling rendered with near-photographic dimensional conviction, dramatic film-grade lighting establishing epic, emotionally charged mood within every frame, soft subsurface-scattered rendering giving skin a convincing, luminous depth, confident ambient occlusion and reflective response settling naturally into every contour, and a polished, blockbuster-grade rendering intensity built for sweeping, story-driven visual spectacle. Influenced by: Square Enix cinematic-rendering direction, real-time 3D-animation technique, subsurface-scattering character-design practice, cinematic three-point lighting method.
Cinematic Final Fantasy 3D Render（电影感最终幻想 3D 渲染）：一种丰富电影感的 3D 插画风格，以近照片维度说服力的 meticulous 细节体积建模，在每个画幅建立史诗、情感满载情绪的戏剧性电影级灯光，赋予皮肤可信、发光纵深的柔软次表面散射渲染，自然 settle 入每个轮廓的自信环境光遮蔽与反射响应，以及为 sweeping、故事驱动视觉 spectacle 而生的抛光、大片级渲染强度。受影响于：Square Enix 电影感渲染方向、实时 3D 动画技法、次表面散射角色设计实践、电影感三点布光方法。

Marble Sculpture: A dimensionally commanding sculptural form built on convincing volumetric mass shaped through confident tonal modeling rather than linear description, dramatic raking light that reveals every carved contour and recessed hollow with sculptural clarity, a translucent depth beneath the outermost extent that softens and catches illumination with quiet luminosity, a monumental stillness carried through deliberate, unhurried mass and proportion, and a timeless, classical gravitas that treats carved form itself as the entire subject. Influenced by: Michelangelo, Antonio Canova, classical figurative-sculpture tradition, academic tonal-modeling technique.
Marble Sculpture（大理石雕塑）：一种维度 command 的雕塑形态，以通过自信色调建模而非线性描述塑造的可信体积质量，揭示每个雕刻轮廓与凹陷 hollow 的戏剧性 raking 光，在最外表层下 soften、catch 照明的半透明纵深，通过 deliberate、从容质量与比例的纪念碑式静谧，以及将雕刻形态本身视为整个主题的永恒、古典庄严感。受影响于：米开朗基罗、Antonio Canova、古典具象雕塑传统、学院派色调建模技法。

Playful Retro Pop: A bold, cheerful graphic illustration style built on flat, confidently bounded color-block construction separated by crisp, uniform outline, a bright, punchy simplicity that favors instant graphic charm over painterly nuance, exaggerated, bouncy proportion carrying lighthearted, nostalgic energy, a mid-century advertising-adjacent cheerfulness applied with contemporary graphic polish, and a fun, unmistakably retro-pop vibrancy that feels playful, confident, and effortlessly charming. Influenced by: mid-century advertising-illustration tradition, retro-pop graphic-design technique, flat-color commercial-illustration practice, contemporary retro-revival illustration method.
Playful Retro Pop（俏皮复古波普）：一种 bold、欢快的图形插画风格，以 crisp、均匀轮廓分隔的 flat、自信 bounded 色块构建，优先瞬时图形魅力而非绘画微妙的明亮、punchy 简化，携带轻快、怀旧能量的夸张、弹跳比例，以当代图形抛光应用的类中叶世纪广告 cheerfulness，以及俏皮、自信、毫不费力迷人的有趣、毋庸置疑复古波普活力。受影响于：中叶世纪广告插画传统、复古波普平面设计技法、平涂商业插画实践、当代复古复兴插画方法。

Low Poly: A geometrically reductive illustration style built on flat-shaded polygonal facets that construct form through angular simplification rather than smooth curvature, crisp hard-edged transitions between adjoining segments standing in for gradual shading, a deliberate faceted minimalism that abstracts detail into confident geometric approximation, a clean, mathematically precise rhythm carried through the visible triangulated structure, and a stylized, modern-craft elegance that celebrates simplified form as its own aesthetic statement. Influenced by: low-poly 3D-modeling technique, geometric abstraction tradition, procedural digital-art practice, minimalist polygonal design method.
Low Poly（低多边形）：一种几何 reductive 插画风格，以通过棱角简化而非平滑曲率构建形态的 flat-shaded 多边形 facet，取代逐渐阴影的相邻段间 crisp 硬边过渡，将细节 abstract 为自信几何近似的 deliberate faceted 极简，通过可见三角化结构携带的干净、数学精度节奏，以及将简化形态本身作为美学陈述的 styleized、现代工艺优雅。受影响于：低多边形 3D 建模技法、几何抽象传统、程序化数字艺术实践、极简多边形设计方法。

LEGO Minifigure: A blocky, modular figure style built on cylindrical joint construction and simplified, interlocking geometric limbs standing in for naturalistic anatomy, a glossy, uniformly smooth plastic-like finish applied consistently across every segment, a distinctive rounded cylindrical head with minimal, printed-style facial simplification, confident, clean separation between individually distinguishable component pieces, and a cheerful, systematized toy-design precision rooted in decades of modular construction-set tradition. Influenced by: LEGO Group product-design tradition, modular construction-toy aesthetic, plastic-injection product-visualization practice, procedural toy-figure rendering method.
LEGO Minifigure（乐高小人仔）：一种 blocky、模块化人物风格，以圆柱关节构建与取代自然解剖的简化、interlocking 几何四肢，一致应用于每个段的 glossy、均匀光滑 plastic 般 finish，带极简、printed 式面部简化的 distinctive 圆圆柱头，个体可辨组件间的自信、干净分离，以及根植数十年模块化构建套装传统的欢快、systematized 玩具设计精度。受影响于：乐高集团产品设计传统、模块化构建玩具美学、塑料注塑产品可视化实践、程序化玩具人物渲染方法。

LEGO Set Style: A blocky, modular illustration style built on cylindrical stud-and-brick construction logic standing in for all organic and structural form, glossy, uniformly smooth plastic-like surface finish applied consistently across every element, a deliberately simplified, interlocking geometric silhouette replacing naturalistic anatomy or structure, clean, confident graphic separation between individually distinguishable component pieces, and a cheerful, systematized toy-design precision rooted in decades of modular construction-set tradition. Influenced by: LEGO Group product-design tradition, modular construction-toy aesthetic, plastic-injection product-visualization practice, procedural brick-based 3D-rendering method.
LEGO Set Style（乐高套装风格）：一种 blocky、模块化插画风格，以取代所有有机与结构形态的圆柱 stud-and-brick 构建逻辑，一致应用于每个元素的 glossy、均匀光滑 plastic 般表面 finish，取代自然解剖或结构的 deliberate 简化、interlocking 几何剪影，个体可辨组件间的干净、自信图形分离，以及根植数十年模块化构建套装传统的欢快、systematized 玩具设计精度。受影响于：乐高集团产品设计传统、模块化构建玩具美学、塑料注塑产品可视化实践、程序化砖基 3D 渲染方法。

Paper Cutout: A layered, tactile illustration style built on flat, distinctly bounded shapes stacked with slight offset to suggest physical depth, crisp, clean-edged silhouette construction replacing any drawn linework, subtle drop-shadow separation between overlapping layers giving the composition a handcrafted dimensionality, a confident, graphic simplicity that favors bold shape over rendered detail, and a charming, tangible craft-quality that feels playful, dimensional, and unmistakably paper-built. Influenced by: paper-cutout collage tradition, stop-motion cutout-animation technique, layered-paper illustration practice, handcrafted collage-art method.
Paper Cutout（剪纸）：一种层叠、tactile 插画风格，以带轻微偏移堆叠、suggest 物理纵深的 flat、distinctly bounded 形状，取代任何绘制线条的 crisp、干净边剪影构建，重叠层间微妙投影分离赋予构图手工维度，优先 bold 形状而非渲染细节的自信、图形简化，以及俏皮、dimensional、毋庸置疑纸作的迷人、有形工艺品质。受影响于：剪纸拼贴传统、定格剪纸动画技法、层叠纸插画实践、手工拼贴艺术方法。

Collage: A layered, tactile illustration style built on the deliberate assembly of disparate fragments arranged into a single unified composition, torn or cleanly cut edges coexisting to create unpredictable, overlapping juxtaposition, a confident compositional looseness that embraces visible seams and assembly rather than hiding them, mixed tonal registers colliding across each fragment for expressive, unexpected contrast, and a raw, handcrafted intensity that treats the act of piecing together itself as the compositional statement. Influenced by: Hannah Höch, Kurt Schwitters, collage-art tradition, mixed-media assemblage practice.
Collage（拼贴）：一种层叠、tactile 插画风格，以 deliberate 组装 disparate 碎片为单一统一构图，撕裂或干净裁切边缘共存以创作不可预测、重叠并置，拥抱可见接缝与组装而非隐藏的自信构图松散，跨每个碎片碰撞以获表现性、意外对比的混合色调，以及将拼合行为本身视为构图陈述的 raw、手工强度。受影响于：Hannah Höch、Kurt Schwitters、拼贴艺术传统、混合媒介组装实践。

Claymation: A charmingly tactile stop-motion illustration style built on soft, rounded forms carrying visible handcrafted imperfection, gentle fingerprint-like dents and subtle irregularity standing in for smooth digital finish, warm, even lighting that gives every contour a cozy, dimensional roundness, a slightly rigid, hand-manipulated quality to posing that carries endearing physical charm, and a nostalgic, cheerfully handmade warmth rooted in decades of stop-motion animation craft. Influenced by: Aardman Animations, stop-motion claymation technique, handcrafted figure-animation practice, British character-animation tradition.
Claymation（黏土动画）：一种迷人 tactile 定格插画风格，以携带可见手工不完美的柔软、圆润形态，取代平滑数字 finish 的温柔指纹状凹痕与微妙不规则，赋予每个轮廓 cozy、维度圆润的温暖、均匀灯光，携带 endearing 物理魅力的姿态略 rigid、手操作特质，以及根植数十年定格动画工艺的怀旧、欢快手工温暖。受影响于：Aardman Animations、定格黏土动画技法、手工人物动画实践、英国角色动画传统。

Mixed Media: A layered, tactile illustration style built on the deliberate collision of disparate techniques — loose ink linework, painterly washes, and collaged textural fragments — coexisting within a single composition, unpredictable textural bleed and torn-edge layering that keeps the whole feeling handmade and accumulated rather than uniform, confident compositional looseness that embraces visible process over polished finish, a rich tactile depth built through overlapping transparency and material contrast, and an expressive, collage-driven intensity that feels spontaneous, layered, and richly textured. Influenced by: mixed-media collage art, Carne Griffiths, ink-and-collage illustration technique, contemporary fine-art assemblage.
Mixed Media（混合媒介）：一种层叠、tactile 插画风格，以 disparate 技法的 deliberate 碰撞——松散墨线、绘画性水洗与拼贴纹理碎片——共存于单一构图，保持整体手工、accumulated 而非 uniform 的不可预测纹理 bleed 与撕裂边缘层叠，优先抛光完成度而非可见过程的自信构图松散，通过重叠透明与材质对比构建的丰富 tactile 纵深，以及自发、层叠、richly textured 的表现性、拼贴驱动强度。受影响于：混合媒介拼贴艺术、Carne Griffiths、墨线与拼贴插画技法、当代 fine-art 组装。

Wallace and Gromit Style: A charmingly tactile stop-motion illustration style built on soft, rounded forms carrying visible handcrafted imperfection, gentle fingerprint-like dents and subtle irregularity standing in for smooth digital finish, warm, even lighting that gives every contour a cozy, dimensional roundness, exaggerated expressive proportions carrying subtle asymmetry and warm British whimsy, a hand-built, slightly wobbly charm that celebrates its own physical process, and a nostalgic, cheerfully eccentric handmade warmth. Influenced by: Aardman Animations, Nick Park, stop-motion claymation technique, British character-animation tradition.
Wallace and Gromit Style（超级无敌掌门狗风格）：一种迷人 tactile 定格插画风格，以携带可见手工不完美的柔软、圆润形态，取代平滑数字 finish 的温柔指纹状凹痕与微妙不规则，赋予每个轮廓 cozy、维度圆润的温暖、均匀灯光，携带 subtle 不对称与温暖英国 whimsy 的夸张表现性比例，庆祝自身物理过程的手作、略 wobbly 魅力，以及怀旧、欢快 eccentric 手工温暖。受影响于：Aardman Animations、Nick Park、定格黏土动画技法、英国角色动画传统。

Muppets Style: A charming, handcrafted puppet-illustration style built on soft, felted texture standing in for skin and fur alike, exaggerated, rounded proportions with oversized expressive features driving comedic readability, gentle, even stage-adjacent lighting that keeps the whole scene warmly lit and theatrical, a slightly rigid, hand-operated quality to posing that carries endearing physical charm, and a wholesome, joyfully theatrical warmth rooted in decades of beloved puppet-variety tradition. Influenced by: Jim Henson, Muppet Workshop puppet-fabrication tradition, television-variety-show staging practice, hand-puppet character-design method.
Muppets Style（布偶风格）：一种迷人、手工布偶插画风格，以取代皮肤与皮毛的柔软、felted 纹理，驱动喜剧可读性的夸张、圆润比例与超大表现性特征，保持整个场景温暖打光与戏剧性的温柔、均匀 stage 邻接灯光，携带 endearing 物理魅力的姿态略 rigid、手操作特质，以及根植数十年 beloved 布偶综艺传统的 wholesome、joyfully 戏剧温暖。受影响于：Jim Henson、Muppet Workshop 布偶制作传统、电视综艺节目 staging 实践、手偶角色设计方法。

Zelda Wind Waker Style: A vibrant, toon-shaded illustration style built on crisp, hard-edged cel shading with minimal tonal gradation, confident, thick outline holding clean, geometric silhouette design, warm painterly sky and terrain rendered with soft matte-painted texture, expressive, rounded character proportions carrying whimsical charm, and a bright, adventurous storybook vibrancy that feels playful, timeless, and quietly heroic.Influenced by: Nintendo character-art direction, cel-shaded toon-rendering technique, painterly matte-background practice, whimsical adventure-game illustration method.
Zelda Wind Waker Style（塞尔达风之杖风格）：一种 vibrant、toon-shaded 插画风格，以极少色调渐变的 crisp、硬边赛璐珞阴影，固定干净、几何剪影设计的自信、厚轮廓，以柔软哑光绘制纹理渲染的温暖绘画性天空与地形，携带异想魅力的表现性、圆润角色比例，以及明亮、冒险的绘本活力，感到俏皮、永恒、quietly 英雄。受影响于：任天堂角色艺术指导、赛璐珞 toon 渲染技法、绘画性哑光背景实践、异想冒险游戏插画方法。

PS1 Graphics: A low-poly, texture-warped 3D-illustration style built on blocky, vertex-snapped geometry that jitters slightly with characteristic early-3D imprecision, low-resolution textures stretched and distorted across simplified polygonal surfaces, harsh, unfiltered pixelated edges replacing any smooth anti-aliasing, a flat, limited lighting model with minimal gradation or dynamic shadow, and a nostalgic, technically constrained charm rooted in early console-era 3D rendering limitation. Influenced by: mid-1990s PlayStation 3D-rendering technique, early polygonal-game aesthetic, retro survival-horror visual tradition, low-fidelity vertex-jitter rendering method.
PS1 Graphics（PS1 图形）：一种低多边形、纹理扭曲的 3D 插画风格，以带特征性早期 3D 不精确、略 jitter 的 blocky、vertex-snapped 几何，拉伸、扭曲跨简化多边形表面的低分辨率纹理，取代任何平滑抗锯齿的 harsh、未过滤像素化边缘，极少渐变或动态阴影的 flat、有限灯光模型，以及根植早期主机时代 3D 渲染限制的怀旧、技术受限魅力。受影响于：1990 年代中期 PlayStation 3D 渲染技法、早期多边形游戏美学、复古生存恐怖视觉传统、低保真顶点 jitter 渲染方法。

Woodcut: A bold, high-contrast printmaking style built on confident, gouged linework that constructs form through carved negative space rather than drawn detail, stark black-and-white division with minimal transitional tone, rough, deliberate edge-texture inherent to the carving process, a rhythmic, hand-pressed graphic clarity favoring bold shape over subtle nuance, and a rustic, timeworn print-craft gravitas rooted in centuries of relief-printing tradition. Influenced by: Albrecht Dürer, Käthe Kollwitz, woodblock relief-printing technique, expressionist print tradition.
Woodcut（木刻）：一种 bold、高对比版画风格，以通过 carved 负空间而非绘制细节构建形态的 confident、gouged 线条，极少过渡色调的 stark 黑白分割，雕刻过程固有的 rough、deliberate 边缘纹理，优先 bold 形状而非微妙微妙的 rhythmic、手压图形清晰度，以及根植数世纪浮雕版画传统的 rustic、饱经岁月版画工艺庄严感。受影响于：Albrecht Dürer、Käthe Kollwitz、木版浮雕印刷技法、表现主义版画传统。

Contemporary Lithography: A refined printmaking-inspired illustration style built on flat, confidently limited color layers reminiscent of hand-pulled stone-plate printing, subtle grain and texture mimicking the imperfect registration of traditional lithographic process, clean bold linework holding each color plane with graphic precision, restrained tonal shading achieved through crosshatched or stippled print texture rather than smooth gradients, and a nostalgic yet contemporary print-craft polish that feels tactile, considered, and editorial. Influenced by: Toulouse-Lautrec, modern lithographic-printmaking technique, screenprint illustration practice, editorial print-design method.
Contemporary Lithography（当代石版画）：一种精炼、受版画启发的插画风格，以 reminiscent 手拉石版印刷的 flat、自信有限色彩层，mimic 传统石版过程不完美套版的微妙颗粒与纹理，以图形精度固定每个色彩平面的干净 bold 线条，通过交叉排线或点刻印刷纹理而非平滑渐变实现的克制色调阴影，以及 tactile、考究、编辑性的怀旧却当代版画抛光感。受影响于：Toulouse-Lautrec、现代石版印刷技法、丝网印插画实践、编辑印刷设计方法。

Variable-Size Ben-Day Dots Illustration: A bold pop-art printing style built on dot patterning that varies deliberately in scale across the composition, larger dots concentrated where tone deepens and smaller dots thinning out toward lighter passages, constructing gradation entirely through dot-size modulation rather than blended shading, crisp confident outline holding each region with graphic precision, a mechanical, offset-print authenticity carried through deliberate registration texture, and a bold, retro commercial-print energy that feels instantly graphic and unmistakably mid-century. Influenced by: Roy Lichtenstein, variable Ben-Day dot printing technique, silver-age comic printing process, pop-art illustration tradition.
Variable-Size Ben-Day Dots Illustration（变尺寸本戴点插画）：一种 bold 波普艺术印刷风格，以全构图 deliberate 变化尺度的点图案，色调加深处集中大点、浅处 thinning 小点，完全通过点尺寸调制而非混合阴影构建渐变，以图形精度固定每个区域的 crisp 自信轮廓，通过 deliberate 套版纹理携带的机械、胶印真实性，以及即时图形、毋庸置疑中叶世纪的 bold、复古商业印刷能量。受影响于：Roy Lichtenstein、变尺寸本戴点印刷技法、白银时代漫画印刷过程、波普艺术插画传统。

Contemporary Ukiyo-E: A contemporary reinterpretation of traditional woodblock-print illustration built on flat, confidently bounded shape divisions layered with a cleaner, more graphic digital precision, fluid calligraphic linework retaining the rhythmic economy of the original medium while shedding its period-specific motifs, restrained tonal layering achieved through overlapping flat planes rather than gradation, a poised, contemplative stillness carried through balanced asymmetrical composition, and a refined print-craft elegance updated with a crisp, present-day graphic sensibility. Influenced by: Katsushika Hokusai, contemporary woodblock-inspired digital illustration, modern Japanese graphic design, Edo-period print technique reimagined.
Contemporary Ukiyo-E（当代浮世绘）：一种传统木刻版画插画的当代重释，以 layered、更干净、更图形数字精度的 flat、自信 bounded 形状分割，保留原媒介 rhythmic 经济却 shed 其时代特定母题的流体书法性线条，通过重叠 flat 平面而非渐变实现的克制色调层叠，通过平衡非对称构图携带的 poised、沉思静谧，以及以 crisp、当下图形感更新的精炼版画工艺优雅。受影响于：葛饰北斋、受木刻启发的当代数字插画、现代日本平面设计、江户时期版画技法 reimagined。

Mosaic Art: A tessellated illustration style built on countless discrete fragments assembled into a unified composition, each individual piece holding a single flat tone while collectively constructing gradual tonal transition through density and arrangement, confident geometric or organic fragment shapes fitted with deliberate, patient precision, a rhythmic, granular texture replacing any smooth or blended rendering entirely, and a timeless, meticulous craft intensity rooted in centuries of tessellated decorative tradition. Influenced by: Byzantine mosaic tradition, tessellation-based decorative technique, Antoni Gaudí trencadís practice, classical tile-assembly craft method.
Mosaic Art（马赛克艺术）：一种 tessellated 插画风格，以 countless 离散碎片组装为统一构图，每个独立片持单一 flat 色调、而通过密度与排列集体构建逐渐色调过渡，以 deliberate、耐心精度 fitting 的自信几何或有机碎片形状，完全取代任何平滑或混合渲染的 rhythmic、颗粒纹理，以及根植数世纪 tessellated 装饰传统的永恒、meticulous 工艺强度。受影响于：拜占庭马赛克传统、tessellation 装饰技法、Antoni Gaudí trencadís 实践、古典瓷砖组装工艺方法。

Stained Glass Flat Art: decorative graphic aesthetics characterized by bold enclosed shapes, segmented composition, strong contour structures, ornamental patterning, and luminous mosaic-inspired design principles. The style builds imagery through interconnected flat regions separated by deliberate leading-like divisions, emphasizing symbolic clarity, visual rhythm, and harmonious decorative balance rather than volumetric realism. Rather than relying on shading or painterly texture, it communicates form through shape relationships, stylized silhouettes, and carefully arranged visual geometry, creating imagery that feels iconic, radiant, and timeless. The resulting aesthetic blends medieval craftsmanship, sacred decorative traditions, and modern graphic simplification into richly symbolic compositions with striking visual presence, reminiscent of Louis Comfort Tiffany and Alphonse Mucha, inspired by Chartres Cathedral stained glass and Tiffany Studios glass panels.
Stained Glass Flat Art（彩绘玻璃平涂艺术）：以 bold enclosed 形状、segmented 构图、强轮廓结构、装饰图案与发光马赛克启发设计原则为特征的装饰图形美学。该风格通过 deliberate leading 式分隔的 interconnected flat 区域构建图像，强调符号清晰度、视觉节奏与和谐装饰平衡而非体积写实；不依赖阴影或绘画性纹理，而通过形状关系、风格化剪影与精心安排视觉几何传达形态，创作 iconic、radiant、永恒的 image。结果美学将中世纪工艺、神圣装饰传统与现代图形简化融合为具 striking 视觉存在的丰富符号构图；令人联想到 Louis Comfort Tiffany 与 Alphonse Mucha，灵感来自沙特尔大教堂彩绘玻璃与 Tiffany Studios 玻璃板。

Isometric 3D Graphic: A compact, dimensionally clever illustration style built on a fixed isometric projection that renders three-dimensional form without true perspective convergence, clean, confidently simplified geometry reduced to its most essential recognizable shapes, flat-to-subtly-gradated shading applied consistently across matching angular faces to reinforce dimensional clarity, a tidy, modular compositional logic designed for small-scale, instantly legible readability, and a polished, contemporary UI-adjacent graphic precision built for consistent iconographic systems. Influenced by: isometric game-art tradition, modern UI/UX iconography practice, flat-design illustration technique, procedural icon-set design method.
Isometric 3D Graphic（等距 3D 图形）：一种 compact、维度巧妙插画风格，以无真实透视收敛的 fixed 等距投影渲染三维形态，reduce 至最本质可辨识形状的干净、自信简化几何，一致应用于匹配棱角面以强化维度清晰度的平涂到微妙渐变阴影，为小尺度、瞬时可读设计的 tidy、模块化构图逻辑，以及为一致图标系统而生的抛光、当代 UI 邻接图形精度。受影响于：等距游戏艺术传统、现代 UI/UX 图标实践、平涂插画技法、程序化图标集设计方法。

Quilling: A delicately dimensional illustration style built on tightly coiled and shaped strips assembled into intricate, raised compositions, confident looping and scrolling forms constructing pattern through repeated coiled units rather than drawn line, a rhythmic, handcrafted precision carried through the deliberate arrangement of each individual coil, subtle raised depth and shadow where coiled edges catch light against the recessed ground, and a delicate, meticulous craft intensity rooted in centuries of paper-coiling tradition. Influenced by: traditional paper-quilling craft, filigree decorative-art technique, handcrafted coil-assembly practice, ornamental paper-art method.
Quilling（衍纸）：一种精致维度插画风格，以 tightly coiled、shaped 纸条组装为 intricate、raised 构图，通过重复 coiled 单元而非绘制线构建图案的自信 looping、scrolling 形态，通过每个独立 coil 的 deliberate 安排携带的 rhythmic、手工精度，coiled 边缘 catch 光对抗 recessed ground 的微妙 raised 纵深与阴影，以及根植数世纪纸卷传统的精致、meticulous 工艺强度。受影响于：传统纸卷工艺、filigree 装饰艺术技法、手工 coil 组装实践、装饰性纸艺方法。

Lowbrow Style: A darkly whimsical illustration style built on cartoonish exaggeration mixed with unsettling, surreal juxtaposition, confident smooth rendering that gives every form a glossy, pop-adjacent polish, bold caricatured proportion pushed toward grotesque or absurd extremes, a rebellious, subversive humor woven through otherwise technically polished composition, and a bold, countercultural intensity that feels irreverent, playful, and quietly unsettling. Influenced by: Robert Williams, Mark Ryden, lowbrow-art movement tradition, pop-surrealist illustration practice.
Lowbrow Style（低眉艺术风格）：一种黑暗异想插画风格，以卡通化夸张混合 unsettling、超现实并置，赋予每个形态 glossy、pop 邻接抛光的自信平滑渲染，推向 grotesque 或 absurd 极端的 bold 漫画比例，穿入否则技术抛光构图的叛逆、subversive 幽默，以及 irreverent、俏皮、quietly unsettling 的 bold、反文化强度。受影响于：Robert Williams、Mark Ryden、低眉艺术运动传统、pop-surrealist 插画实践。

Fortnite Style: A glossy, saturated 3D-cartoon illustration style built on smooth, stylized proportion with exaggerated, action-figure-adjacent silhouette, bright, evenly saturated cel-adjacent shading that keeps every contour crisp and readable, confident volumetric rendering balancing cartoon simplicity with dimensional polish, dynamic, energetic posing charged with playful competitive flair, and a vibrant, high-production game-art vibrancy built for instant character appeal and broad accessibility. Influenced by: Epic Games character-art direction, stylized 3D-rendering technique, competitive-game key-art practice, cel-adjacent cartoon-render method.
Fortnite Style（堡垒之夜风格）：一种 glossy、饱和的 3D 卡通插画风格，以带夸张、action-figure 邻接剪影的平滑、风格化比例，保持每个轮廓 crisp、可读的明亮、均匀饱和赛璐珞邻接阴影，平衡卡通简化与维度抛光的自信体积渲染，携带俏皮竞争 flair 的动态、充满能量姿态，以及为瞬时角色吸引力与广泛可及性的 vibrant、高制作游戏艺术活力。受影响于：Epic Games 角色艺术指导、风格化 3D 渲染技法、竞技游戏主视觉实践、赛璐珞邻接卡通渲染方法。

Coraline Stop-Motion Style: A meticulously crafted stop-motion illustration style built on precise, doll-like proportion carrying an uncanny, porcelain-smooth finish, subtle handcrafted imperfection visible in gentle asymmetry rather than digital uniformity, moody, controlled lighting establishing a quietly unsettling, dreamlike atmosphere, meticulous attention to fine detail rendered with obsessive miniature-scale patience, and a whimsical yet faintly eerie handcrafted intensity that feels intimate, meticulous, and gently uncanny. Influenced by: Henry Selick, LAIKA Studios stop-motion tradition, meticulous puppet-fabrication technique, gothic-whimsical animation practice.
Coraline Stop-Motion Style（鬼妈妈定格动画风格）：一种 meticulously crafted 定格插画风格，以携带 uncanny、porcelain-smooth finish 的精准、doll 般比例，于温柔不对称而非数字均匀中可见的微妙手工不完美，建立 quietly unsettling、梦境氛围的 moody、受控灯光，以偏执 miniature 尺度耐心渲染的精细细节，以及 intimate、meticulous、gently uncanny 的异想却 faintly eerie 手工强度。受影响于：Henry Selick、LAIKA Studios 定格传统、meticulous 布偶制作技法、哥特异想动画实践。

Kurzgesagt Style: A clean, flat vector-illustration style built on confident geometric simplification of every form into smooth, rounded shapes, minimal shading limited to subtle gradient or soft contact shadow, a friendly, approachable clarity that favors instant conceptual readability over naturalistic rendering, meticulous compositional organization guiding the eye through dense informational content with playful ease, and a bright, whimsical educational charm that feels scientific, cheerful, and effortlessly digestible. Influenced by: Kurzgesagt studio house style, flat-vector explainer-illustration tradition, minimalist infographic-design technique, contemporary educational-animation practice.
Kurzgesagt Style（Kurzgesagt 风格）：一种干净、flat 矢量插画风格，以每个形态 smooth、圆润形状的自信几何简化，限制为微妙渐变或柔和接触阴影的极少阴影，优先瞬时概念可读性而非自然渲染的友好、易接近清晰度，以俏皮轻松引导眼睛穿过密集信息内容的 meticulous 构图组织，以及科学、欢快、毫不费力可消化的明亮、异想教育魅力。受影响于：Kurzgesagt 工作室 house 风格、flat-vector 解说插画传统、极简信息图设计技法、当代教育动画实践。

Modern Super Mario (Nintendo) Style: A vibrant, polished 3D-cartoon illustration style built on smooth, rounded proportion carrying confident, appealing simplicity, glossy, evenly saturated shading with soft, controlled specular highlight rather than gritty realism, bright, clean cinematic lighting establishing cheerful, family-friendly warmth within every frame, meticulous attention to bouncy, exaggerated posing charged with playful energy, and a polished, high-production platformer vibrancy that feels bright, joyful, and unmistakably iconic. Influenced by: Nintendo character-art direction, contemporary 3D-platformer rendering technique, stylized cartoon-render practice, family-adventure animation method.
Modern Super Mario (Nintendo) Style（现代超级马里奥风格）：一种 vibrant、抛光 3D 卡通插画风格，以携带自信、讨喜简化的平滑、圆润比例，带柔软、受控高光而非 gritty 写实的 glossy、均匀饱和阴影，在每个画幅建立欢快、家庭友好温暖的明亮、干净电影光，对携带俏皮能量的弹跳、夸张姿态的精细考究，以及明亮、欢乐、毋庸置疑 iconic 的抛光、高制作平台游戏活力。受影响于：任天堂角色艺术指导、当代 3D 平台游戏渲染技法、风格化卡通渲染实践、家庭冒险动画方法。
