Star Wars: The Clone Wars Style: A bold, angular animation-illustration style built on stylized, geometric character proportion with sharply defined, faceted silhouette construction, confident cel-shaded rendering with graphic, high-contrast shadow blocking rather than smooth gradation, dramatic cinematic lighting establishing epic, sweeping mood within stylized composition, meticulous attention to dynamic, action-driven posing balanced against clean graphic clarity, and a bold, blockbuster-grade stylized-animation intensity that feels both angular and cinematically grand. Influenced by: Dave Filoni, stylized 3D-animation technique, geometric character-design practice, cinematic sci-fi animation method.
Star Wars: The Clone Wars Style（星球大战：克隆人战争风格）：一种 bold、棱角动画插画风格，以具 sharply defined、faceted 剪影构建的风格化、几何角色比例，具图形、高对比暗块而非平滑渐变的自信赛璐珞渲染，在风格化构图建立史诗、sweeping 情绪的戏剧性电影光，对平衡干净图形清晰度的动态、动作驱动姿态的精细考究，以及既棱角又电影宏大的 bold、大片级风格化动画强度。受影响于：Dave Filoni、风格化 3D 动画技法、几何角色设计实践、电影感科幻动画方法。

M.C. Escher Style: A meticulously precise illustration style built on impossible, self-referential perspective that folds space back onto itself with mathematical rigor, confident, exacting linework rendering interlocking, tessellated form with obsessive geometric discipline, restrained tonal shading achieved through fine hatching that reinforces structural logic rather than atmosphere, a hypnotic, paradoxical rhythm carried through recursive, endlessly repeating pattern, and a meditative, intellectually rigorous precision that treats visual paradox itself as the compositional subject. Influenced by: M.C. Escher, tessellation-based printmaking technique, mathematical-illustration tradition, engraving-adjacent linework practice.
M.C. Escher Style（埃舍尔风格）：一种 meticulous 精度插画风格，以数学 rigor 将空间 fold 回自身的 impossible、自指透视，以偏执几何纪律渲染 interlocking、tessellated 形态的自信、exacting 线条，通过强化结构逻辑而非氛围的细排线实现克制色调阴影，通过 recursive、endlessly repeating 图案携带的催眠、悖论节奏，以及将视觉悖论本身视为构图主题的沉思、智性严谨精度。受影响于：M.C. Escher、tessellation 版画技法、数学插画传统、版画邻接线条实践。

Vignette-Framed Central Composition: A tightly unified illustration style built on a distinct central cluster containing figure and surrounding elements together as one cohesive silhouette, generous negative space extending outward on every side rather than a continuous filled composition, the enclosing elements arranged deliberately to form an implied frame-like boundary rather than a naturalistic full scene, meticulous compositional discipline keeping every detail confined within that central shape while the periphery stays quiet and empty, and a poised, deliberate graphic clarity that treats negative space itself as an essential compositional partner. Influenced by: contemporary editorial-illustration composition technique, vignette-portrait framing practice, negative-space compositional design, decorative-border illustration tradition.
Vignette-Framed Central Composition（晕影框定中心构图）：一种 tightly unified 插画风格，以包含人物与周围元素为单一 cohesive 剪影的 distinct 中心簇，向外的 generous 负空间而非连续填充构图，deliberate 安排 enclosing 元素以形成 implied frame 式边界而非自然全场景，将每个细节 confine 于该中心形状的 meticulous 构图纪律、而 periphery 安静空置，以及将负空间本身视为必要构图伙伴的 poised、deliberate 图形清晰度。受影响于：当代编辑插画构图技法、晕影肖像取景实践、负空间构图设计、装饰边框插画传统。

1960s Psychedelic Poster Art: A vibrant, maximalist illustration style built on radiating, sunburst-like linework fanning outward from a central focal point with hypnotic, rhythmic repetition, dense, swirling ornamental pattern-work filling every available extent with restless organic motion, bold, confidently bounded flat shapes separated by crisp, unmodulated outline, an ecstatic, kaleidoscopic energy that treats pattern density itself as the compositional subject, and a joyfully exuberant, retro commercial-art intensity that feels immersive, celebratory, and unmistakably era-defining. Influenced by: Peter Max, 1960s concert-poster illustration tradition, psychedelic advertising-art technique, Art Nouveau ornamental linework reimagined.
1960s Psychedelic Poster Art（1960年代迷幻海报艺术）：一种 vibrant、maximalist 插画风格，以从中心焦点 outward fanning 的 radiating、sunburst 式线条与催眠、rhythmic 重复，以 restless 有机运动填充每个可用范围的 dense、swirling 装饰图案，以 crisp、unmodulated 轮廓分隔的 bold、自信 bounded flat 形状，将图案密度本身视为构图主题的 ecstatic、kaleidoscopic 能量，以及 immersive、celebratory、毋庸置疑定义时代的 joyfully exuberant、复古商业艺术强度。受影响于：Peter Max、1960 年代演唱会海报插画传统、迷幻广告艺术技法、新艺术运动装饰线条 reimagined。

Frutiger Aero: A glossy, optimistic digital-aesthetic style built on smooth, dewy translucence layered over every surface like a soft protective sheen, bright, evenly diffused lighting that keeps the whole composition feeling clean and airy, gentle lens-flare bloom radiating with cheerful, tech-utopian positivity, crisp, rounded rendering logic that softens every edge into approachable, friendly polish, and a buoyant, forward-looking digital sincerity rooted in mid-2000s techno-optimism. Influenced by: mid-2000s tech-interface design tradition, Frutiger Aero aesthetic movement, glossy UI-rendering practice, early Web 2.0 digital-design technique.
Frutiger Aero（Frutiger Aero 风格）：一种 glossy、乐观数字美学风格，以如柔软保护光泽层叠每个表面的平滑、dewy 半透明，保持整个构图干净、airy 的明亮、均匀漫射光，以欢快、tech-utopian 积极性的温柔镜头光晕 bloom，将每个边缘 soften 为易接近、友好抛光的 crisp、圆润渲染逻辑，以及根植 2000 年代中期 techno-optimism 的 buoyant、forward-looking 数字真诚感。受影响于：2000 年代中期 tech 界面设计传统、Frutiger Aero 美学运动、glossy UI 渲染实践、早期 Web 2.0 数字设计技法。

Alex Grey Style: A visionary, anatomically luminous illustration style built on translucent layered rendering that reveals internal structure and energetic form simultaneously within a single figure, meticulous symmetrical composition radiating outward with mandala-like precision, an intense, glowing internal luminosity that seems to emanate from beneath the outermost extent, dense, interwoven detail merging scientific anatomical accuracy with spiritual, mystical iconography, and a mesmerizing, transcendent intensity that treats the body as both physical structure and radiant energy field. Influenced by: Alex Grey, visionary-art movement tradition, anatomical illustration technique, sacred-geometry digital-painting practice.
Alex Grey Style（亚历克斯·格雷风格）：一种 visionary、解剖发光的插画风格，以 simultaneous 揭示内部结构与能量形态的半透明层叠渲染，以 mandala 般精度 outward radiating 的 meticulous 对称构图，似从最外表层下 emanate 的 intense、glowing 内部发光，merge 科学解剖精度与精神、mystical 图像的 dense、interwoven 细节，以及将身体视为物理结构与原 radiant 能量场的 mesmerizing、transcendent 强度。受影响于：Alex Grey、visionary 艺术运动传统、解剖插画技法、神圣几何数字绘画实践。

Vignette Tattoo Design (Flat Print Format): A bold, graphic linework style built on confident unbroken contour lines contained within a distinct central cluster, generous negative space surrounding that cluster rather than a full-frame composition, deliberate simplification of form into clean readable shapes with minimal fine detail, restrained shading achieved through controlled linework density rather than gradation, a strong emphasis on symmetrical, balanced compositional flow suited for clean printable reproduction, and a bold, timeless graphic durability that reads clearly at both close range and a distance. Influenced by: traditional American tattoo flash-art tradition, Japanese irezumi technique, blackwork tattoo-design practice, negative-space compositional design.
Vignette Tattoo Design (Flat Print Format)（晕影纹身设计·平涂印刷格式）：一种 bold、图形线条风格，以 contained 于 distinct 中心簇的自信 unbroken 轮廓线，该簇周围而非全画幅的 generous 负空间，deliberate 简化形态为干净可读形状、极少精细细节，通过受控线条密度而非渐变实现的克制阴影，适合干净可印刷再现的对称、平衡构图流强强调，以及近距离与远距离皆清晰可读的 bold、永恒图形耐久性。受影响于：传统美式纹身 flash 艺术传统、日本 irezumi 技法、blackwork 纹身设计实践、负空间构图设计。

Vignette Sticker: A bold, self-contained graphic illustration style built on a crisp, clean bold white outline that isolates the subject from a plain white background, as a single cohesive shape contained within a distinct central cluster, generous white negative space surrounding that cluster with a thick, uniform contour holding the entire boundary, confidently simplified color fields with minimal internal shading, a slightly raised, glossy die-cut quality suggested through a subtle contour highlight tracing the outer extent, and a cheerful, punchy graphic charm built for immediate, repeatable visual impact. Influenced by: vinyl sticker/decal design tradition, detailed vector illustration technique, die-cut print production method, negative-space compositional design.
Vignette Sticker（晕影贴纸）：一种 bold、self-contained 图形插画风格，以将主体从纯白背景孤立的 crisp、干净 bold 白轮廓，contained 于 distinct 中心簇的单一 cohesive 形状，带 thick、uniform 轮廓 holding 整个边界的该簇周围 generous 白负空间，极少内部阴影的自信简化色彩场，通过 tracing 最外表层的微妙轮廓高光 suggest 的略 raised、glossy 模切品质，以及为瞬时、可重复视觉冲击的欢快、punchy 图形魅力。受影响于：vinyl 贴纸/转移贴设计传统、精细矢量插画技法、模切印刷生产方法、负空间构图设计。

Icon/Emoticon Vignette: A compact, simplified illustration style built on a distinct central cluster containing the subject reduced to its most essential recognizable shapes, generous negative space surrounding that cluster rather than a full-frame scene, bold, confidently bounded flat color fields separated by clean, uniform outline, minimal internal detail favoring instant small-scale readability over rendering complexity, and a cheerful, graphic economy that treats the whole composition as a single, immediately legible symbol. Influenced by: emoji/icon-design tradition, flat-vector illustration technique, minimalist symbol-design practice, negative-space compositional design.
Icon/Emoticon Vignette（图标/表情晕影）：一种 compact、简化插画风格，以将主体 reduce 至最本质可辨识形状的 distinct 中心簇，该簇周围而非全画幅场景的 generous 负空间，以干净、uniform 轮廓分隔的 bold、自信 bounded flat 色彩场，优先小尺度瞬时可读性而非渲染复杂度的极少内部细节，以及将整个构图视为单一、即时可读符号的欢快、图形经济。受影响于：emoji/图标设计传统、flat-vector 插画技法、极简符号设计实践、负空间构图设计。

Blueprint: A precise, technical illustration style built on crisp white linework laid over a deep, uniform blue field, exacting mechanical accuracy in every construction line and dimensional marker, minimal to no shading beyond the line itself carrying all structural information, a rigorous, drafting-level clarity that favors exact proportion over expressive interpretation, and a clean, methodical exactness rooted in centuries of engineering and architectural drafting discipline. Influenced by: technical/engineering-drawing tradition, architectural-blueprint drafting technique, cyanotype printing process, orthographic-projection drawing method.
Blueprint（蓝图）：一种精确、技术插画风格，以 deep、uniform 蓝场上的 crisp 白线，每个构建线与尺寸标记的 exacting 机械精度，除承载所有结构信息的线本身外极少或无阴影，优先精确比例而非表现性诠释的 rigorous、制图级清晰度，以及根植数世纪工程与建筑制图纪律的干净、methodical 精确。受影响于：技术/工程制图传统、建筑蓝图制图技法、蓝晒印刷过程、正投影画法。

Bold Painterly Impasto: A richly textured painting style built on thick, confident circular brushstrokes that curl and swirl across every extent, dense impasto ridges catching directional light with sculptural, tactile weight, sweeping curved strokes carrying dynamic, windswept motion throughout the composition, a warm, glowing luminosity built through layered circular dabs rather than smooth blending, and a lush, expressive painterly grandeur that feels alive with rhythmic, swirling energy. Influenced by: J.C. Leyendecker, Vincent van Gogh, impasto oil-painting technique, contemporary digital-painting practice.
Bold Painterly Impasto（大胆厚涂笔触）：一种 richly textured 绘画风格，以 across 每个范围 curl、swirl 的厚、自信 circular 笔触，以雕塑、tactile 重量 catch 方向光的 dense impasto ridge，携带 dynamic、windswept 运动的 sweeping curved stroke，通过层叠 circular dab 而非平滑混合构建的温暖、glowing 发光，以及 alive with rhythmic、swirling 能量的 lush、表现性绘画宏伟感。受影响于：J.C. Leyendecker、Vincent van Gogh、impasto 油画技法、当代数字绘画实践。

The Mitchells vs. The Machines animation Style: A vibrant, hybrid animation-illustration style built on smooth 3D-rendered character forms overlaid with wet fluid brushstrokes and painterly-like accents layered as texture directly into the frame, exaggerated, elastic proportion balancing 3D dimensionality with 2D cartoon energy, bold, expressive shading that mixes painterly gradient with graphic flat-color punch, meticulous attention to chaotic, personality-driven posing charged with comedic warmth, and a distinctive, boundary-breaking animation intensity that feels handmade, playful, and unmistakably contemporary. Influenced by: Sony Pictures Animation, mixed 2D/3D hybrid-animation technique, paint-overlay practice, contemporary indie-animation character-design method.
The Mitchells vs. The Machines animation Style（米切尔一家大战机器人动画风格）：一种 vibrant、混合动画插画风格，以叠加 wet fluid 笔触与绘画性 accent 直接作纹理层入画幅的平滑 3D 渲染人物形态，平衡 3D 维度与 2D 卡通能量的夸张、elastic 比例，mix 绘画性渐变与图形 flat 色 punch 的 bold、表现性阴影，对携带喜剧温暖的 chaotic、个性驱动姿态的精细考究，以及手工感、俏皮、毋庸置疑当代的 distinctive、破界动画强度。受影响于：索尼动画、混合 2D/3D 混合动画技法、paint-overlay 实践、当代独立动画角色设计方法。

Spider-Verse Style: A kinetic mixed-media animation-illustration style built on visible halftone dot patterning and offset-print texture layered directly into character rendering, deliberate chromatic-fringe misregistration echoing vintage comic-printing imperfection, bold graphic linework,  animation intensity that feels handmade, glitchy, and alive. Influenced by: Sony Pictures Animation, Kris Anka, halftone comic-printing technique, mixed-media collage-animation practice.
Spider-Verse Style（蜘蛛侠：平行宇宙风格）：一种 kinetic 混合媒介动画插画风格，以直接层叠进人物渲染的可见半调点图案与胶印纹理，echo 复古漫画印刷不完美的 deliberate 色差 misregistration，bold 图形线条，感到手工、glitchy、alive 的动画强度。受影响于：索尼动画、Kris Anka、半调漫画印刷技法、混合媒介拼贴动画实践。

Steampunk: A richly ornamental Victorian-industrial illustration style built on intricate mechanical detail rendered with meticulous, gear-and-cog precision, dramatic directional lighting catching every riveted contour and polished brass-adjacent gleam, confident linework blending vintage engraving discipline with fantastical technical invention, a hushed, adventurous grandeur carried through richly layered ornamental complexity, and a nostalgic, retro-futurist intensity that feels handcrafted, inventive, and unmistakably era-blending. Influenced by: Jules Verne illustration tradition, Victorian engraving technique, retro-futurist mechanical-design practice, steampunk concept-art method.,
Steampunk（蒸汽朋克）：一种 richly ornamental 维多利亚工业插画风格，以 meticulous、gear-and-cog 精度的 intricate 机械细节渲染，catch 每个铆接轮廓与 polished brass 邻接 gleam 的戏剧性方向光，blend 复古版画纪律与 fantastical 技术发明的自信线条，通过 richly layered 装饰复杂携带的轻声、冒险宏伟，以及手工感、inventive、毋庸置疑跨界 blend 的怀旧、复古未来主义强度。受影响于：儒勒·凡尔纳插画传统、维多利亚版画技法、复古未来主义机械设计实践、蒸汽朋克概念艺术方法。

Unreal Engine: A dimensionally precise real-time rendering style built on smooth, volumetric modeling calculated through simulated physical light and geometry, meticulously computed illumination with soft ambient occlusion settling naturally into every contour, polished specular response giving forms convincing physical depth and weight, confident dynamic reflection and atmospheric scattering rendered in real time, and a technically precise, computer-generated intensity built for cinematic, game-ready visual fidelity. Influenced by: Unreal Engine 5 real-time rendering technique, physically based rendering (PBR) method, contemporary game-cinematic rendering practice, real-time ray-tracing technology.
Unreal Engine（虚幻引擎）：一种维度精确的实时渲染风格，以通过模拟物理光与几何计算的平滑、体积建模，自然 settle 入每个轮廓的柔软环境光遮蔽的 meticulous 计算照明，赋予形态可信物理纵深与重量的抛光高光响应，实时渲染的自信动态反射与大气散射，以及为电影感、game-ready 视觉保真而生的技术精确、计算机生成强度。受影响于：虚幻引擎 5 实时渲染技法、基于物理渲染（PBR）方法、当代游戏电影渲染实践、实时光线追踪技术。

Jean-Gabriel Domergue Style: An elegant Art Deco portrait-illustration style built on smooth, confident linework tracing idealized, statuesque proportion with graceful economy, refined tonal shading applied with restrained, sophisticated subtlety rather than dramatic contrast, a poised, glamorous stillness carried through deliberate, composed posing, meticulous attention to graceful line quality over exhaustive detail, and a chic, timeless sophistication that feels effortlessly elegant and quietly luxurious. Influenced by: Jean-Gabriel Domergue, Art Deco portraiture but modern, French illustration contemporary technique , glamour-digital illustration,
Jean-Gabriel Domergue Style（让-加布里埃尔·多默格风格）：一种优雅装饰艺术肖像插画风格，以 graceful 经济 tracing 理想化、statuesque 比例的平滑、自信线条，以克制、成熟微妙而非戏剧对比应用的精炼色调阴影，通过 deliberate、composed 姿态携带的 poised、glamorous 静谧，对优雅线质而非 exhaustive 细节的精细考究，以及毫不费力优雅、quietly 奢华的 chic、永恒成熟感。受影响于：Jean-Gabriel Domergue、装饰艺术肖像但现代、法国当代插画技法、glamour 数字插画。

Modern Lithography: A refined printmaking-inspired contemporary  illustration style built on flat, confidently limited color layers reminiscent of hand-pulled stone-plate printing, subtle grain and texture mimicking the imperfect registration of lithographic process, clean bold linework holding each color plane with graphic precision, restrained tonal shading achieved through crosshatched or stippled print texture rather than smooth gradients, and a contemporary print-craft polish that feels tactile, considered, and editorial. Influenced by: modern lithographic printmaking, poster art, screenprint illustration technique, editorial print design.
Modern Lithography（现代石版画）：一种精炼、受版画启发的当代插画风格，以 reminiscent 手拉石版印刷的 flat、自信有限色彩层，mimic 石版过程不完美套版的微妙颗粒与纹理，以图形精度固定每个色彩平面的干净 bold 线条，通过交叉排线或点刻印刷纹理而非平滑渐变实现的克制色调阴影，以及 tactile、考究、编辑性的当代版画抛光感。受影响于：现代石版印刷、海报艺术、丝网印插画技法、编辑印刷设计。

Neon-Based Drawing: A vivid, glow-driven illustration style built on intensely saturated linework that appears to emit light rather than simply mark the surface, deep, engulfing darkness surrounding luminous, radiant contour, sharp electric bloom softening the edges of every glowing line, a hypnotic, nocturnal energy carried through dramatic tonal extremes, and a striking, electric vibrancy that feels charged, contemporary, and unmistakably illuminated from within. Influenced by: neon-sign illustration tradition, glow-line digital-rendering technique, contemporary vector-glow illustration practice, luminous linework design method.
Neon-Based Drawing（霓虹线条绘画）：一种 vivid、glow 驱动插画风格，以似 emit 光而非 mere 标记表面的 intensely 饱和线条，包围 luminous、radiant 轮廓的深、吞噬黑暗，soften 每个发光线边缘的 sharp electric bloom，通过戏剧性色调极端携带的催眠、夜间能量，以及 charged、当代、毋庸置疑由内照亮的 striking、electric 活力。受影响于：霓虹招牌插画传统、glow-line 数字渲染技法、当代矢量 glow 插画实践、luminous 线条设计方法。

Op-Art Style: A minimalist line-based hypnotic optical-abstract-illustration style built on precise repeating geometric stylized and angular patterning that tricks the eye into perceived movement and depth, rhythmic curvy and angular bold linework or shape progressions that pulse and vibrate, a rigorous mathematical geometry replacing any painterly looseness, figure and pattern merging so the body itself becomes part of the rhythm, and a crisp, disorienting, almost hypnotic graphic intensity that rewards sustained looking.subject abstracted into shapes within shapes, Influenced by: Bridget Riley, Victor Vasarely, minimalist bold-fixed-width-lines geometric abstraction, kinetic op-art technique.
Op-Art Style（欧普艺术风格）：一种基于极简线、催眠光学抽象插画风格，以 trick 眼睛感知运动与纵深的精确重复几何风格化与棱角图案，pulse、vibrate 的 rhythmic curvy 与棱角 bold 线条或形状递进，取代任何绘画性松散的 rigorous 数学几何，身体本身成为节奏一部分的 figure 与图案 merge，以及 reward 持续细看的 crisp、disorienting、almost 催眠图形强度（subject abstracted into shapes within shapes）。受影响于：Bridget Riley、Victor Vasarely、极简 bold-fixed-width-lines 几何抽象、kinetic 欧普艺术技法。

Overwatch Concept Art: A polished, vibrant game-illustration style built on clean, confident linework blended with smooth painterly rendering, bright, saturated lighting logic establishing bold, heroic clarity within a single dynamic composition, clean, readable silhouette design, dynamic, energetic posing charged with confident personality and readable character archetype, and a polished, blockbuster-grade game-art vibrancy built for instant character appeal and franchise recognition. Influenced by: Overwatch video-game, Blizzard Entertainment concept-art direction, stylized hero-shooter character-design tradition, painterly game-illustration technique, vibrant franchise key-art practice.
Overwatch Concept Art（守望先锋概念艺术）：一种抛光、vibrant 游戏插画风格，以干净、自信线条 blend 平滑绘画性渲染，在单一动态构图建立 bold、英雄清晰度的明亮、饱和灯光逻辑，干净、可读剪影设计，携带自信个性与可读角色原型的动态、充满能量姿态，以及为瞬时角色吸引力与 IP 辨识而生的抛光、大片级游戏艺术活力。受影响于：守望先锋电子游戏、暴雪娱乐概念艺术指导、风格化 hero-shooter 角色设计传统、绘画性游戏插画技法、vibrant IP 主视觉实践。

Psychedelia Style: A hallucinatory, maximalist illustration style built on swirling, undulating linework that warps form into rhythmic, organic distortion, dense repeating pattern-work that pulses and radiates outward from every surface, a deliberate flattening of depth that lets ornamental motif and figure merge into a single continuous visual field, hypnotic radial or wave-based compositional energy charged with sensory overload, and an ecstatic, mind-bending intensity that feels perpetually in motion even in stillness. Influenced by: Peter Max, concert-poster illustration tradition, psychedelic rock album-art technique,drug induced art, Art Nouveau ornamental linework reimagined.
Psychedelia Style（迷幻风格）：一种 hallucinatory、maximalist 插画风格，以 warp 形态为 rhythmic、有机扭曲的 swirling、undulating 线条，从每个表面 pulse、radiate outward 的 dense repeating 图案，让装饰母题与 figure merge 为单一连续视觉场的 deliberate 深度 flattening，charged 感官过载的催眠 radial 或 wave 式构图能量，以及即便静止也 perpetual 运动的 ecstatic、mind-bending 强度。受影响于：Peter Max、演唱会海报插画传统、迷幻摇滚专辑艺术技法、致幻艺术、新艺术运动装饰线条 reimagined。

Playmobil: A blocky, modular figure style built on simplified, cylindrical limbs and clip-jointed construction standing in for naturalistic anatomy, a glossy, uniformly smooth plastic-like finish applied consistently across every segment, a distinctive rounded head with minimal, painted-style facial simplification, confident, clean separation between individually distinguishable component pieces, and a cheerful, systematized toy-design precision rooted in decades of modular construction-set tradition. Influenced by: Playmobil product-design tradition, modular construction-toy aesthetic, plastic-injection product-visualization practice, procedural toy-figure rendering method.
Playmobil（摩比世界/Playmobil）：一种 blocky、模块化人物风格，以取代自然解剖的简化、圆柱四肢与 clip-jointed 构建，一致应用于每个段的 glossy、均匀光滑 plastic 般 finish，带极简、painted 式面部简化的 distinctive 圆头，个体可辨组件间的自信、干净分离，以及根植数十年模块化构建套装传统的欢快、systematized 玩具设计精度。受影响于：Playmobil 产品设计传统、模块化构建玩具美学、塑料注塑产品可视化实践、程序化玩具人物渲染方法。

Camille Walala Style: A vibrant, maximalist illustration style built on bold, confidently bounded geometric shapes arranged in dense, rhythmic repetition, thick, uniform outline holding every flat-color region with graphic precision, an energetic, kaleidoscopic pattern-work covering nearly every extent of the composition, a playful, optical-pop confidence replacing any naturalistic rendering entirely, and a joyful, high-energy graphic-design intensity that feels bold, celebratory, and unmistakably contemporary. Influenced by: Camille Walala,  geometric pattern-illustration technique, contemporary mural-art practice.
Camille Walala Style（卡米尔·瓦拉拉风格）：一种 vibrant、maximalist 插画风格，以 dense、rhythmic 重复安排的 bold、自信 bounded 几何形状，以图形精度固定每个 flat 色彩区域的 thick、uniform 轮廓，覆盖几乎整个构图的 energetic、kaleidoscopic 图案，完全取代任何自然渲染的俏皮、optical-pop 自信，以及 bold、celebratory、毋庸置疑当代的欢乐、高能量平面设计强度。受影响于：Camille Walala、几何图案插画技法、当代壁画艺术实践。

C.F.A. Voysey illustration: An elegant decorative illustration built on flowing, stylized organic patterning arranged with disciplined, rhythmic repetition, flat, confidently bounded shape divisions replacing naturalistic shading entirely, meticulous linear precision tracing every motif with restrained, handcrafted care, a calm, orderly compositional harmony favoring balance over ornamental excess, and a quietly refined, timeless craft sincerity rooted in early-20th-century decorative-design reform. Influenced by: C.F.A. Voysey, Arts and Crafts movement tradition, William Morris decorative-pattern technique, English textile-and-wallpaper-design practice.
C.F.A. Voysey illustration（C.F.A. Voysey 插画）：一种优雅装饰插画，以 disciplined、rhythmic 重复安排的 flowing、风格化有机图案，完全取代自然阴影的 flat、自信 bounded 形状分割，以克制、手工 care tracing 每个母题的 meticulous 线性精度，优先平衡而非装饰过度的 calm、orderly 构图和谐，以及根植 20 世纪初装饰设计改革的 quiet 精炼、永恒工艺真诚感。受影响于：C.F.A. Voysey、工艺美术运动传统、William Morris 装饰图案技法、英国纺织品与壁纸设计实践。

Neon Halftone Retro-Thriller Illustration: A striking screenprint-inspired illustration style built on bold, high-contrast graphic shapes rendered through dense horizontal line-scan patterning that mimics offset-print texture, dramatic dual-toned lighting where sharp electric accents cross the subject against deep, silhouetted shadow, confident vector-clean contour holding every form with poster-ready precision, atmospheric radiating arcs suggesting motion and depth behind the central figure, and a bold, cinematic screenprint intensity that feels stylish, tense, and unmistakably retro-thriller. Influenced by: Jonathan Burton, contemporary screenprint movie-poster tradition, halftone scan-line illustration technique, synthwave-adjacent poster-design practice.
Neon Halftone Retro-Thriller Illustration（霓虹半调复古惊悚插画）：一种 striking、受丝网印启发插画风格，以 mimic 胶印纹理的 dense 水平线扫描图案渲染的 bold、高对比图形形状，sharp electric accent 横切主体对抗 deep、silhouetted 阴影的戏剧性双色调灯光，以海报级精度固定每个形态的自信矢量干净轮廓，suggest 中心人物后运动与纵深的氛围 radiating 弧，以及 stylish、tense、毋庸置疑复古惊悚的 bold、电影感丝网印强度。受影响于：Jonathan Burton、当代丝网印电影海报传统、半调扫描线插画技法、synthwave 邻接海报设计实践。

Topographic Linework Illustration: intricate graphic aesthetics characterized by flowing contour networks, layered elevation-inspired line structures, organic spatial mapping, rhythmic density variation, and a strong emphasis on visual terrain-like construction. The style builds form through successive line accumulations rather than traditional shading, creating depth, volume, and movement through contour logic, directional patterning, and carefully orchestrated line repetition. Rather than relying on solid masses or painterly rendering, it celebrates structure, flow, and information-rich design, producing imagery that feels both analytical and artistic. The resulting aesthetic combines cartographic sophistication, decorative complexity, and contemporary illustration sensibilities into highly distinctive visual compositions, reminiscent of Matthew Cusick and Paula Scher, inspired by USGS Topographic Maps and The Atlas of Design.
Topographic Linework Illustration（地形线条插画）：以 flowing 轮廓网络、layered 高程启发线结构、有机空间映射、rhythmic 密度变化，以及强视觉地形式构建为特征的 intricate 图形美学。该风格通过 successive 线积累而非传统阴影构建形态，通过轮廓逻辑、方向图案与 carefully orchestrated 线重复创作纵深、体积与运动；不依赖实mass 或绘画性渲染，而赞美结构、flow 与信息丰富设计，产出既分析又艺术的 image。结果美学将制图 sophistication、装饰复杂性与当代插画感融合为 highly distinctive 视觉构图；令人联想到 Matthew Cusick 与 Paula Scher，灵感来自 USGS 地形图与《设计地图集》。

Negative Space Focused Illustration: A deliberately restrained illustration style built on a single confident focal cluster surrounded by vast, empty extent, minimal linework rendering only what's essential to read the subject clearly, a confident economy where the unmarked space carries as much compositional weight as the mark itself, silhouette and shape doing the primary storytelling work rather than internal detail, and a quiet, contemplative elegance that treats emptiness as an active, deliberate compositional partner. Influenced by: minimalist negative-space illustration tradition, Japanese ma compositional philosophy, contemporary editorial silhouette technique, reductive graphic-design practice.
Negative Space Focused Illustration（负空间聚焦插画）：一种 deliberately restrained 插画风格，以 vast、empty 范围包围的 single 自信焦点簇，仅渲染清晰读取主体所需 minimal 线条，未标记空间承载与标记同等构图重量的自信经济，silhouette 与形状而非内部细节做主要叙事工作，以及将空虚视为 active、deliberate 构图伙伴的安静、沉思优雅。受影响于：极简负空间插画传统、日本 ma 构图哲学、当代编辑剪影技法、reductive 平面设计实践。

Collectible Figure : A meticulously sculpted 1:12 scale action subject cast in rigid injection-molded ABS plastic with a glossy top coat, photographed under diffused lighting with a deep focus shot capturing every sculpted detail. Influenced by: the classic action figure toy, Hasbro product, 1980s action-toy.
Collectible Figure（收藏级可动人偶）：一种 meticulously sculpted 1:12 比例可动主体，以 glossy 顶涂浇铸的 rigid 注塑 ABS 塑料，在 deep focus 拍摄下捕捉每个雕塑细节的漫射灯光下拍摄。受影响于：经典可动人偶玩具、孩之宝产品、1980 年代可动玩具。

Tessellation-Based Illustration: A geometrically disciplined illustration style built on repeating interlocking shapes that fit together with mathematical precision leaving no gap between forms, flat, confidently bounded regions replacing any curved or blended transition, a rhythmic, meditative pattern-logic that treats the repeated unit itself as the compositional subject, confident symmetry and rotational repetition guiding the eye across the entire extent, and a hypnotic, meticulous graphic intensity rooted in centuries of geometric-pattern discipline. Influenced by: M.C. Escher, Islamic geometric-pattern tradition, tessellation-based printmaking technique, mathematical-illustration practice.
Tessellation-Based Illustration（镶嵌式插画）：一种几何 disciplined 插画风格，以数学精度 fit 在一起、形态间无间隙的 repeating interlocking 形状，取代任何曲线或混合过渡的 flat、自信 bounded 区域，将重复单元本身视为构图主题的 rhythmic、冥想图案逻辑，引导眼睛跨整个范围的自信对称与旋转重复，以及根植数世纪几何图案纪律的催眠、meticulous 图形强度。受影响于：M.C. Escher、伊斯兰几何图案传统、tessellation 版画技法、数学插画实践。

Neon Sign Design: A luminous graphic illustration style built on crisp, continuous linework that appears to glow rather than sit flat, an electric brightness concentrated along every contour with soft radiating bloom bleeding into the surrounding darkness, confident single-stroke construction mimicking the unbroken tubing of illuminated signage, minimal internal detail letting the glowing outline carry the entire composition, and a vibrant, nightlife-adjacent intensity that feels electric, retro-commercial, and unmistakably illuminated. Influenced by: neon-sign fabrication tradition, glow-line digital-rendering technique, mid-century commercial-signage design, contemporary vector-glow illustration practice.
Neon Sign Design（霓虹招牌设计）：一种 luminous 图形插画风格，以似 glow 而非 flat 的 crisp、continuous 线条，沿每个轮廓集中、bleed 入周围黑暗的 soft radiating bloom 的 electric 亮度，mimic 照明招牌 unbroken 管的自信单笔构建，让 glowing 轮廓承载整个构图的极少内部细节，以及 electric、retro-commercial、毋庸置疑照亮的 vibrant、nightlife 邻接强度。受影响于：霓虹招牌制作传统、glow-line 数字渲染技法、中叶世纪商业招牌设计、当代矢量 glow 插画实践。

Plastic Arts Sculpture: A synthetic figure built from glossy injection-molded vinyl and matte PVC, seam lines running subtly like a high-end designer art, the surface catching light with sharp specular falloff and glassy resin shine, translucent plastic edges glowing with internal light diffusion, microscopic surface scratches visible across the clear coat, soft gradient cyclorama lighting, soft shadows fall, the entire form reading as a physical collectible object rendered with ray-traced precision and shallow depth of field. Influenced by: Michael Lau's vinyl sculptures, Coarse's minimalism, KAWS' glossy sculpture, Parra's clean graphics, the rotocast vinyl tradition, the soft vinyl kaiju manufacturing aesthetic, and high-end product visualization photography.
Plastic Arts Sculpture（塑料艺术雕塑）：一种以 glossy 注塑 vinyl 与哑光 PVC 构建的合成人物，如高端设计师艺术的 subtle seam 线，表面以 sharp 高光衰减与 glassy resin 光泽 catch 光，translucent 塑料边缘以内部光扩散 glow，clear coat 上可见的显微表面划痕，柔软渐变 cyclorama 灯光，soft 阴影 fall，整个形态读作以 ray-traced 精度与浅景深渲染的物理收藏对象。受影响于：Michael Lau 的 vinyl 雕塑、Coarse 的极简主义、KAWS 的 glossy 雕塑、Parra 的干净图形、rotocast vinyl 传统、soft vinyl kaiju 制造美学，以及高端产品可视化摄影。

PS1 Screenshot: A low-polygon 3D scene rendered with the distinct technical limitations of playstation 1996, the subject built from sharp, unblended triangular facets with visibly segmented joint connections and blocky geometric anatomy, texture maps stretched and warped across flat polygon surfaces with visible pixelation and bilinear filtering artifacts, the environment constructed from repeating tiled textures with harsh seams and obvious draw-distance fog creeping in at mid-range, jagged stair-step edges along every diagonal line and object silhouette, the lighting flat and vertex-shaded with abrupt transitions between lit and shadowed faces, 320x240 resolution, muted 16-bit color depth, dithering patterns visible in shadow gradients and transparency effects achieved through stippled pixel alternation, Influenced by: Tomb Raider 1995 screenshot, Tekken 1996 screenshot, PS1 rendering pipeline, the tile-based environment construction of early 1990s 3D games, 32-bit console hardware, low-poly modeling,
PS1 Screenshot（PS1 截图）：以 1996 年 PlayStation 独特技术限制渲染的低多边形 3D 场景，主体由 sharp、unblended 三角 facet 构建，带可见 segmented 关节连接与 blocky 几何解剖，拉伸、扭曲跨 flat 多边形表面的纹理贴图带可见像素化与双线性过滤 artifacts，由 repeating tiled 纹理构建、带 harsh seams 与中距 creep in 的明显 draw-distance 雾的环境，沿每个对角线与对象剪影的 jagged stair-step 边缘，flat、vertex-shaded 灯光在 lit 与 shadowed 面间 abrupt 过渡，320x240 分辨率、muted 16-bit 色深，阴影渐变与经 stippled 像素交替实现的透明效果中可见 dithering 图案。受影响于：古墓丽影 1995 截图、铁拳 1996 截图、PS1 渲染管线、早期 1990 年代 3D 游戏 tile-based 环境构建、32-bit 主机硬件、低多边形建模。

Low Relief Sculpture: A dimensionally subtle sculptural style built on shallow, raised form projecting only slightly beyond its background rather than standing fully in the round, confident tonal modeling constructed entirely through carved depth and directional light rather than drawn line, dramatic raking illumination that reveals every subtle contour and recessed groove with graphic clarity, a restrained, disciplined economy of projection where even minimal relief conveys convincing volume, and a timeless, classical gravitas rooted in centuries of relief-carving tradition. Influenced by: classical bas-relief sculpture tradition, Lorenzo Ghiberti, architectural relief-carving technique, academic sculptural-modeling method.
Low Relief Sculpture（低浮雕雕塑）：一种维度 subtle 雕塑风格，以仅轻微突出背景而非 fully in the round 的 shallow、raised 形态，完全通过 carved 深度与方向光而非绘制线构建的自信色调建模，揭示每个微妙轮廓与凹陷 groove 的戏剧性 raking 照明，即便最小 relief 也 convey 可信体积的克制、disciplined 投影经济，以及根植数世纪浮雕雕刻传统的永恒、古典庄严感。受影响于：古典浅浮雕雕塑传统、Lorenzo Ghiberti、建筑浮雕雕刻技法、学院派雕塑建模方法。

The Sims Game Screenshot: A polished, gently stylized 3D-simulation illustration style built on smooth, rounded character proportion balancing cartoonish charm with grounded believability, bright, even lighting that keeps every scene warmly lit and legible, glossy specular response giving texture, sim-adjacent sheen, a confident, catalog-ready UI-clean rendering finish, and a cheerful, simulation intensity that feels bright, approachable, and unmistakably game-like. Influenced by: Maxis simulation-game rendering direction, The Sims Game, stylized 3D technique, cheerful lighting practice, simulation game-art,
The Sims Game Screenshot（模拟人生游戏截图）：一种抛光、gently 风格化 3D 模拟插画风格，以平衡卡通魅力与 grounded 可信度的平滑、圆润角色比例，保持每个场景温暖打光与可读的明亮、均匀灯光，赋予纹理 glossy 高光响应的 sim 邻接 sheen，自信、catalog-ready UI-clean 渲染完成度，以及明亮、approachable、毋庸置疑游戏感的欢快模拟强度。受影响于：Maxis 模拟游戏渲染方向、模拟人生游戏、风格化 3D 技法、欢快灯光实践、模拟游戏艺术。

Animal Crossing Style: A soft, rounded 3D-simulation illustration style built on gentle, oversized proportion favoring charm, smooth subsurface-scattered shading giving forms a warm, inviting glow, bright, cheerful lighting logic that keeps every scene feeling wholesome and inviting, meticulous attention to whimsical, cozy detail rendered with cheerful simplicity rather than technical grit, and a wholesome, gentle life-simulation intensity that feels playful, comforting, and effortlessly charming. Influenced by: Animal Crossing Nintendo art, stylized 3D-simulation, cozy life-simulation game-art, whimsical cartoon-render,.
Animal Crossing Style（动物森友会风格）：一种柔软、圆润 3D 模拟插画风格，以优先魅力的温柔、超大比例，赋予形态温暖、inviting glow 的平滑次表面散射阴影，保持每个场景 wholesome、inviting 的明亮、欢快灯光逻辑，以欢快简单而非技术 grit 渲染异想、cozy 细节的精细考究，以及俏皮、comforting、毫不费力迷人的 wholesome、gentle 生活模拟强度。受影响于：动物森友会任天堂艺术、风格化 3D 模拟、cozy 生活模拟游戏艺术、异想卡通渲染。

Partial Wireframe 3D: A dimensionally precise digital-illustration style built on smooth, volumetric modeling that transitions abruptly into exposed geometric mesh across a portion of the extent, meticulously calculated illumination and specular response covering the fully rendered sections while the mesh-revealed areas stay flat and diagrammatic, confident sculptural construction contrasted against clean, technical grid-lines, a deliberate tension between finished dimensional realism and raw underlying structure, and a technically precise, computer-generated intensity that treats construction itself as part of the compositional subject. Influenced by: real-time 3D-rendering technique, wireframe-mesh visualization practice, physically based rendering (PBR) method, CGI production-pipeline aesthetic.
Partial Wireframe 3D（局部线框 3D）：一种维度精确的数字插画风格，以平滑、体积建模在部分范围 abrupt 转为暴露几何网格，覆盖完全渲染段的 meticulous 计算照明与高光响应，而 mesh 暴露区保持 flat、diagrammatic，自信雕塑性构建对比干净、技术网格线，finished 维度写实与 raw 底层结构间的 deliberate 张力，以及将构建本身视为构图主题一部分的技术精确、计算机生成强度。受影响于：实时 3D 渲染技法、wireframe-mesh 可视化实践、基于物理渲染（PBR）方法、CGI 制作管线美学。

Digital Painting: A richly layered illustration style built on confident brushwork translated through digital tools while retaining the tactile logic of traditional painting, meticulous control over light behavior achieved through non-destructive layered blending, visible painterly texture preserved even within highly refined rendering, a technically flexible balance between loose gestural mark-making and precise, considered detail, and a polished, contemporary painterly gravitas that bridges classical technique with modern production tools. Influenced by: contemporary digital-painting tradition, Photoshop/Procreate painting workflow, classical oil-painting technique translated digitally, modern concept-art rendering practice.
Digital Painting（数字绘画）：一种 richly layered 插画风格，以经数字工具转译、保留传统绘画 tactile 逻辑的自信笔触，通过非破坏层叠混合实现对光行为的 meticulous 控制，即便高度精炼渲染中也保留可见绘画性纹理，松散 gestural mark-making 与精准、考究细节间的技术灵活平衡，以及桥接古典技法与现代制作工具的抛光、当代绘画性庄严感。受影响于：当代数字绘画传统、Photoshop/Procreate 绘画工作流、数字转译的古典油画技法、现代概念艺术渲染实践。

Castlevania Concept Art: A dark gothic fantasy concept-art style rendered with painterly digital linework and moody, desaturated color palettes, gothic vibes rendered in dramatic vertical perspective, richly textured surfaces, atmospheric fog and shadow pooling around sharply lit focal points, romantic horror atmosphere blending anime-influenced character design with Western dark-fantasy illustration. Influenced by: Castlevania (Netflix/Powerhouse Animation), Ayami Kojima, gothic horror illustration, dark fantasy concept art.
Castlevania Concept Art（恶魔城概念艺术）：一种黑暗哥特奇幻概念艺术风格，以绘画性数字线条与 moody、去饱和配色渲染，戏剧性垂直透视中的哥特氛围，richly textured 表面、atmospheric 雾与阴影 pooling 于锐利打光焦点，blend 动漫影响角色设计与西方黑暗奇幻插画的浪漫恐怖氛围。受影响于：《恶魔城》（Netflix/Powerhouse Animation）、Ayami Kojima、哥特恐怖插画、黑暗奇幻概念艺术。

Sakimichan Style: A glossy, ultra-polished fan-art illustration style blending photorealistic skin rendering and soft airbrushed shading with idealized anime facial proportions and large luminous eyes, richly saturated skin tones warmed by dramatic rim lighting and soft glowing highlights, meticulously rendered hair with individually defined glossy strands, sensual curve-forward poses softened by smooth gradient blending rather than hard cel lines, and a dreamy, high-production digital-painting finish that reads as premium fantasy pin-up art. Influenced by: Sakimichan, digital painting airbrush technique, anime-realism fusion art, fantasy character illustration.
Sakimichan Style（Sakimichan 风格）：一种 glossy、超抛光同人插画风格，blend 照片写实皮肤渲染与柔软喷枪阴影、理想化动漫面部比例与大 luminous 眼，由戏剧性边缘光与柔软发光高光温暖的 richly saturated 肤色，逐缕定义 glossy 发丝的 meticulous 渲染，由平滑渐变而非硬赛璐珞线 softened 的 sensual curve-forward 姿态，以及读作 premium 奇幻海报艺术的梦境、高制作数字绘画完成度。受影响于：Sakimichan、数字绘画喷枪技法、动漫-写实融合艺术、奇幻角色插画。

Scribble Fine Art: expressive fine art aesthetics characterized by energetic freeform mark-making, layered scribbled structures, spontaneous visual construction, dynamic line accumulation, and a deliberate balance between chaos and control. The style builds form, texture, and atmosphere through overlapping gestural lines, exploratory drawing decisions, and visible artistic thought processes, transforming sketch-like marks into sophisticated visual compositions. Rather than concealing construction, it celebrates improvisation, intuition, and the physical act of drawing, allowing complexity and emotion to emerge organically from repeated line interactions. The resulting aesthetic feels raw, intelligent, and deeply personal, blending contemporary fine art experimentation with refined compositional awareness and a strong sense of visual rhythm, reminiscent of Cy Twombly and Julie Mehretu, inspired by Untitled (New York City) and Stadia II.
Scribble Fine Art（涂鸦 Fine Art）：以充满能量自由 mark-making、层叠涂鸦结构、自发视觉构建、dynamic 线积累，以及 chaos 与 control 间 deliberate 平衡为特征的 expressive fine art 美学。该风格通过重叠 gestural 线、探索性绘画决策与可见艺术思维过程构建形态、纹理与氛围，将 sketch 般标记转为 sophisticated 视觉构图；不 conceal 构建，而赞美即兴、直觉与绘画物理行为，让复杂与情感从重复线交互中 organic 涌现。结果美学 raw、intelligent、deeply personal，blend 当代 fine art 实验与精炼构图意识及强视觉节奏；令人联想到 Cy Twombly 与 Julie Mehretu，灵感来自《Untitled (New York City)》与《Stadia II》。

Gris Grimly-Inspired Hyperrealism: A hyperrealistic gothic-macabre illustration style built on gnarled, spindly proportion rendered with obsessive photographic precision rather than loose sketch energy, meticulously detailed texture giving twisted, elongated form an uncanny, tangible weight, dramatic isolated lighting carving stark contrast between richly rendered detail and enveloping shadow, a hushed, eerie theatricality heightened rather than softened by the hyperreal finish, and a darkly whimsical yet unsettling intensity that feels both storybook and disturbingly convincing. Influenced by: Gris Grimly, hyperrealist digital-painting technique, gothic macabre-illustration tradition, dark-fantasy character-design practice.
Gris Grimly-Inspired Hyperrealism（格里斯·格里姆利启发超写实）：一种超写实哥特-macabre 插画风格，以而非松散速写能量的偏执照片精度渲染 gnarled、spindly 比例，赋予扭曲、拉长形态 uncanny、tangible 重量的 meticulous 细节纹理，在 richly 渲染细节与 enveloping 阴影间 carving stark 对比的戏剧性孤立灯光，被超写实完成度 heightened 而非 softened 的轻声、eerie 戏剧性，以及既绘本又 disturbingly convincing 的黑暗异想却 unsettling 强度。受影响于：Gris Grimly、超写实数字绘画技法、哥特 macabre 插画传统、黑暗奇幻角色设计实践。

Absolute Painterly Style: A richly tactile digital painting style built entirely on visible, confident brushwork rather than smooth blending, thick impasto-like strokes carrying real physical weight and direction across every surface, loose expressive edges that dissolve fine detail in favor of gestural energy, dramatic value contrast doing the heavy lifting instead of line, and a raw, emotive, unmistakably hand-painted intensity that celebrates the mark of the brush over polished precision. Influenced by: traditional oil painting technique, John Singer Sargent, impasto brushwork, classical atelier painting method.
Absolute Painterly Style（绝对绘画性风格）：一种 richly tactile 数字绘画风格，完全建于可见、自信笔触而非平滑混合，携带真实物理重量与方向的 thick impasto 般 strokes，dissolve 精细细节、优先 gestural 能量的松散表现性边缘，而非线条做重活的戏剧性明度对比，以及赞美笔触 mark 而非抛光精度的 raw、emotive、毋庸置疑手绘强度。受影响于：传统油画技法、John Singer Sargent、impasto 笔触、古典 atelier 绘画方法。

Artgerm Style: A polished digital-painting portrait style blending photorealistic rendering with idealized comic-glamour proportions, luminous porcelain skin with soft airbrushed gradients and crisp specular highlights, richly detailed glossy rendering carried strand by strand where fine linework is called for, confident glamorous poses framed with clean painterly composition, and a slick, high-production pin-up polish that bridges Western comic-cover art and digital beauty illustration. Influenced by: Stanley "Artgerm" Lau, comic-book cover illustration, digital pin-up art, airbrush portrait technique.
Artgerm Style（Artgerm 风格）：一种 blend 照片写实渲染与理想化漫画 glamour 比例的抛光数字绘画肖像风格，带柔软喷枪渐变与 crisp 高光的 luminous porcelain 皮肤，需细线处逐缕 glossy 渲染的 richly detailed，以干净绘画性构图框定的自信 glamour 姿态，以及桥接西方漫画封面艺术与数字美容插画的 slick、高制作海报抛光感。受影响于：Stanley "Artgerm" Lau、漫画书封面插画、数字海报艺术、喷枪肖像技法。

ArtStation Masterpiece: A high-production concept-art illustration style built on meticulously controlled rendering that balances painterly atmosphere with technical precision, dramatic cinematic lighting establishing mood and scale within a single portfolio-ready composition, richly detailed material and textural nuance rewarding close inspection without sacrificing overall painterly cohesion, confident, ambitious compositional scale calibrated for maximum visual impact at first glance, and a polished, industry-grade rendering intensity built to command attention within a competitive professional showcase. Influenced by: contemporary ArtStation concept-art community tradition, painterly digital-illustration technique, industry-standard portfolio-rendering practice, cinematic concept-art composition method.
ArtStation Masterpiece（ArtStation 杰作）：一种高制作概念艺术插画风格，以平衡绘画性氛围与技术精度的 meticulous 受控渲染，在单一 portfolio-ready 构图建立情绪与规模的戏剧性电影光，值得近观而不牺牲整体绘画性 cohesion 的 richly detailed 材质与纹理微妙，为第一眼最大视觉冲击校准的自信、ambitious 构图规模，以及为在竞争性专业展示中 command 注意而生的抛光、industry-grade 渲染强度。受影响于：当代 ArtStation 概念艺术社区传统、绘画性数字插画技法、industry-standard portfolio 渲染实践、电影感概念艺术构图方法。

Bastien Lecouffe-Deharme Fantasy: ethereal dark fantasy aesthetics blending refined realism, dreamlike atmosphere, elegant visual storytelling, painterly textures, and sophisticated cinematic lighting, emphasizing emotional depth, poetic melancholy, intricate craftsmanship, and immersive worldbuilding through highly polished rendering and a strong sense of mystery, reminiscent of Bastien Lecouffe-Deharme and Kinuko Y. Craft, inspired by Memories of Emanon and Pan's Labyrinth.
Bastien Lecouffe-Deharme Fantasy（Bastien Lecouffe-Deharme 奇幻）：blend 精炼写实、梦境氛围、优雅视觉叙事、绘画性纹理与成熟电影光的 ethereal 黑暗奇幻美学，通过高度抛光渲染与强神秘感，强调情感纵深、诗意忧郁、intricate 工艺与沉浸世界观构建；令人联想到 Bastien Lecouffe-Deharme 与 Kinuko Y. Craft，灵感来自《Memories of Emanon》与《潘神的迷宫》。

Bernie Wrightson Gothic: richly detailed gothic illustration characterized by intricate linework, masterful texture rendering, atmospheric darkness, organic complexity, dramatic chiaroscuro, and exceptional craftsmanship, blending classical draftsmanship with horror-infused visual storytelling through meticulous detail, immersive mood, and a strong sense of haunting romanticism, reminiscent of Bernie Wrightson and Gustave Doré, inspired by Frankenstein and Swamp Thing.
Bernie Wrightson Gothic（Bernie Wrightson 哥特）：以 intricate 线条、masterful 纹理渲染、atmospheric 黑暗、organic 复杂、戏剧性 chiaroscuro 与 exceptional 工艺为特征的 richly detailed 哥特插画，通过 meticulous 细节、沉浸情绪与强 haunting 浪漫主义，blend 古典造型与恐怖 infused 视觉叙事；令人联想到 Bernie Wrightson 与 Gustave Doré，灵感来自《科学怪人》与《沼泽怪物》。

Blacklight Style: A luminous glow-reactive illustration style built on stark contrast between an inert, absorbed base surface and intensely radiant fluorescing accents, an otherworldly internal luminosity that appears to emit rather than reflect, sharp separation between glowing and non-glowing regions with almost no transitional gradation, a surreal, electric intensity charged with nightclub-adjacent energy, and a hypnotic, ultraviolet-reactive vibrancy that feels alive in the dark. Influenced by: blacklight poster art tradition, fluorescent pigment illustration technique, psychedelic 1960s-70s poster design, UV-reactive body-art photography.
Blacklight Style（黑光风格）：一种 luminous glow-reactive 插画风格，以 inert、absorbed 基底表面与 intensely radiant 荧光 accent 间的 stark 对比，似 emit 而非 reflect 的超凡内部发光，glowing 与 non-glowing 区几乎无过渡渐变的 sharp 分离，charged 夜店邻接能量的 surreal、electric 强度，以及黑暗中 alive 的催眠、ultraviolet-reactive 活力。受影响于：黑光海报艺术传统、荧光颜料插画技法、1960–70 年代迷幻海报设计、UV-reactive 身体艺术摄影。

X-Ray Style: A translucent anatomical rendering style built on layered internal visibility beneath an otherwise solid exterior, soft luminous glow radiating from within rather than reflecting off the outer plane, precise structural linework revealing underlying form with clinical exactness, a stark, high-contrast tonal separation between dense and translucent areas, and an eerie, revelatory clarity that treats the body as both solid and see-through simultaneously. Influenced by: medical radiography technique, scientific illustration tradition, digital x-ray rendering effects, anatomical cross-section illustration.
X-Ray Style（X 光风格）：一种半透明解剖渲染风格，以 otherwise 固体 exterior 下的层叠内部可见性，从内而非外反射的柔软 luminous glow，以临床精度揭示底层形态的精确结构线条，dense 与 translucent 区间的 stark、高对比色调分离，以及将身体同时读作 solid 与 see-through 的 eerie、revelatory 清晰度。受影响于：医学放射摄影技法、科学插画传统、数字 X 光渲染效果、解剖横截面插画。

Brassaï Night Photography: nocturnal photographic aesthetics characterized by atmospheric illumination, deep tonal richness, urban mystery, reflective surfaces, cinematic shadow play, and evocative documentary realism, capturing the poetry of night through carefully observed light, immersive mood, and timeless visual storytelling while maintaining exceptional compositional discipline and photographic craftsmanship, reminiscent of Brassaï and Robert Doisneau, inspired by Paris de Nuit and French Humanist Photography.
Brassaï Night Photography（Brassaï 夜景摄影）：以 atmospheric 照明、deep 色调丰富度、urban 神秘、反射表面、电影感阴影 play 与 evocative 纪实写实为特征的 nocturnal 摄影美学，通过 carefully observed 光、沉浸情绪与永恒视觉叙事捕捉夜之诗，同时保持 exceptional 构图纪律与摄影工艺；令人联想到 Brassaï 与 Robert Doisneau，灵感来自《Paris de Nuit》与法国人道主义摄影。

Character Design Illustration: design-focused illustration aesthetics characterized by strong silhouette language, appealing form construction, clear visual hierarchy, expressive anatomy, refined shape design, and highly readable visual identity, emphasizing memorable presence and cohesive artistic intent through disciplined draftsmanship, polished rendering, and a balance between stylization and realism, reminiscent of Mike Mignola and Craig Mullins, inspired by Arcane and The Art of Overwatch.
Character Design Illustration（角色设计插画）：以强剪影语言、讨喜形态构建、清晰视觉层级、表现性解剖、精炼形状设计与高度可读视觉 identity 为特征的设计焦点插画美学，通过自律造型、抛光渲染与风格化-写实平衡，强调 memorable 存在感与 cohesive 艺术意图；令人联想到 Mike Mignola 与 Craig Mullins，灵感来自《双城之战》与《守望先锋艺术集》。

Charlie Bowater Style: A richly painterly digital illustration style built on soft, glowing directional lighting that sculpts form with confident value control, delicately rendered skin and hair carrying a luminous, almost photographic softness within an otherwise painterly composition, fine decorative detail in costume and ornamentation rendered with meticulous restraint, a hushed, cinematic stillness charged with quiet emotional weight, and a polished, atmospheric fantasy-illustration intensity that balances realism with painterly warmth. Influenced by: Charlie Bowater, digital fantasy portraiture, painterly concept-art technique, cinematic character illustration.
Charlie Bowater Style（Charlie Bowater 风格）：一种 richly painterly 数字插画风格，以雕塑形态、confident 明度控制的柔软、glowing 方向光，在 otherwise painterly 构图中带几乎照片柔软的 delicately 渲染皮肤与头发，以 meticulous 克制渲染服装与装饰的精细细节，charged 安静情感重量的轻声、电影感静谧，以及平衡写实与绘画性温暖的抛光、atmospheric 奇幻插画强度。受影响于：Charlie Bowater、数字奇幻肖像、绘画性概念艺术技法、电影感角色插画。

Contemporary Fantasy Art: modern fantasy aesthetics characterized by sophisticated visual design, refined painterly rendering, cinematic atmosphere, immersive worldbuilding, and a balance between realism and stylization, emphasizing artistic originality, premium craftsmanship, and contemporary entertainment-art sensibilities through controlled detail, elegant lighting, and highly polished execution, reminiscent of Marc Simonetti and Ruan Jia, inspired by The Witcher Concept Art and Magic: The Gathering Modern Masters.
Contemporary Fantasy Art（当代奇幻艺术）：以成熟视觉设计、精炼绘画性渲染、电影感氛围、沉浸世界观与写实-风格化平衡为特征的现代奇幻美学，通过受控细节、优雅灯光与高度抛光执行，强调艺术原创性、premium 工艺与当代娱乐艺术感；令人联想到 Marc Simonetti 与阮佳（Ruan Jia），灵感来自《巫师概念艺术》与《万智牌现代大师》。

Cosmic Horror Illustration: unsettling visionary aesthetics characterized by vast incomprehensible scale, intricate organic complexity, surreal symbolism, atmospheric dread, and richly detailed visual storytelling, blending refined craftsmanship with existential mystery through layered textures, immersive depth, dramatic illumination, and a profound sense of the unknown that transcends ordinary reality, reminiscent of H.R. Giger and Zdzisław Beksiński, inspired by At the Mountains of Madness and Bloodborne.
Cosmic Horror Illustration（宇宙恐怖插画）：以 vast 不可理解规模、intricate organic 复杂、surreal 象征、atmospheric dread 与 richly detailed 视觉叙事为特征的 unsettling visionary 美学，通过层叠纹理、沉浸纵深、戏剧性照明与超越平凡现实的未知感，blend 精炼工艺与存在主义神秘；令人联想到 H.R. Giger 与 Zdzisław Beksiński，灵感来自《疯狂山脉》与《血源诅咒》。

Contemporary Commercial Illustration: A polished, versatile illustration style built on clean, confident linework balanced with smooth digital shading calibrated for broad commercial appeal, bright, approachable lighting logic that keeps every composition legible and inviting, a deliberate, market-tested clarity favoring instant communication over artistic ambiguity, meticulous attention to appealing, on-trend rendering finish, and a professional, versatile illustrative polish rooted in contemporary advertising and editorial practice. Influenced by: contemporary commercial-illustration tradition, advertising-illustration technique, editorial-illustration practice, digital-brand illustration method.
Contemporary Commercial Illustration（当代商业插画）：一种抛光、versatile 插画风格，以平衡平滑数字阴影（为广泛商业吸引力校准）的干净、自信线条，保持每构图可读、inviting 的明亮、易接近灯光逻辑，优先瞬时传达而非艺术模糊的 deliberate、market-tested 清晰度，对讨喜、on-trend 渲染完成度的 meticulous 关注，以及根植当代广告与编辑实践的专业、versatile 插画抛光感。受影响于：当代商业插画传统、广告插画技法、编辑插画实践、数字品牌插画方法。

Editorial Illustration: A conceptually driven illustration style built on confident, considered composition designed to communicate a single idea with immediate clarity, restrained shading and color logic that supports rather than overwhelms the central concept, meticulous attention to symbolic or metaphorical visual shorthand, a polished, deadline-tested economy of mark-making that favors clever communication over exhaustive rendering, and a sharp, intelligent illustrative wit rooted in contemporary magazine and publication practice. Influenced by: contemporary editorial-illustration tradition, conceptual magazine-illustration technique, symbolic visual-metaphor practice, publication-illustration method.
Editorial Illustration（编辑插画）：一种概念驱动插画风格，以经设计、为瞬时清晰度传达单一想法的自信、考究构图，支持而非 overwhelm 中心概念的克制阴影与色彩逻辑，对符号或隐喻视觉速记的 meticulous 关注，优先聪明传达而非 exhaustive 渲染的抛光、deadline-tested mark-making 经济，以及根植当代杂志与出版实践的锐利、intelligent 插画机智。受影响于：当代编辑插画传统、概念杂志插画技法、符号视觉隐喻实践、出版插画方法。

Cover Art: highly polished illustration aesthetics designed for immediate visual impact, characterized by strong focal hierarchy, compelling composition, refined rendering, premium surface treatment, and memorable storytelling cues, blending commercial appeal with artistic sophistication through meticulous craftsmanship, dramatic presentation, and collector-quality execution, reminiscent of Drew Struzan and Frank Frazetta, inspired by Star Wars theatrical posters and Conan the Barbarian paperback covers.
Cover Art（封面艺术）：为瞬时视觉冲击设计的高度抛光插画美学，以强焦点层级、compelling 构图、精炼渲染、premium 表面处理与 memorable 叙事线索为特征，通过 meticulous 工艺、戏剧性呈现与收藏级执行，blend 商业吸引力与艺术 sophistication；令人联想到 Drew Struzan 与 Frank Frazetta，灵感来自《星球大战》院线海报与《蛮王柯南》平装封面。

Interplay of Shadow and Light: A dramatically balanced illustration style built entirely on the tension between two forces, with sharply divided planes of illumination and darkness meeting along crisp, deliberate boundaries rather than gradual transition, form constructed almost sculpturally through the push and pull of what is revealed versus what is withheld, a rhythmic alternation of exposed and concealed detail that guides the eye across the composition like a visual heartbeat, a quiet, contemplative gravity carried through the deliberate restraint of full illumination, and a masterful, chiaroscuro-driven intensity where absence carries as much weight as presence. Influenced by: Caravaggio, chiaroscuro painting tradition, dramatic studio-lighting technique, classical tenebrist illustration practice.
Interplay of Shadow and Light（光影互构）：一种完全建于两力张力上的戏剧性平衡插画风格，以 crisp、deliberate 边界而非逐渐过渡 meet 的照明与黑暗 sharp 分割平面，通过 revealed 与 withheld 的推拉几乎雕塑般构建形态，引导眼睛如视觉心跳般跨构图的 exposed 与 concealed 细节 rhythmic 交替，通过 full illumination 的 deliberate 克制携带的轻声、沉思重力，以及 absence 承载与 presence 同等重量的 masterful、chiaroscuro 驱动强度。受影响于：卡拉瓦乔、chiaroscuro 绘画传统、戏剧性影棚灯光技法、古典 tenebrist 插画实践。

Kekai Kotaki Style: A richly atmospheric fantasy concept-art style built on loose, confident brushwork that favors mood and light over crisp detail, dramatic soft-edged lighting that dissolves forms into glowing haze at the peripheries, richly layered painterly texture giving textures a lived-in, tactile weight, sweeping atmospheric depth that lets grand scale breathe through soft value transitions, and an evocative, immersive painterly intensity built for epic fantasy world-building. Influenced by: Kekai Kotaki, Guild Wars 2 concept art, painterly fantasy illustration, atmospheric concept-art technique.,
Kekai Kotaki Style（Kekai Kotaki 风格）：一种 richly atmospheric 奇幻概念艺术风格，以优先情绪与光而非 crisp 细节的松散、自信笔触，在 periphery 将形态 dissolve 为 glowing haze 的戏剧性软边光，赋予纹理 lived-in、tactile 重量的 richly layered 绘画性纹理，让 grand 规模通过柔软明度过渡 breathe 的 sweeping atmospheric 纵深，以及为史诗奇幻世界观构建的 evocative、immersive 绘画性强度。受影响于：Kekai Kotaki、《激战2》概念艺术、绘画性奇幻插画、atmospheric 概念艺术技法。

Robin Eley Style: A photorealistic figurative painting style defined by translucent elments, rendered with uncanny material precision, cool, clinical studio lighting that reveals every fold, wrinkle, and light-refraction in the material textures, skin rendered with hyperreal softness, neutral backgrounds that keep full focus on the interplay between body and translucency, and a quiet, conceptual tension between concealment and exposure rendered with immaculate painterly control. Influenced by: Robin Eley, hyperrealist oil painting, contemporary figurative fine art, photorealism technique.,
Robin Eley Style（Robin Eley 风格）：一种以 translucent 元素定义、以 uncanny 材质精度渲染的照片写实人物绘画风格，揭示每个褶皱、皱纹与材质纹理光折射的 cool、clinical 影棚灯光，以 hyperreal 柔软渲染的皮肤，保持 full focus 于身体与半透明互动的中性背景，以及以 immaculate 绘画性控制渲染的 concealment 与 exposure 间安静、概念张力。受影响于：Robin Eley、hyperrealist 油画、当代人物 fine art、照片写实技法。

Dark Fantasy Illustration: A brooding, atmospheric fantasy painting style built on deep tenebrist shadow that swallows the edges of every composition, a single dramatic source of light carving form out of near-total darkness, richly textured painterly surfaces giving textures a weathered, visceral weight, an oppressive, foreboding stillness charged with looming threat, and a heavy, mythic gravitas that feels both epic and quietly menacing. Influenced by: dark fantasy concept-art tradition, Zdzisław Beksiński, tenebrist oil painting technique, gothic fantasy illustration.,
Dark Fantasy Illustration（黑暗奇幻插画）：一种 brooding、atmospheric 奇幻绘画风格，以吞噬每构图边缘的 deep tenebrist 阴影，从 near-total 黑暗雕刻形态的单一戏剧性光源，赋予纹理 weathered、visceral 重量的 richly textured 绘画性表面，charged looming 威胁的 oppressive、foreboding 静谧，以及既史诗又 quietly menacing 的 heavy、mythic 庄严感。受影响于：黑暗奇幻概念艺术传统、Zdzisław Beksiński、tenebrist 油画技法、哥特奇幻插画。

GTA V Polished Cover Art: A bold, stylized illustration style built on crisp airbrushed rendering with confident graphic outline separating the subject into clean, poster-ready silhouette, saturated high-contrast lighting that punches the subject  forward against a dynamic collage-style arrangement, exaggerated attitude-driven posing charged with kinetic swagger, meticulous costume and prop detail rendered with commercial illustration polish, and a slick, larger-than-life pop-culture cover intensity built for instant, iconic recognition. Influenced by: Stephen Bliss, Rockstar Games box-art tradition, contemporary commercial illustration practice, pop-culture collage cover-design method.
GTA V Polished Cover Art（GTA V 抛光封面艺术）：一种 bold、风格化插画风格，以 crisp 喷枪渲染与自信图形轮廓将主体分隔为干净、海报级剪影，将主体 punch forward 对抗 dynamic collage 式安排的饱和高对比灯光，charged kinetic swagger 的夸张态度驱动姿态，以商业插画抛光渲染的 meticulous 服装与道具细节，以及为瞬时、iconic 辨识而生的 slick、超越现实流行文化封面强度。受影响于：Stephen Bliss、Rockstar Games 盒面艺术传统、当代商业插画实践、流行文化 collage 封面设计方法。

Expressive Character Illustration: A vividly emotive illustration style built on confident, deliberate linework calibrated to amplify feeling over anatomical precision, exaggerated facial and postural modeling that pushes emotional readability to the forefront of every composition, loose, energetic shading applied to reinforce mood rather than achieve full rendering, a spirited, immediate mark-making quality that keeps the piece feeling alive and reactive, and a spontaneous, character-driven intensity that treats emotional truth as the primary compositional goal. Influenced by: contemporary character-design illustration practice, expressive digital-sketch technique, gesture-driven concept-art tradition, animation-adjacent character-study method.
Expressive Character Illustration（表现性角色插画）：一种 vividly emotive 插画风格，以校准为放大感受而非解剖精度的自信、deliberate 线条，将情感可读性推至每构图前沿的夸张面部与姿态建模，为强化情绪而非完整渲染应用的松散、充满能量阴影，保持作品 alive、reactive 的 spirited、即时 mark-making 品质，以及将情感真实视为首要构图目标的自发、角色驱动强度。受影响于：当代角色设计插画实践、表现性数字速写技法、gesture 驱动概念艺术传统、动画邻接角色研究法。

Splatterpunk Horror Illustration: A visceral, unflinching horror-illustration style built on dense, chaotic mark-making that mirrors uncontrolled violence and bodily rupture, harsh, high-contrast rendering that swallows the composition in engulfing darkness broken by sudden, brutal illumination, deliberately crude, raw texture that rejects polish in favor of primal shock, aggressive, fragmented composition charged with confrontational intensity, and a relentless, extreme atmosphere built to overwhelm rather than merely unsettle. Influenced by: splatterpunk horror-fiction illustration tradition, extreme body-horror illustration practice, underground horror-zine technique, visceral pulp-horror rendering method.
Splatterpunk Horror Illustration（血肉朋克恐怖插画）：一种 visceral、不回避的恐怖插画风格，以 mirror 失控暴力与身体 rupture 的 dense、chaotic mark-making，吞噬构图、被 sudden、brutal 照明打破的 harsh、高对比渲染，reject 抛光、优先 primal shock 的 deliberate crude、raw 纹理，charged 对抗性强度的 aggressive、fragmented 构图，以及为 overwhelm 而非 mere unsettle 构建的 relentless、extreme 氛围。受影响于：splatterpunk 恐怖小说插画传统、极端身体恐怖插画实践、地下恐怖 zine 技法、visceral 通俗恐怖渲染方法。

James Jean Style: A richly symbolic, dreamlike illustration style built on flowing, intricate linework that weaves organic pattern seamlessly through composition, delicate translucent washes blending soft color transition with sharply rendered focal detail, a hushed, contemplative surrealism where imagery seems to grow and dissolve into itself, meticulous ornamental density balanced against generous atmospheric space, and a poetic, otherworldly elegance that feels both meticulously crafted and effortlessly dreamlike. Influenced by: James Jean, contemporary illustrative surrealism, ink-and-wash painting technique, gallery-adjacent narrative-illustration practice.
James Jean Style（詹姆斯·简风格）：一种 richly symbolic、dreamlike 插画风格，以 weaving organic 图案无缝 through 构图的 flowing、intricate 线条，blend 柔软色彩过渡与 sharply 渲染焦点细节的精致半透明水洗，似生长并 dissolve 入自身的 imagery 的轻声、沉思超现实主义，与 generous atmospheric 空间平衡的 meticulous 装饰密度，以及既 meticulously crafted 又毫不费力 dreamlike 的诗歌、超凡优雅。受影响于：James Jean、当代插图超现实主义、墨线水洗绘画技法、gallery 邻接叙事插画实践。

Victo Ngai Style: A luminous, decorative illustration style built on richly patterned ornamental linework woven seamlessly through flowing, dreamlike composition, soft glowing color transitions blending seamlessly across intricate layered detail, a hushed, whimsical stillness carried through generous negative space balanced against dense focal ornamentation, meticulous narrative symbolism embedded within every decorative flourish, and a poetic, storybook elegance that feels both meticulously crafted and effortlessly enchanting. Influenced by: Victo Ngai, contemporary editorial-illustration tradition, decorative gouache-painting technique, narrative symbolic-illustration practice.
Victo Ngai Style（Victo Ngai 风格）：一种 luminous、装饰插画风格，以 woven seamlessly through flowing、dreamlike 构图的 richly patterned 装饰线条，跨 intricate 层叠细节无缝 blend 的柔软 glowing 色彩过渡，与 dense 焦点装饰平衡的 generous 负空间携带的轻声、异想静谧，embedded 于每个装饰 flourish 的 meticulous 叙事象征，以及既 meticulously crafted 又毫不费力 enchanting 的诗歌、绘本优雅。受影响于：Victo Ngai、当代编辑插画传统、装饰水粉绘画技法、叙事符号插画实践。

Kay Nielsen Style: An elegant, ornamental fairy-tale illustration style built on sinuous, decorative linework that flows with calligraphic grace, flat decorative patterning applied with meticulous restraint rather than painterly shading, elongated, stylized delicacy, a hushed, dreamlike stillness pervading every composition, and a refined, storybook elegance that feels simultaneously ancient and timeless in its decorative precision. Influenced by: Kay Nielsen, Golden Age children's book illustration, Art Nouveau decorative technique, Scandinavian fairy-tale illustration.
Kay Nielsen Style（Kay Nielsen 风格）：一种优雅、ornamental 童话插画风格，以 calligraphic 优雅 flowing 的 sinuous、装饰线条，以 meticulous 克制而非绘画性阴影应用的 flat 装饰图案，拉长的、风格化 delicacy，弥漫每构图的轻声、dreamlike 静谧，以及同时古老、在其装饰精度中永恒的精炼、绘本优雅。受影响于：Kay Nielsen、黄金时代童书插画、新艺术运动装饰技法、斯堪的纳维亚童话插画。

Hyper-Stylized Digital Painting: A boldly exaggerated painterly illustration style built on confident distortion of proportion pushed far beyond naturalistic reference, rich, layered brushwork rendering dramatically simplified or elongated form with total painterly conviction, saturated tonal contrast applied with deliberate, controlled boldness rather than restraint, an assertive, larger-than-life visual rhythm carried through exaggerated posing and dynamic composition, and a striking, unapologetically stylized painterly intensity that treats distortion itself as the core creative statement. Influenced by: contemporary stylized digital-painting tradition, exaggerated-proportion character-illustration technique, bold painterly concept-art practice, confident stylization-driven rendering method.
Hyper-Stylized Digital Painting（超风格化数字绘画）：一种 boldly 夸张的绘画性插画风格，以推远超出自然参考的自信比例扭曲，以 total 绘画性信念渲染 dramatically 简化或拉长形态的 rich、层叠笔触，以 deliberate、受控 boldness 而非克制应用的饱和色调对比，通过夸张姿态与动态构图携带的 assertive、超越现实视觉节奏，以及将扭曲本身视为核心创作陈述的 striking、毫不歉疚风格化绘画性强度。受影响于：当代风格化数字绘画传统、夸张比例角色插画技法、bold 绘画性概念艺术实践、自信风格化驱动渲染方法。

Movie Digital art: A bold, high-octane action-movie poster illustration style built on dramatic low-angle framing that conveys raw power and momentum, intensely saturated directional lighting carving sharp, aggressive contrast across confident, larger-than-life composition, dynamic diagonal energy suggesting explosive forward motion even in stillness, meticulous photoreal rendering blended with graphic poster-ready boldness, and a bold, adrenaline-charged blockbuster intensity built for instant, high-impact visual spectacle. Influenced by: contemporary action-movie poster-illustration tradition, cinematic key-art rendering technique, high-octane blockbuster marketing practice, dynamic photoreal-composite illustration method.
Movie Digital art（电影数字艺术）：一种 bold、high-octane 动作电影海报插画风格，以传达 raw 力量与 momentum 的戏剧性低角度取景，在自信、超越现实构图 carving sharp、aggressive 对比的 intensely 饱和方向光，即便静止也 suggest 爆炸向前运动的 dynamic 对角能量，blend 精细照片写实与图形海报级 boldness 的 meticulous 渲染，以及为瞬时、高冲击视觉 spectacle 而生的 bold、adrenaline-charged 大片强度。受影响于：当代动作电影海报插画传统、电影主视觉渲染技法、high-octane 大片营销实践、动态照片合成插画方法。

RossDraws Style: An energetic, glowing painterly illustration style built on bold, confident brushwork radiating vibrant internal luminosity, a dynamic push-and-pull between crisp focal rendering and loose, expressive atmospheric edges, gentle bloom and glow accents scattered across the composition for lively visual rhythm, an upbeat, spontaneous energy carried through confident gestural mark-making, and a warm, charismatic painterly polish that feels enthusiastic, immediate, and effortlessly appealing. Influenced by: RossDraws, painterly digital-illustration technique, glow-driven character-illustration practice, contemporary streaming-artist rendering method.
RossDraws Style（RossDraws 风格）：一种充满能量、glowing 绘画性插画风格，以 radiating vibrant 内部发光的 bold、自信笔触，crisp 焦点渲染与松散、表现性 atmospheric 边缘间的 dynamic push-and-pull，为 lively 视觉节奏散布构图的 gentle bloom 与 glow accent，通过自信 gestural mark-making 携带的 upbeat、自发能量，以及 warm、charismatic、热情、即时、毫不费力吸引人的绘画性抛光感。受影响于：RossDraws、绘画性数字插画技法、glow 驱动角色插画实践、当代 streamer 艺术家渲染方法。

Loish Ultrarealism: A luminous, painterly digital illustration style blending soft ultrarealistic rendering with expressive, loosely gestural brushwork at the edges, richly glowing skin built through translucent layered glazing rather than flat shading, delicate visible brushstrokes preserved even within highly refined facial detail, a dreamy, romantic softness radiating from confident directional lighting, and a warm, emotionally intimate polish that balances technical realism with unmistakably hand-painted feeling. Influenced by: Loish (Lois van Baarle), digital gouache painting, painterly portrait illustration, semi-realistic character art.
Loish Ultrarealism（Loish 超写实）：一种 luminous、painterly 数字插画风格，blend 柔软 ultrarealistic 渲染与边缘的 expressive、松散 gestural 笔触，通过半透明层叠 glazing 而非 flat 阴影构建的 richly glowing 皮肤，即便高度精炼面部细节也保留的精致可见笔触，从自信方向光 radiating 的 dreamy、romantic 柔软，以及平衡技术写实与毋庸置疑手绘感的 warm、情感亲密抛光。受影响于：Loish（Lois van Baarle）、数字水粉绘画、painterly 肖像插画、半写实角色艺术。

J.C. Leyendecker Style: A crisp, confident golden-age illustration style built on bold, brushy strokes that define form with economical precision rather than exhaustive detail, richly modeled rendering through sweeping tonal transition that suggests weight and movement, idealized, statuesque figures posed with theatrical poise and confident presence, a smooth, glowing painterly light that wraps forms in graduated highlight, and a polished, commercial-art elegance that feels both classical and unmistakably iconic. Influenced by: J.C. Leyendecker, Golden Age American illustration, oil-painting technique, classic advertising-illustration practice.
J.C. Leyendecker Style（J.C. Leyendecker 风格）：一种 crisp、自信黄金时代插画风格，以经经济精度而非 exhaustive 细节定义形态的 bold、brushy strokes，通过 sweeping 色调过渡 suggest 重量与运动的 richly modeled 渲染，以戏剧性 poise 与自信存在 posed 的理想化、statuesque 人物，wrap 形态于 graduated 高光的平滑、glowing 绘画性光，以及既古典又毋庸置疑 iconic 的抛光、商业艺术优雅。受影响于：J.C. Leyendecker、黄金时代美式插画、油画技法、经典广告插画实践。

Ismail Inceoglu Style: A moody, luminous digital-painting style built on soft glowing directional light that carves powerful, sculptural dimensionality against enveloping darkness, richly layered painterly brushwork blending into smooth atmospheric transition, dramatic anatomical grounding rendered with confident, almost hyperreal muscular tension, a hushed, cinematic intensity carried through restrained, dramatic contrast, and a technically masterful, contemporary painterly gravitas that feels both epic and intimately grounded. Influenced by: Ismail Inceoglu, contemporary digital-painting technique, cinematic figurative-illustration practice, dramatic chiaroscuro rendering method.
Ismail Inceoglu Style（Ismail Inceoglu 风格）：一种 moody、luminous 数字绘画风格，以在 enveloping 黑暗雕刻强大、雕塑性维度的柔软 glowing 方向光，blend 入平滑 atmospheric 过渡的 richly layered 绘画性笔触，以自信、almost hyperreal 肌肉张力的戏剧性解剖基础渲染，通过克制、戏剧性对比携带的轻声、电影感强度，以及既史诗又 intimately grounded 的技术 masterful、当代绘画性庄严感。受影响于：Ismail Inceoglu、当代数字绘画技法、电影感人物插画实践、戏剧性 chiaroscuro 渲染方法。

Faceted Painterly Digital painting: A confidently stylized digital-painting style built on sharp, angular brushwork that constructs form through faceted, geometric planes of light and shadow rather than smooth blending, crisp specular highlight catching every sharp transitional edge with glossy precision, a soft-to-graphic tension between rendered texture and boldly simplified surrounding shape, meticulous attention to expressive, softly rendered detail set against otherwise confident graphic construction, and a polished, editorial-illustration finish that feels contemporary, stylish, and effortlessly striking. Influenced by: contemporary faceted digital-painting technique, semi-realistic editorial-illustration practice, stylized rendering method, painterly geometric-shading tradition.
Faceted Painterly Digital painting（棱面绘画性数字绘画）：一种自信风格化数字绘画风格，以通过 faceted、几何光暗平面而非平滑混合构建形态的 sharp、棱角笔触，以 glossy 精度 catch 每个 sharp 过渡边的 crisp 高光，渲染纹理与 boldly 简化周围形状间的 soft-to-graphic 张力，与否则自信图形构建对比的 expressive、柔软渲染细节 meticulous 关注，以及当代、stylish、毫不费力 striking 的抛光、编辑插画完成度。受影响于：当代 faceted 数字绘画技法、半写实编辑插画实践、风格化渲染方法、painterly 几何阴影传统。

Sharp-Lined Glossy Semi-Realistic Portrait: A striking digital-illustration style built on confident, angular linework combined with smooth airbrushed shading, glassy, high-shine specular highlight concentrated in the eyes and lips for immediate emotional focus, meticulous attention to soft luminous rendering contrasted against crisp, decisive contour, a hushed, contemplative intimacy carried through close, direct framing, and a polished, contemporary character-illustration intensity that feels both painterly and graphically sharp. Influenced by: Ilya Kuvshinov, WLOP, Guweiz, semi-realistic anime-portrait rendering technique.
Sharp-Lined Glossy Semi-Realistic Portrait（锐线光泽半写实肖像）：一种 striking 数字插画风格，以自信、棱角线条 combined 平滑喷枪阴影，集中于眼睛与嘴唇、为即时情感焦点的 glassy、high-shine 高光，与 crisp、decisive 轮廓对比的柔软 luminous 渲染 meticulous 关注，通过 close、direct 取景携带的轻声、沉思亲密，以及既 painterly 又 graphically sharp 的抛光、当代角色插画强度。受影响于：Ilya Kuvshinov、WLOP、Guweiz、半写实动漫肖像渲染技法。

Aleksi Briclot and Sparth-Inspired Art: A moody, cinematic concept-illustration style built on dramatic low-key lighting that carves powerful form out of dense, atmospheric shadow, richly textured painterly rendering giving costume and structure convincing weight balanced against sleek, architectural minimalism in the surrounding composition, dynamic, foreshortened posing charged with kinetic tension set against calm, monumental spatial restraint, meticulous material detail contrasted with soft, moody atmospheric dissolution at the edges, and a polished, epic-scale intensity built for high-stakes fantasy and sci-fi key-art. Influenced by: Aleksi Briclot, Sparth (Nicolas Bouvier), painterly concept-art technique, cinematic key-art composition practice.
Aleksi Briclot and Sparth-Inspired Art（Aleksi Briclot 与 Sparth 启发艺术）：一种 moody、电影感概念插画风格，以从 dense、atmospheric 阴影雕刻强大形态的戏剧性低_key 灯光，richly textured 绘画性渲染赋予服装与结构可信重量、与周围构图 sleek、建筑极简主义平衡，与 calm、monumental 空间克制对比、charged kinetic 张力的动态、foreshortened 姿态，与边缘 soft、moody atmospheric dissolution 对比的 meticulous 材质细节，以及为 high-stakes 奇幻与科幻主视觉而生的抛光、史诗规模强度。受影响于：Aleksi Briclot、Sparth（Nicolas Bouvier）、painterly 概念艺术技法、电影主视觉构图实践。

Marvel Rivals Character Design (Painterly Comic Illustration): A polished, high-production character-illustration style built on confident, digital-art brushstrokes that feels hand-made, small impact-driven imperfections conveying dynamism and energy, sharp digital linework merged with dense, painterly cel-to-gradient shading, dramatic effects rendered with crisp graphic linework against soft ambient glow,  clean, readable heroic silhouette, Influenced by: Marvel Rivals, NetEase Games character-art direction, painterly hero-shooter illustration technique, cinematic key-art composition practice, contemporary superhero-game character-design method.
Marvel Rivals Character Design (Painterly Comic Illustration)（漫威争锋角色设计·绘画性漫画插画）：一种抛光、高制作角色插画风格，以感 hand-made 的自信、数字艺术笔触，convey 动态与能量的小 impact-driven 不完美，blend 密集、painterly 赛璐珞到渐变阴影的 sharp 数字线条，与柔软 ambient glow 对比、以 crisp 图形线条渲染的戏剧性效果，干净、可读英雄剪影。受影响于：漫威争锋、网易游戏角色艺术指导、painterly hero-shooter 插画技法、电影主视觉构图实践、当代超英游戏角色设计方法。

Crisp Semi-Realistic Anime Illustration: A polished, glossy digital-illustration style built on confident, clean linework merged seamlessly with smooth cel-to-gradient shading, luminous semi-realistic skin rendering with sharp specular highlight concentrated in the eyes for immediate emotional focus, meticulous attention to reflective glassy detail rendered with crisp precision, a bright, high-key illumination logic keeping the whole composition feeling vivid and immediate, and a vibrant, contemporary character-illustration polish that feels editorial, lively, and effortlessly appealing. Influenced by: Ilya Kuvshinov, WLOP, semi-realistic anime-portrait rendering technique, contemporary gacha-adjacent character-illustration practice.
Crisp Semi-Realistic Anime Illustration（清脆半写实动漫插画）：一种抛光、glossy 数字插画风格，以无缝 merged 平滑赛璐珞到渐变阴影的自信、干净线条，集中于眼睛、为即时情感焦点的 sharp 高光 luminous 半写实皮肤渲染，以 crisp 精度渲染的反射 glassy 细节 meticulous 关注，保持整个构图 vivid、即时的明亮、高_key 照明逻辑，以及编辑、lively、毫不费力吸引人的 vibrant、当代角色插画抛光感。受影响于：Ilya Kuvshinov、WLOP、半写实动漫肖像渲染技法、当代 gacha 邻接角色插画实践。

ArtStation Masterpiece: A high-production concept-art illustration style built on meticulously controlled rendering that balances painterly atmosphere with technical precision, dramatic cinematic lighting establishing mood and scale within a single portfolio-ready composition, richly detailed material and textural nuance rewarding close inspection without sacrificing overall painterly cohesion, confident, ambitious compositional scale calibrated for maximum visual impact at first glance, and a polished, industry-grade rendering intensity built to command attention within a competitive professional showcase. Influenced by: contemporary ArtStation concept-art community tradition, painterly digital-illustration technique, industry-standard portfolio-rendering practice, cinematic concept-art composition method.
ArtStation Masterpiece（ArtStation 杰作·其二）：一种高制作概念艺术插画风格，以平衡绘画性氛围与技术精度的 meticulous 受控渲染，在单一 portfolio-ready 构图建立情绪与规模的戏剧性电影光，值得近观而不牺牲整体绘画性 cohesion 的 richly detailed 材质与纹理微妙，为第一眼最大视觉冲击校准的自信、ambitious 构图规模，以及为在竞争性专业展示中 command 注意而生的抛光、industry-grade 渲染强度。受影响于：当代 ArtStation 概念艺术社区传统、绘画性数字插画技法、industry-standard portfolio 渲染实践、电影感概念艺术构图方法。

Glossy Semi-Realistic: A striking digital-illustration style built on confident, clean digital-gradient-linework combined with extremely smooth airbrushed shading, very glassy specular highlights for immediate emotional focus, meticulous attention to soft luminous rendering contrasted against crisp, decisive contour, a hushed, moody intimacy carried through close, direct framing, and a polished, contemporary illustration intensity that feels both delicate and graphically sharp. Influenced by: Sakimichan,  Ilya Kuvshinov, WLOP, semi-realistic anime rendering technique, contemporary gacha-adjacent illustration practice.  ray-tracing-adjacent aesthetics,
Glossy Semi-Realistic（光泽半写实）：一种 striking 数字插画风格，以自信、干净数字渐变线条 combined 极平滑喷枪阴影，为即时情感焦点的 very glassy 高光，与 crisp、decisive 轮廓对比的柔软 luminous 渲染 meticulous 关注，通过 close、direct 取景携带的轻声、moody 亲密，以及既 delicate 又 graphically sharp 的抛光、当代插画强度。受影响于：Sakimichan、Ilya Kuvshinov、WLOP、半写实动漫渲染技法、当代 gacha 邻接插画实践、ray-tracing 邻接美学。

Ilya Kuvshinov Style: A soft, luminous anime-realism illustration style built on delicately blended airbrushed shading that gives skin a gentle glowing translucency, fine detailed linework reserved for hair and eyes while broader forms stay softly rendered, a hushed, contemplative stillness carried through gentle ambient lighting, subtle painterly texture in hair strands contrasted against smooth porcelain-like skin, and a quietly elegant, understated polish that feels intimate, modern, and effortlessly refined. Influenced by: Ilya Kuvshinov, modern anime-realism illustration, digital airbrush technique, semi-realistic character art.
Ilya Kuvshinov Style（Ilya Kuvshinov 风格）：一种柔软、luminous 动漫写实插画风格，以赋予皮肤温柔 glowing 半透明的 delicately blended 喷枪阴影，保留给头发与眼睛的细细节线条、而 broader 形态保持柔软渲染，通过温柔 ambient 光携带的轻声、沉思静谧，与平滑 porcelain 般皮肤对比的发丝 subtle painterly 纹理，以及 intimate、modern、毫不费力精炼的 quietly 优雅、understated 抛光感。受影响于：Ilya Kuvshinov、现代动漫写实插画、数字喷枪技法、半写实角色艺术。

Luminous Pastel: A softly radiant illustration style built on gentle, powdery tonal layering that blends effortlessly at every transition, a diffused, glowing luminosity emerging from within rather than reflecting off the exterior, delicate, unhurried blending replacing any sharp contrast or hard edge, a hushed, tender softness carried through subtle textural graininess reminiscent of layered chalk, and a quietly graceful, dreamlike gentleness that feels intimate, delicate, and effortlessly warm. Influenced by: pastel painting tradition, soft-chalk blending technique, classical photographic drawing practice, contemporary digital pastel-rendering method.
Luminous Pastel（发光粉彩）：一种柔软 radiant 插画风格，以在每个过渡 effortlessly blend 的温柔、powdery 色调层叠，从内而非外反射的 diffused、glowing 发光，取代任何 sharp 对比或硬边的 delicate、从容混合，remind 层叠粉笔的 subtle 纹理 graininess 携带的轻声、tender 柔软，以及 intimate、delicate、毫不费力温暖的 quietly 优雅、dreamlike 温柔感。受影响于：粉彩绘画传统、软粉笔混合技法、古典摄影绘画实践、当代数字粉彩渲染方法。
