Melancholic Hyperrealism: A quietly haunting hyperrealist illustration style built on obsessive photographic precision applied with restrained, contemplative sensitivity rather than clinical detachment, soft, muted directional light casting long, wistful shadow across meticulously rendered form, a hushed, introspective stillness carried through subtle, unhurried tonal transition, painstaking attention to subtle emotional nuance in expression rendered with the same devotion as physical detail, and a technically masterful yet deeply tender intensity that treats sorrow itself as worthy of meticulous, patient craft. Influenced by: hyperrealist oil-painting technique, contemporary photorealism tradition, emotionally driven fine-art portraiture practice, melancholic figurative-painting method.
Melancholic Hyperrealism（忧郁超写实）：一种 quietly haunting 超写实插画风格，以克制、沉思敏感性而非临床超然应用的偏执照片精度，在 meticulously 渲染形态投下 long、wistful 阴影的柔软、muted 方向光，通过 subtle、从容色调过渡携带的轻声、内省静谧，以与物理细节同等 devotion 渲染表情微妙情感 nuance 的 painstaking 关注，以及将悲伤本身视为值得 meticulous、耐心工艺的技术 masterful 却 deeply tender 强度。受影响于：hyperrealist 油画技法、当代照片写实传统、情感驱动 fine-art 肖像实践、忧郁人物绘画方法。

MTG (Magic: The Gathering) Style: A richly painterly fantasy card-illustration style built on confident traditional-media brushwork rendered with meticulous narrative clarity, dramatic atmospheric lighting that establishes mood and magical energy within a single evocative frame, richly textured surface detail across textures, and environment rendered with tactile painterly weight, a wide range of stylistic voices unified by strong storytelling composition and evocative world-building intensity, and a polished, collectible-card-art gravitas built to spark imagination within a small frame. Influenced by: Rebecca Guay, Wizards of the Coast card-art tradition, painterly fantasy illustration technique, narrative concept-art composition.
MTG (Magic: The Gathering) Style（万智牌风格）：一种 richly painterly 奇幻卡牌插画风格，以 meticulous 叙事清晰度渲染的自信传统媒介笔触，在单一 evocative 画幅建立情绪与魔法能量的戏剧性 atmospheric 光，跨纹理的 richly textured 表面细节，以及以 tactile 绘画性重量渲染的环境，由强叙事构图与 evocative 世界观构建强度统一的 wide range 风格 voice，以及为在小画幅 spark 想象而生的抛光、收藏卡牌艺术庄严感。受影响由：Rebecca Guay、威世智卡牌艺术传统、painterly 奇幻插画技法、叙事概念艺术构图。

Tom Bagshaw-Inspired Digital Painting: A hauntingly elegant digital painting style built on desaturated color palettes broken by a single striking accent hue, porcelain-pale skin rendered with soft painterly translucency, richly detailed fabric, rendered in fine contrasting detail against loosely blurred or minimal backgrounds, subjects posed with a still, melancholic, intimacy, and a delicate, painterly restraint that favors quiet mood and texture over dramatic action. Influenced by: Tom Bagshaw,  digital matte painting technique, muted chiaroscuro illustration.
Tom Bagshaw-Inspired Digital Painting（Tom Bagshaw 启发数字绘画）：一种 hauntingly 优雅数字绘画风格，以被单一 striking accent hue 打破的去饱和配色，以柔软 painterly 半透明渲染的 porcelain-pale 皮肤，与松散模糊或极简背景对比精细渲染的 richly detailed 织物，以 still、melancholic、intimacy posed 的主体，以及优先 quiet 情绪与纹理而非戏剧动作的 delicate、painterly 克制。受影响于：Tom Bagshaw、数字接景绘画技法、muted chiaroscuro 插画。

Action Movie Poster with Diagonal Speed Strokes: A composition built on aggressive diagonal motion streaks slashing behind the subject,  the strokes applied with a dry, fractured drag that skips across the canvas creating an explosive backlit burst of velocity and impact, the subject rendered with razor-sharp clarity and cinematic rim lighting while the surrounding strokes dissolve into raw kinetic abstraction, the atmosphere charged with high-stakes intensity, the contrast between the hyper-detailed subject and the chaotic streaked background amplifying the sense of motion, power, and larger-than-life heroism. Influenced by: Drew Struzan's illustrated movie poster compositions, the fractured-speed aesthetic of Marvel Studios key art, Saul Bass's dynamic diagonal poster design, Hollywood blockbuster marketing,
Action Movie Poster with Diagonal Speed Strokes（对角速度笔触动作电影海报）：一种以 aggressive 对角运动 streak slashing 主体后构建的构图，以 dry、fractured drag 应用、skip across 画布的 strokes 创作 velocity 与 impact 的 explosive 逆光爆发，以 razor-sharp 清晰度与电影边缘光渲染主体、而周围 strokes dissolve 为 raw kinetic 抽象，charged high-stakes 强度的氛围，hyper-detailed 主体与 chaotic streaked 背景的对比放大运动、力量与超越现实英雄感。受影响于：Drew Struzan 的插图电影海报构图、漫威影业主视觉的 fractured-speed 美学、Saul Bass 的动态对角海报设计、好莱坞大片营销。

Painting: A richly layered illustration style built on confident brushwork that constructs form through visible, considered strokes rather than flat mechanical fill, blended tonal transition achieved through layered pigment application, a tactile, hand-crafted texture that preserves the character of the medium even within refined rendering, deliberate value and contrast control that gives form convincing dimensional weight, and a timeless, painterly sincerity rooted in foundational fine-art tradition. Influenced by: traditional oil-painting tradition, classical atelier technique, painterly fine-art practice, foundational tonal-rendering method.
Painting（绘画）：一种 richly layered 插画风格，以通过可见、考究 strokes 而非 flat 机械填充构建形态的自信笔触，通过层叠颜料应用实现的 blended 色调过渡，即便精炼渲染也保留媒介特性的 tactile、手作纹理，赋予形态可信维度重量的 deliberate 明度与对比控制，以及根植基础 fine-art 传统的永恒、painterly 真诚感。受影响于：传统油画传统、古典 atelier 技法、painterly fine-art 实践、基础色调渲染方法。

Abstract Expressionism: A raw, gestural painting style built on sweeping, spontaneous brushwork that prioritizes emotional intensity over representational accuracy, bold physical mark-making where the visible energy of the stroke becomes the subject itself, dramatic tonal contrast and dense layered texture built through accumulated, unrestrained applications of paint, a chaotic yet deliberate compositional freedom that resists conventional structure, and a raw, cathartic painterly intensity that channels pure emotional immediacy over polished technique. Influenced by: Jackson Pollock, Willem de Kooning, action painting technique, mid-century American painting movement.
Abstract Expressionism（抽象表现主义）：一种 raw、gestural 绘画风格，以优先情感强度而非具象准确的 sweeping、自发笔触，可见 stroke 能量本身成为主题的 bold 物理 mark-making，通过 accumulated、unrestrained 颜料应用构建的戏剧性色调对比与 dense 层叠纹理，resist 常规结构的 chaotic 却 deliberate 构图自由，以及 channel 纯粹情感即时性而非抛光技法的 raw、cathartic painterly 强度。受影响于：Jackson Pollock、Willem de Kooning、行动绘画技法、中叶世纪美式绘画运动。

Academic Precision Realism: A meticulously rendered academic-realist illustration style built on immaculate, glass-smooth surface finish achieved through countless thin layered glazes rather than visible brushwork, dramatic architectural lighting that rakes across richly detailed ornamental surface and fabric with near-photographic exactness, a controlled, studio-disciplined precision in every fold, texture, and reflective highlight, a hushed, contemplative grandeur carried through carefully staged compositional balance, and a polished, old-world craftsmanship that treats technical mastery itself as the subject. Influenced by: William-Adolphe Bouguereau, academic realism painting tradition, oil-glazing technique, classical atelier training method.
Academic Precision Realism（学院派精确写实）：一种 meticulously 渲染的学院派写实插画风格，以通过 countless 薄层 glaze 而非可见笔触实现的 immaculate、glass-smooth 表面 finish，以近照片精度 rake across richly detailed 装饰表面与织物的戏剧性建筑光，每个褶皱、纹理与反射高光的受控、影棚纪律精度，通过 carefully staged 构图平衡携带的轻声、沉思宏伟，以及将技术 mastery 本身视为主题的抛光、old-world 工艺感。受影响于：William-Adolphe Bouguereau、学院派写实绘画传统、油画 glazing 技法、古典 atelier 训练方法。

Acrylic Dreamscape: expressive painterly rendering with layered acrylic textures, blended edges, atmospheric transitions, rich surface variation, handcrafted brushwork, contemporary fine art sensibilities, dreamlike visual rhythm, softly abstracted realism, tactile material presence, luminous depth, gallery-quality execution, reminiscent of Gerhard Richter and Jeremy Mann, inspired by Abstract Painting and Contemporary Fine Art Exhibitions.
Acrylic Dreamscape（丙烯梦境）：以层叠丙烯纹理、blended 边缘、atmospheric 过渡、rich 表面变化、handcrafted 笔触、当代 fine art 感、dreamlike 视觉节奏、softly abstracted 写实、tactile 材质存在、luminous 纵深、gallery-quality 执行为特征的 expressive painterly 渲染；令人联想到 Gerhard Richter 与 Jeremy Mann，灵感来自《抽象绘画》与《当代 fine art 展览》。

Adventure Illustration: dynamic narrative artwork emphasizing strong visual storytelling, confident draftsmanship, expressive action, atmospheric depth, adventurous mood, detailed craftsmanship, readable composition, classic illustration traditions, polished rendering, and timeless sense of exploration, reminiscent of N.C. Wyeth and Howard Pyle, inspired by Treasure Island and The Adventures of Robin Hood.
Adventure Illustration（冒险插画）：强调强视觉叙事、自信造型、表现性动作、atmospheric 纵深、冒险情绪、detailed 工艺、可读构图、经典插画传统、抛光渲染与永恒探索感的 dynamic 叙事艺术；令人联想到 N.C. Wyeth 与 Howard Pyle，灵感来自《金银岛》与《罗宾汉历险记》。

Agnes Cecile Style: A delicately dissolving watercolor illustration style built on translucent washes that bleed and diffuse outward from a sharply rendered focal point, a deliberate contrast between crisp, precise detail at the center and loose, dripping fluidity toward the edges, ethereal fragmented form that seems to be dissolving into or emerging from pure pigment, a hushed, melancholic tenderness carried through soft tonal gradation, and a poetic, emotionally raw fragility that treats controlled dissolution as the compositional heart of the piece. Influenced by: Agnes Cecile, watercolor-and-ink illustration technique, contemporary fine-art dissolution practice, expressive wet-media portraiture method.
Agnes Cecile Style（Agnes Cecile 风格）：一种 delicately dissolving 水彩插画风格，以从 sharply 渲染焦点 outward bleed、diffuse 的半透明水洗，中心 crisp、precise 细节与边缘松散、dripping 流体间的 deliberate 对比，似 dissolving into 或 emerging from 纯颜料的 ethereal fragmented 形态，通过柔软色调渐变携带的轻声、melancholic tenderness，以及将受控 dissolution 视为作品构图核心的诗歌、情感 raw fragility。受影响于：Agnes Cecile、水彩墨线插画技法、当代 fine-art dissolution 实践、表现性湿媒介肖像方法。

Airbrush Fantasy: highly polished fantasy illustration featuring exceptionally smooth gradients, refined atmospheric rendering, soft transitions, luminous depth, meticulous surface treatment, commercial illustration sensibilities, elegant visual flow, and a perfected painterly finish that emphasizes clarity and visual appeal, reminiscent of Boris Vallejo and Chris Achilleos, inspired by Heavy Metal Magazine and classic fantasy paperback cover art.
Airbrush Fantasy（喷枪奇幻）：以 exceptionally 平滑渐变、精炼 atmospheric 渲染、柔软过渡、luminous 纵深、meticulous 表面处理、商业插画感、优雅视觉 flow 与强调清晰度与视觉吸引力的 perfected painterly finish 为特征的高度抛光奇幻插画；令人联想到 Boris Vallejo 与 Chris Achilleos，灵感来自《Heavy Metal》杂志与经典奇幻平装封面艺术。

Van Gogh Style: An intensely expressive painting style built on thick, visible impasto brushstrokes applied with rhythmic, almost vibrating directional energy, dense, swirling mark-making that gives even still forms a sense of restless movement, confident, emotionally charged tonal contrast built through accumulated layered strokes rather than smooth blending, a raw, unrestrained painterly intensity where the physical gesture of the brush becomes inseparable from the emotion depicted, and a passionate, deeply personal painterly urgency rooted in late-19th-century post-impressionist rebellion. Influenced by: Vincent van Gogh, post-impressionist painting tradition, impasto brushwork technique, expressive plein-air painting practice.
Van Gogh Style（梵高风格）：一种 intensely expressive 绘画风格，以 rhythmic、almost vibrating 方向能量应用的 thick、可见 impasto 笔触，赋予即便静止形态 restless 运动感的 dense、swirling mark-making，通过 accumulated 层叠 strokes 而非平滑混合构建的自信、情感 charge 色调对比，物理笔触 gesture 与所描绘情感 inseparable 的 raw、unrestrained painterly 强度，以及根植 19 世纪末后印象派反叛的 passionate、deeply personal painterly urgency。受影响于：Vincent van Gogh、后印象派绘画传统、impasto 笔触技法、表现性 plein-air 绘画实践。

Monet Style: A luminous, atmospheric painting style built on loose, broken brushstrokes that dissolve precise form in favor of captured light and momentary sensation, delicate layered dabs of pigment that blend optically at a distance rather than through physical mixing, a soft, hazy compositional atmosphere that prioritizes fleeting impression over structural clarity, an unhurried, contemplative rhythm carried through repeated, patient mark-making, and a tranquil, light-drenched painterly sincerity rooted in plein-air impressionist observation. Influenced by: Claude Monet, French impressionist painting tradition, plein-air painting technique, broken-color brushwork practice.
Monet Style（莫奈风格）：一种 luminous、atmospheric 绘画风格，以 dissolve 精确形态、优先捕捉光与瞬间感觉的松散、broken 笔触，在距离 optical 而非物理混合的 delicate 层叠颜料 dabs，优先 fleeting impression 而非结构清晰度的柔软、hazy 构图氛围，通过 repeated、patient mark-making 携带的从容、沉思节奏，以及根植 plein-air 印象派观察的 tranquil、light-drenched painterly 真诚感。受影响于：Claude Monet、法国印象派绘画传统、plein-air 绘画技法、broken-color 笔触实践。

Wet-on-Wet Watercolor Ink Painting: A fluid, unpredictable painting style built on pigment applied directly into an already-saturated wet layer, allowing color and tone to bloom, bleed, and diffuse with organic unpredictability rather than controlled placement, soft, feathered edges replacing any crisp boundary as washes merge and blend spontaneously, a delicate, translucent layering that builds depth through repeated wet passes rather than opaque coverage, an unhurried, meditative looseness carried through the medium's inherent unpredictability, and a poetic, ephemeral fluidity that captures the beauty of controlled chance. Influenced by: traditional wet-on-wet watercolor technique, ink-wash painting tradition, expressive fluid-media practice, spontaneous wash-blending method.
Wet-on-Wet Watercolor Ink Painting（湿接湿水彩墨画）：一种 fluid、不可预测的绘画风格，以直接 applied 入已 saturated 湿层的颜料，让色彩与色调以 organic 不可预测性 bloom、bleed、diffuse 而非受控放置，水洗 merge、spontaneously blend 时取代任何 crisp 边界的柔软、feathered 边缘，通过 repeated wet pass 而非不透明覆盖构建纵深的 delicate、半透明层叠，通过媒介固有不可预测性携带的从容、冥想松散，以及捕捉受控偶然之美的诗歌、ephemeral fluidity。受影响于：传统湿接湿水彩技法、墨线水洗传统、表现性 fluid-media 实践、自发水洗混合方法。

Alphonse Mucha Masterwork: elegant decorative illustration characterized by flowing organic linework, intricate ornamental detailing, harmonious visual rhythms, refined craftsmanship, graceful contours, and highly polished surface treatment, blending fine art sophistication with graphic clarity and a strong sense of decorative unity, reminiscent of Alphonse Mucha and Gustav Klimt, inspired by Job Cigarettes Poster and The Slav Epic.
Alphonse Mucha Masterwork（阿尔丰斯·慕夏杰作）：以 flowing organic 线条、intricate 装饰细节、harmonious 视觉节奏、精炼工艺、graceful 轮廓与高度抛光表面处理为特征的优雅装饰插画，blend fine art sophistication 与图形清晰度及强装饰统一感；令人联想到 Alphonse Mucha 与 Gustav Klimt，灵感来自《Job 香烟海报》与《斯拉夫史诗》。

Anato Finnstark Style: An epic, luminous fantasy-portrait style built on sweeping atmospheric glow that radiates outward from a central focal point, richly layered painterly brushwork dissolving into soft luminous haze at the periphery, dramatic scale conveyed through radiant backlight that illuminates simultaneously, meticulous attention to soft motion caught within the glow, and a breathtaking, cinematic grandeur that feels both intimate and monumentally epic. Influenced by: Anato Finnstark, painterly fantasy-illustration technique, luminous atmospheric concept-art practice, cinematic key-art lighting method.
Anato Finnstark Style（Anato Finnstark 风格）：一种 epic、luminous 奇幻肖像风格，以从中心焦点 outward radiating 的 sweeping atmospheric glow，在 periphery dissolve 为柔软 luminous haze 的 richly layered painterly 笔触，simultaneously illuminate 的 radiant 逆光传达的戏剧性规模，对 glow 内 captured 柔软运动的 meticulous 关注，以及既 intimate 又 monumentally epic 的 breathtaking、电影感宏伟。受影响于：Anato Finnstark、painterly 奇幻插画技法、luminous atmospheric 概念艺术实践、电影主视觉灯光方法。

Rembrandt Painting: A masterfully controlled classical painting style built on single-source directional light carving a small, dignified highlight against an otherwise deeply shadowed composition, rich, warm chiaroscuro tonal harmony bathing every plane in umber and near-black depth, thick, confident impasto brushwork in the illuminated passages contrasted against thinly glazed shadow, a quiet, contemplative gravitas carried through restrained, dignified composition, and an old-master intensity rooted in centuries of masterful oil-painting tradition. Influenced by: Rembrandt van Rijn, Dutch Golden Age painting tradition, chiaroscuro oil-painting technique, classical impasto-and-glazing method.
Rembrandt Painting（伦勃朗绘画）：一种 masterfully 受控古典绘画风格，以单一方向光在 otherwise deeply shadowed 构图雕刻 small、dignified 高光，以 umber 与近黑纵深沐浴每个平面的 rich、warm chiaroscuro 色调和谐， illuminated 段 thick、自信 impasto 笔触对比 thinly glazed 阴影，通过克制、dignified 构图携带的轻声、沉思庄严，以及根植数世纪 masterful 油画传统的 old-master 强度。受影响于：Rembrandt van Rijn、荷兰黄金时代绘画传统、chiaroscuro 油画技法、古典 impasto-and-glazing 方法。

Art Deco Gold Highlights: An elegant geometric-illustration style built on crisp, confidently bounded shape divisions arranged with rhythmic, symmetrical precision, sharp radiant linework tracing sunburst and stepped-motif patterning with meticulous graphic discipline, a luminous, reflective sheen concentrated along every decorative edge and contour, a poised, glamorous formality carried through balanced, deliberate composition, and a chic, opulent sophistication that feels streamlined, luxurious, and unmistakably interwar in spirit. Influenced by: Tamara de Lempicka, Art Deco decorative-design tradition, geometric ornamental-illustration technique.
Art Deco Gold Highlights（装饰艺术金色高光）：一种优雅几何插画风格，以 rhythmic、对称精度安排的 crisp、自信 bounded 形状分割，以 meticulous 图形纪律 tracing sunburst 与 stepped-motif 图案的 sharp radiant 线条，集中于每个装饰边与轮廓的 luminous、reflective sheen，通过平衡、deliberate 构图携带的 poised、glamorous 形式感，以及 streamlined、luxurious、精神上浮 interwar 的 chic、opulent 成熟感。受影响于：Tamara de Lempicka、装饰艺术装饰设计传统、几何装饰插画技法。

Ashley Wood Illustration: expressive mixed-media aesthetics combining energetic brushwork, sketch-like spontaneity, painterly abstraction, layered textures, and graphic novel sensibilities, blending raw artistic gesture with sophisticated visual design through atmospheric rendering, controlled chaos, and a distinctly handcrafted appearance that prioritizes mood, emotion, and visual impact over strict realism, reminiscent of Ashley Wood and Bill Sienkiewicz, inspired by World War Robot and Metal Gear Solid concept artwork.
Ashley Wood Illustration（Ashley Wood 插画）：combine 充满能量笔触、sketch 般自发、painterly 抽象、层叠纹理与图形小说感的 expressive 混合媒介美学，通过 atmospheric 渲染、受控 chaos 与 distinctly handcrafted 外观，blend raw 艺术 gesture 与 sophisticated 视觉设计，优先情绪、情感与视觉冲击而非严格写实；令人联想到 Ashley Wood 与 Bill Sienkiewicz，灵感来自《World War Robot》与《潜龙谍影》概念艺术。

Tenebrism: A dramatically shadowed painting style built on an extreme, exaggerated contrast between deep, engulfing darkness and a single sharp pool of illumination, form emerging abruptly from near-total shadow rather than through gradual tonal transition, meticulous, controlled brushwork concentrated entirely within the illuminated passages while shadow remains deliberately unresolved, a theatrical, almost violent immediacy carried through the stark light-dark confrontation, and a haunting, intensely focused gravitas rooted in the most extreme tradition of chiaroscuro painting. Influenced by: Caravaggio, tenebrist painting tradition, Baroque chiaroscuro technique, dramatic single-source oil-painting method.
Tenebrism（暗调主义）：一种 dramatically shadowed 绘画风格，以 deep、engulfing 黑暗与单一 sharp 照明池间的 extreme、exaggerated 对比，从 near-total 阴影而非逐渐色调过渡 abrupt emerge 的形态，concentrated entirely within illuminated 段的 meticulous、受控笔触、而阴影 deliberate unresolved，通过 stark 光暗对抗携带的戏剧性、almost violent 即时性，以及根植最极端 chiaroscuro 绘画传统的 haunting、intensely focused 庄严感。受影响于：卡拉瓦乔、tenebrist 绘画传统、巴洛克 chiaroscuro 技法、戏剧性单源油画方法。

Chiaroscuro: A dramatically balanced painting style built on strong contrast between illuminated and shadowed passages that sculpts form with three-dimensional conviction, a single dominant light source establishing clear hierarchy between what is revealed and what recedes into darkness, meticulous, gradual tonal transition bridging highlight and shadow rather than abrupt separation, a controlled, deliberate modeling that gives every plane convincing volumetric weight, and a masterful, timeless painterly gravitas rooted in centuries of classical oil-painting tradition. Influenced by: Caravaggio, Rembrandt van Rijn, Baroque tonal-painting technique, classical chiaroscuro method.
Chiaroscuro（明暗对照法）：一种 dramatically balanced 绘画风格，以雕塑般 conviction 雕塑形态的 illuminated 与 shadowed 段强对比，建立 revealed 与 recede into 黑暗间清晰层级的单一主导光源，bridging 高光与阴影而非 abrupt 分离的 meticulous、逐渐色调过渡，赋予每个平面可信体积重量的受控、deliberate 建模，以及根植数世纪古典油画传统的 masterful、永恒 painterly 庄严感。受影响于：卡拉瓦乔、Rembrandt van Rijn、巴洛克色调绘画技法、古典 chiaroscuro 方法。

Bill Sienkiewicz Expressionism: highly experimental illustration aesthetics combining expressive brushwork, mixed-media textures, abstract visual fragmentation, painterly distortion, emotional intensity, and unconventional storytelling techniques, blending fine art sensibilities with graphic narrative through bold mark-making, layered surfaces, and a fearless approach to visual interpretation, reminiscent of Bill Sienkiewicz and Ralph Steadman, inspired by Elektra: Assassin and Stray Toasters.
Bill Sienkiewicz Expressionism（Bill Sienkiewicz 表现主义）：combine 表现性笔触、混合媒介纹理、abstract 视觉碎片化、painterly 扭曲、情感强度与 unconventional 叙事技法的高度实验插画美学，通过 bold mark-making、层叠表面与对视觉诠释的 fearless 态度，blend fine art 感与图形叙事；令人联想到 Bill Sienkiewicz 与 Ralph Steadman，灵感来自《Elektra: Assassin》与《Stray Toasters》。

Graffiti Painting: A bold, kinetic street-art illustration style built on dynamic, exaggerated linework with dripping, spray-textured edges that carry raw improvisational energy, confident layered tagging and lettering integrated directly into the composition as rhythmic graphic accents, a rough, unpolished texture that embraces overspray and imperfection as part of its character, aggressive diagonal composition charged with urgent, rebellious momentum, and a raw, unapologetic street-culture intensity that feels loud, immediate, and defiantly handmade. Influenced by: Jean-Michel Basquiat, spray-paint stencil technique, New York subway graffiti tradition, street-art muralism.
Graffiti Painting（涂鸦绘画）：一种 bold、kinetic 街头艺术插画风格，以携带 raw 即兴能量的 dripping、spray-textured 边缘 dynamic、夸张线条，直接 integrated 为 rhythmic 图形 accent 的自信层叠 tagging 与 lettering，embrace overspray 与 imperfection 为特性一部分的 rough、unpolished 纹理，charged urgent、rebellious momentum 的 aggressive 对角构图，以及 loud、即时、defiantly handmade 的 raw、毫不歉疚街头文化强度。受影响于：Jean-Michel Basquiat、喷漆 stencil 技法、纽约地铁涂鸦传统、街头艺术 muralism。

Boris Vallejo Style: A hyper-idealized fantasy painting style built on smooth, airbrushed skin rendering that gives powerfully sculpted anatomy a glossy, almost polished-marble finish, dramatic directional lighting wrapping muscular, statuesque forms in warm heroic glow, meticulously blended tonal transitions that eliminate visible brushwork in favor of glassy precision, confident heroic posing charged with theatrical grandeur, and a bold, glossy, larger-than-life painterly radiance. Influenced by: Boris Vallejo, airbrush fantasy painting technique, heroic fantasy illustration, classical figure painting.
Boris Vallejo Style（Boris Vallejo 风格）：一种 hyper-idealized 奇幻绘画风格，以赋予强力雕塑解剖 glossy、almost polished-marble finish 的平滑喷枪皮肤渲染，以温暖英雄 glow wrap 肌肉、statuesque 形态的戏剧性方向光，eliminate 可见笔触、换取 glassy 精度的 meticulously blended 色调过渡，charged 戏剧性宏伟的自信英雄姿态，以及 bold、glossy、超越现实的 painterly radiance。受影响于：Boris Vallejo、喷枪奇幻绘画技法、英雄奇幻插画、古典人物绘画。

Brushwork Emphasis: painterly aesthetics centered on expressive brush handling, visible mark-making, sophisticated texture variation, dynamic surface energy, and handcrafted visual character, prioritizing artistic gesture and material presence over perfectly smooth rendering while maintaining strong structural cohesion, visual depth, and refined craftsmanship throughout the image, reminiscent of John Singer Sargent and Joaquín Sorolla, inspired by Carnation, Lily, Lily, Rose and Walk on the Beach.
Brushwork Emphasis（笔触强调）：以 expressive 笔触处理、可见 mark-making、sophisticated 纹理变化、dynamic 表面能量与 handcrafted 视觉特征为中心的 painterly 美学，在保持强结构 cohesion、视觉纵深与精炼工艺的同时，优先艺术 gesture 与材质存在而非完美平滑渲染；令人联想到 John Singer Sargent 与 Joaquín Sorolla，灵感来自《康乃馨，百合，百合，玫瑰》与《海滩漫步》。

Pin-Up: A charming, playful glamour-illustration style built on smooth, confident linework tracing idealized, curve-forward proportion with cheerful vitality, soft, evenly blended shading giving skin a flattering, luminous glow, a flirtatious, lighthearted posing sensibility charged with cheeky confidence, meticulous attention to graceful gesture and inviting expression, and a nostalgic, wholesome glamour charm that feels playful, warm, and effortlessly appealing. Influenced by: Gil Elvgren, Alberto Vargas, classic American pin-up illustration tradition, mid-century airbrush glamour technique.
Pin-Up（海报女郎）：一种 charming、俏皮 glamour 插画风格，以 tracing 理想化、curve-forward 比例、带欢快活力的自信、平滑线条，赋予皮肤 flattering、luminous glow 的柔软、均匀 blended 阴影，charged cheeky 自信的 flirtatious、lighthearted 姿态感，对 graceful gesture 与 inviting 表情的 meticulous 关注，以及 playful、warm、毫不费力吸引人的怀旧、wholesome glamour 魅力。受影响于：Gil Elvgren、Alberto Vargas、经典美式海报女郎插画传统、中叶世纪喷枪 glamour 技法。

2020s Pin-Up: A polished, contemporary glamour-illustration style built on smooth digital rendering that blends idealized, curve-forward proportion with a cleaner, high-production finish than its mid-century roots, richly airbrushed shading giving skin a flawless, luminous glow, a confident, self-assured posing sensibility balancing playful charm with modern empowerment, meticulous attention to crisp, contemporary silhouette and expression, and a vibrant, current glamour-illustration polish that feels playful, confident, and unmistakably of-the-moment. Influenced by: contemporary digital pin-up illustration tradition, semi-realistic airbrush-rendering technique, modern glamour-illustration practice, digital-painting portrait method.
2020s Pin-Up（2020年代海报女郎）：一种抛光、当代 glamour 插画风格，以 blend 理想化、curve-forward 比例与比中叶世纪根源更干净、高制作 finish 的平滑数字渲染，赋予皮肤 flawless、luminous glow 的 richly 喷枪阴影，平衡俏皮魅力与现代赋能的自信、self-assured 姿态感，对 crisp、当代剪影与表情的 meticulous 关注，以及 playful、自信、毋庸置疑 of-the-moment 的 vibrant、current glamour 插画抛光感。受影响于：当代数字海报女郎插画传统、半写实喷枪渲染技法、现代 glamour 插画实践、数字绘画肖像方法。

Hyperrealism: A meticulously rendered painting style built on obsessive photographic precision that reproduces every subtle tonal shift and textural nuance with painstaking accuracy, dramatic, controlled lighting establishing convincing sculptural dimensionality throughout, countless layered passes building convincing depth and translucency beneath the outermost extent, a disciplined, patient rendering approach that prioritizes photographic conviction over visible brushwork, and a technically virtuosic intensity that treats absolute fidelity itself as the compositional achievement. Influenced by: Chuck Close, hyperrealist oil-painting tradition, photorealism painting technique, contemporary hyperreal-portraiture practice.
Hyperrealism（超写实）：一种 meticulously 渲染绘画风格，以 painstaking 精度 reproduce 每个微妙色调偏移与纹理 nuance 的偏执照片精度，建立全构图 convincing 雕塑性维度的戏剧性、受控灯光，在最外表层下构建 convincing 纵深与半透明的 countless 层叠 pass，优先照片说服力而非可见笔触的 disciplined、耐心渲染方法，以及将绝对 fidelity 本身视为构图成就的技术 virtuoso 强度。受影响于：Chuck Close、hyperrealist 油画传统、照片写实绘画技法、当代 hyperreal 肖像实践。

Ultrarealism: A meticulously rendered painting style built on hyper-precise attention to minute detail that surpasses ordinary photographic fidelity, dramatic, controlled lighting establishing an almost tangible dimensionality throughout the composition, obsessive layering that builds convincing depth, translucency, and subtle tonal variation, a disciplined, painstaking rendering approach that treats absolute technical mastery as the primary goal, and an uncanny, almost hyper-vivid intensity that pushes representational accuracy beyond what unaided vision typically perceives. Influenced by: hyperrealist and ultrarealist oil-painting tradition, contemporary photorealism technique, meticulous layered-glazing practice, technically obsessive figurative-painting method.
Ultrarealism（超写实主义/极致写实）：一种 meticulously 渲染绘画风格，以 surpass 普通照片保真的 hyper-precise 微观细节关注，建立全构图 almost tangible 维度的戏剧性、受控灯光，构建 convincing 纵深、半透明与微妙色调变化的 obsessive 层叠，将绝对技术 mastery 视为首要目标的 disciplined、painstaking 渲染方法，以及 pushes 具象准确 beyond 无辅助视觉通常感知的 uncanny、almost hyper-vivid 强度。受影响于：hyperrealist 与 ultrarealist 油画传统、当代照片写实技法、meticulous 层叠 glazing 实践、技术偏执人物绘画方法。

Casey Baugh Style: A richly painterly portrait style built on bold, confident brushwork that constructs form through visible, decisive strokes rather than smooth blending, dramatic tonal contrast carving powerful dimensionality across every plane, a loose, expressive looseness at the edges contrasted against tightly resolved focal areas, an emotionally charged, contemplative stillness carried through restrained, moody atmosphere, and a technically masterful, contemporary painterly gravitas that feels both classically grounded and boldly modern. Influenced by: Casey Baugh, contemporary painterly-portrait tradition, alla prima oil-painting technique, classical-contemporary portrait practice.
Casey Baugh Style（Casey Baugh 风格）：一种 richly painterly 肖像风格，以通过可见、decisive strokes 而非平滑混合构建形态的 bold、自信笔触，carving 每个平面强大维度的戏剧性色调对比，与 tightly resolved 焦点区对比的松散、表现性边缘，通过克制、moody 氛围携带的情感 charge、沉思静谧，以及既古典 grounded 又 boldly 现代的技術 masterful、当代 painterly 庄严感。受影响于：Casey Baugh、当代 painterly 肖像传统、alla prima 油画技法、古典-当代肖像实践。

Commercial Illustration: polished professional illustration aesthetics characterized by exceptional visual clarity, refined draftsmanship, sophisticated rendering, strong communication value, and broad audience appeal, emphasizing readability, craftsmanship, and conceptual effectiveness through balanced composition, controlled detail hierarchy, premium surface treatment, and production-ready execution, reminiscent of J.C. Leyendecker and Norman Rockwell, inspired by The Saturday Evening Post and American Illustrators Gallery exhibitions.
Commercial Illustration（商业插画）：以 exceptional 视觉清晰度、精炼造型、sophisticated 渲染、强传达价值与广泛受众吸引力为特征的抛光专业插画美学，通过平衡构图、受控细节层级、premium 表面处理与 production-ready 执行，强调可读性、工艺与概念有效性；令人联想到 J.C. Leyendecker 与 Norman Rockwell，灵感来自《Saturday Evening Post》与美式插画家画廊展览。

Giallo Poster Art: A stylized, high-tension illustration style built on stark, high-contrast graphic composition anchored by a dramatic central figure isolated against bold negative space, illustrative airbrushed rendering charged with lurid, theatrical menace, sharp, angular shadow shapes suggesting concealed threat just beyond the frame, a deliberate tension between glamorous surface polish and gruesome implication lurking beneath, and a stylish, pulpy suspense-cinema intensity built for maximum lurid impact on a theater marquee. Influenced by: Italian giallo film-poster tradition, airbrush illustration technique, pulp-thriller poster design, 1970s European genre-cinema graphic practice.
Giallo Poster Art（意大利 giallo 海报艺术）：一种风格化、高张力插画风格，以 anchored 于 dramatic 中心人物、against bold 负空间的 stark、高对比图形构图，charged lurid、戏剧性 menace 的 illustrative 喷枪渲染，suggest 画框外 concealed 威胁的 sharp、棱角阴影形状，glamorous 表面抛光与潜伏 beneath 的 gruesome implication 间 deliberate 张力，以及为戏院 marquee 最大 lurid 冲击而生的 stylish、pulpy 悬念电影强度。受影响于：意大利 giallo 电影海报传统、喷枪插画技法、pulp-thriller 海报设计、1970 年代欧洲类型电影图形实践。

Grindhouse Poster Illustration: A lurid, exploitation-cinema illustration style built on bold, high-saturation graphic composition anchored by a dramatic, central figure, painterly airbrushed rendering charged with sensationalist, over-the-top intensity, sharp, angular shadow shapes suggesting danger and spectacle in equal measure, a deliberate tension between garish showmanship and gritty pulp menace, and a brash, unapologetically sleazy theatrical energy built for maximum newsstand shock value. Influenced by: 1970s grindhouse film-poster tradition, exploitation-cinema illustration technique, pulp-thriller poster design, airbrush cinematic-poster practice.
Grindhouse Poster Illustration（磨坊影院海报插画）：一种 lurid、exploitation 电影插画风格，以 anchored 于 dramatic 中心人物的 bold、高饱和图形构图，charged sensationalist、over-the-top 强度的 painterly 喷枪渲染，suggest 危险与 spectacle 的 sharp、棱角阴影形状，garish showmanship 与 gritty pulp menace 间 deliberate 张力，以及为最大报摊 shock 价值而生的 brash、毫不歉疚 sleazy 戏剧能量。受影响于：1970 年代 grindhouse 电影海报传统、exploitation 电影插画技法、pulp-thriller 海报设计、喷枪电影海报实践。

Cubism: A fragmented, multi-perspective painting style built on form broken into overlapping angular facets viewed from several vantage points simultaneously, a flattened, deconstructed sense of depth that rejects single-viewpoint realism entirely, confident geometric simplification reducing figure and space alike into intersecting planar shards, a rigorous, analytical restructuring that prioritizes conceptual seeing over optical accuracy, and a bold, intellectually radical visual rhythm rooted in early-20th-century avant-garde rebellion. Influenced by: Pablo Picasso, Georges Braque, Analytic Cubism tradition, early-20th-century avant-garde painting movement.
Cubism（立体主义）：一种 fragmented、多视角绘画风格，以从数个 vantage point simultaneously 观看、broken into overlapping angular facet 的形态，reject 单视点写实的 flattened、deconstructed 纵深感，将人物与空间 alike reduce 为 intersecting planar shard 的自信几何简化，优先概念性 seeing 而非光学准确的 rigorous、分析性重构，以及根植 20 世纪初前卫反叛的 bold、intellectually radical 视觉节奏。受影响于：Pablo Picasso、Georges Braque、分析立体主义传统、20 世纪初前卫绘画运动。

Carne Griffiths Style: A delicately fluid mixed-media illustration style built on flowing, ink-and-wash linework that bleeds and drips with organic unpredictability, translucent layered washes that let forms dissolve into loose atmospheric bloom at the edges, fine calligraphic detail threading through the figure like living linework, a hushed, ethereal fragility carried through delicate transparency, and a poetic, painterly looseness where control and chance blend into something dreamlike and organic. Influenced by: Carne Griffiths, ink and tea-wash illustration technique, mixed-media fine art, botanical linework technique.
Carne Griffiths Style（Carne Griffiths 风格）：一种 delicately fluid 混合媒介插画风格，以 bleed、drip 有机不可预测的 flowing、墨线水洗线条，在边缘 dissolve 为松散 atmospheric bloom 的半透明层叠水洗，thread through 人物如 living 线条的精细书法细节，通过 delicate 透明度携带的轻声、ethereal fragility，以及控制与偶然 blend 为 dreamlike、organic 之物的诗歌、painterly 松散。受影响于：Carne Griffiths、墨与茶洗插画技法、混合媒介 fine art、植物线条技法。

Jackson Pollock Style: A raw, gestural painting style built on sweeping, spontaneous drips and flung strokes that prioritize emotional intensity over representational accuracy, bold physical mark-making where the visible energy of the trajectory becomes the subject itself, dense, layered tangles built through accumulated, unrestrained passes across the entire composition, a chaotic yet deliberate compositional freedom that resists any conventional structure, and a raw, cathartic painterly intensity that channels pure emotional immediacy over polished technique. Influenced by: Jackson Pollock, action-painting technique, drip-painting tradition, mid-century American Abstract Expressionist movement.
Jackson Pollock Style（杰克逊·波洛克风格）：一种 raw、gestural 绘画风格，以 priority 情感强度而非具象准确的 sweeping、自发 drips 与 flung strokes，可见 trajectory 能量本身成为主题的 bold 物理 mark-making，通过 entire 构图 accumulated、unrestrained pass 构建的 dense、层叠 tangles，resist 任何常规结构的 chaotic 却 deliberate 构图自由，以及 channel 纯粹情感即时性而非抛光技法的 raw、cathartic painterly 强度。受影响于：Jackson Pollock、行动绘画技法、滴画传统、中叶世纪美式抽象表现主义运动。

Watercolor Painting: A fluid, translucent painting style built on delicate layered washes that build depth through repeated transparent passes rather than opaque coverage, soft, feathered edges where pigment bleeds and diffuses with organic unpredictability, a luminous quality achieved by letting light pass through thin pigment layers, an unhurried, patient looseness carried through the medium's inherent fluidity, and a poetic, gentle sincerity that captures spontaneity within controlled technical discipline. Influenced by: traditional watercolor-painting tradition, wet-on-wet washing technique, plein-air watercolor practice, classical wash-and-glaze method.
Watercolor Painting（水彩画）：一种 fluid、半透明绘画风格，以通过 repeated 透明 pass 而非不透明覆盖构建纵深的 delicate 层叠水洗，颜料 bleed、diffuse 有机不可预测的柔软、feathered 边缘，让光穿过薄颜料层的 luminous 质感，通过媒介固有 fluidity 携带的从容、耐心松散，以及于受控技术纪律内捕捉自发的诗歌、温柔真诚感。受影响于：传统水彩绘画传统、湿接湿水洗技法、plein-air 水彩实践、古典水洗 glaze 方法。

Contemporary Oil Painting: A richly layered painting style built on confident, visible brushwork that constructs form through deliberate, physically present strokes, meticulous blended tonal transition achieved through wet-on-wet and glazing technique in equal measure, a tactile, hand-crafted depth that preserves brush character even within refined passages, deliberate value and contrast control giving every form convincing dimensional weight, and a timeless yet unmistakably current painterly sincerity that bridges classical technique with a contemporary rendering sensibility. Influenced by: contemporary figurative-oil-painting tradition, classical atelier technique, alla prima painting practice, modern gallery-oil-painting method.
Contemporary Oil Painting（当代油画）：一种 richly layered 绘画风格，以通过 deliberate、physically present strokes 构建形态的自信、可见笔触，通过湿接湿与 glazing 技法 equally 实现的 meticulous blended 色调过渡，即便精炼段也保留笔触特性的 tactile、hand-crafted 纵深，赋予每个形态可信维度重量的 deliberate 明度与对比控制，以及 bridge 古典技法与当代渲染感的永恒却毋庸置疑 current painterly 真诚感。受影响于：当代人物油画传统、古典 atelier 技法、alla prima 绘画实践、现代画廊油画方法。

Pop Art: A bold, graphic illustration style built on flat, punchy color fields separated by crisp confident outline, mechanically repeated dot or halftone patterning standing in for naturalistic shading, a deliberately mass-produced, commercial-print aesthetic replacing painterly nuance with graphic clarity, exaggerated expressive framing that borrows from advertising and comic-panel drama, and a bold, ironic, instantly recognizable commercial-culture punch. Influenced by: Roy Lichtenstein, Andy Warhol, Ben-Day dot printing technique, mid-century advertising illustration.
Pop Art（波普艺术）：一种 bold、图形插画风格，以 crisp 自信轮廓分隔的 flat、punchy 色场，取代自然阴影的机械重复点或半调图案，以图形清晰度取代 painterly 微妙、deliberately mass-produced 商业印刷美学，borrow 自广告与漫画分镜戏剧的夸张表现性取景，以及 bold、ironic、瞬时可辨识的商业文化 punch。受影响于：Roy Lichtenstein、Andy Warhol、本戴点印刷技法、中叶世纪广告插画。

Thick Brushstrokes Painting: A richly physical painting style built on visible, confident strokes carrying real textural weight and directional energy across the entire composition, dense, impasto-like pigment application replacing any smooth or blended transition, dramatic value contrast doing the heavy lifting instead of fine linework, loose, expressive edges that dissolve detail in favor of gestural conviction, and a raw, emotive intensity that celebrates the physical mark of the brush over polished precision. Influenced by: John Singer Sargent, Vincent van Gogh, impasto brushwork technique, alla prima oil-painting method.
Thick Brushstrokes Painting（厚笔触绘画）：一种 richly physical 绘画风格，以携带真实纹理重量与方向能量、跨整个构图的可见、自信 strokes，取代任何平滑或混合过渡的 dense、impasto 般颜料应用，而非细线条做重活的戏剧性明度对比，dissolve 细节、优先 gestural 信念的松散、表现性边缘，以及赞美物理笔触 mark 而非抛光精度的 raw、emotive 强度。受影响于：John Singer Sargent、Vincent van Gogh、impasto 笔触技法、alla prima 油画方法。

Ashcan School Style: A gritty, unromanticized illustration style built on loose, confident brushwork capturing everyday urban life with raw, unpolished honesty, muted, somber tonal range applied with heavy, atmospheric shadow rather than bright clarity, a candid, unglamorous composition favoring social realism over idealized beauty, meticulous attention to expressive, working-class gesture and posture, and a grounded, socially conscious painterly gravitas rooted in early-20th-century American realist rebellion. Influenced by: George Bellows, John Sloan, Ashcan School painting tradition, early-20th-century American Realist movement.
Ashcan School Style（垃圾箱画派风格）：一种 gritty、不浪漫化插画风格，以捕捉 everyday 城市生活、raw、unpolished 诚意的松散、自信笔触，以重、atmospheric 阴影而非明亮清晰度应用的 muted、somber 色调范围，优先社会写实而非理想美、unglamorous 的 candid 构图，对 expressive、working-class gesture 与 posture 的 meticulous 关注，以及根植 20 世纪初美式写实反叛的 grounded、socially conscious painterly 庄严感。受影响于：George Bellows、John Sloan、垃圾箱画派绘画传统、20 世纪初美式写实主义运动。

Charles Tunnicliffe Style: A meticulously observed natural-history illustration style built on precise, confident linework rendering fine detail with taxonomic accuracy, restrained watercolor-adjacent washes applied with quiet, disciplined restraint, a hushed, patient attention to texture and form achieved through careful, unhurried observation, a naturalist's rigor balanced against genuine painterly warmth, and a timeless, scholarly sincerity rooted in decades of dedicated field-illustration craft. Influenced by: Charles Tunnicliffe, British natural-history illustration tradition, wood-engraving-and-watercolor technique, field-guide illustration practice.
Charles Tunnicliffe Style（Charles Tunnicliffe 风格）：一种 meticulously observed 自然史插画风格，以 taxonomic 精度渲染精细细节的精确、自信线条，以 quiet、disciplined 克制应用的 restrained 水彩邻接水洗，通过 careful、从容观察实现的纹理与形态轻声、耐心关注，与 genuine painterly 温暖平衡的 naturalist rigor，以及根植数十年 dedicated 野外插画工艺的永恒、scholarly 真诚感。受影响于：Charles Tunnicliffe、英国自然史插画传统、木刻与水彩技法、野外指南插画实践。

Cloisonnism: A boldly graphic painting style built on flat, confidently bounded color fields separated by thick, dark contour lines functioning like the metal partitions of enamelwork, a deliberate flattening of form that rejects naturalistic shading in favor of pure shape-and-outline construction, simplified, decorative silhouette taking precedence over anatomical nuance, a rhythmic, almost stained-glass compositional clarity carried through confident linear division, and a bold, symbolic graphic intensity rooted in late-19th-century painterly rebellion against academic realism. Influenced by: Émile Bernard, Paul Gauguin, cloisonnist painting tradition, enamel-partition decorative technique.
Cloisonnism（分隔主义/景泰蓝主义）：一种 boldly 图形绘画风格，以厚、dark 轮廓线（如珐琅的 metal partition）分隔的 flat、自信 bounded 色场，reject 自然阴影、优先 pure 形状-轮廓构建的 deliberate 形态 flattening，优先解剖微妙、simplified、装饰剪影，通过自信线性分割携带的 rhythmic、almost 彩绘玻璃构图清晰度，以及根植 19 世纪末反学院写实的 bold、symbolic 图形强度。受影响于：Émile Bernard、Paul Gauguin、cloisonnist 绘画传统、珐琅分区装饰技法。

Salvador Dalí Style: A hallucinatory surrealist painting style built on meticulously photorealistic rendering applied to fundamentally impossible, dreamlike form, melting, elongated distortion warping otherwise precisely modeled elements into uncanny, elastic softness, vast, contemplative negative space stretching into an unsettling, hazy distance, obsessive academic precision paradoxically deployed in service of pure irrational imagery, and a meticulous, uncanny intensity that treats the collision between technical mastery and impossible subject matter as the entire compositional statement. Influenced by: Salvador Dalí, Surrealist painting tradition, classical academic-painting technique, dreamlike hyperrealist practice.
Salvador Dalí Style（萨尔瓦多·达利风格）：一种 hallucinatory 超现实主义绘画风格，以应用于 fundamentally impossible、dreamlike 形态的 meticulously 照片写实渲染，将 otherwise precisely modeled 元素 warp 为 uncanny、elastic 柔软的 melting、拉长扭曲，stretching into unsettling、hazy 距离的 vast、沉思负空间，paradoxically 服务于 pure irrational imagery 的偏执学院精度，以及将技术 mastery 与 impossible 主题碰撞视为整个构图陈述的 meticulous、uncanny 强度。受影响于：Salvador Dalí、超现实主义绘画传统、古典学院绘画技法、dreamlike hyperrealist 实践。

Contemporary Gothic Art: A darkly atmospheric illustration style built on deep, engulfing tenebrist shadow that swallows the edges of every composition, meticulous ornamental detail rendered with obsessive, moody precision, luminous, porcelain-pale skin contrasted against densely rendered dark ornamentation, a hushed, melancholic stillness carried through restrained directional glow, and a haunting, romantic intensity that feels both reverent and quietly unsettling. Influenced by: Marta Bevacqua, Andrew Ferez, contemporary dark-portrait photography practice, gothic-romantic digital-painting technique.
Contemporary Gothic Art（当代哥特艺术）：一种 darkly atmospheric 插画风格，以吞噬每构图边缘的 deep、engulfing tenebrist 阴影，以偏执、moody 精度渲染的 meticulous 装饰细节，与 densely rendered 黑暗装饰对比的 luminous、porcelain-pale 皮肤，通过克制方向 glow 携带的轻声、melancholic 静谧，以及既 reverent 又 quietly unsettling 的 haunting、romantic 强度。受影响于：Marta Bevacqua、Andrew Ferez、当代黑暗肖像摄影实践、哥特浪漫数字绘画技法。

Norman Rockwell Style: A warm, narrative-driven illustration style built on meticulous, brushy realism with sincere, storytelling detail, confident tonal modeling giving convincing dimensional presence, a nostalgic, wholesome sensibility carried through carefully staged, relatable composition, richly rendered expression and gesture conveying gentle humor or quiet emotion, and a timeless, homespun sincerity that feels deeply American, comforting, and effortlessly narrative. Influenced by: Norman Rockwell, Golden Age American illustration tradition, oil-painting technique, magazine-cover narrative-illustration practice.
Norman Rockwell Style（诺曼·洛克威尔风格）：一种 warm、叙事驱动插画风格，以 sincere、storytelling 细节的 meticulous、brushy 写实，赋予 convincing 维度存在的自信色调建模，通过 carefully staged、relatable 构图携带的怀旧、wholesome 感，传达 gentle 幽默或 quiet 情感的 richly 渲染表情与 gesture，以及 deeply American、comforting、毫不费力叙事的永恒、homespun 真诚感。受影响于：Norman Rockwell、黄金时代美式插画传统、油画技法、杂志封面叙事插画实践。

Edvard Munch Style: A raw, emotionally charged painting style built on sweeping, wave-like brushwork that distorts form into expressive, undulating curves, an anxious, unsettled compositional rhythm carried through swirling, restless linework, a limited, heavy tonal range weighted toward oppressive darkness broken by isolated, searing highlight, dramatic elongation and distortion of figure conveying raw psychological turmoil, and a haunting, deeply personal painterly intensity that feels visceral, unsettled, and quietly overwhelming. Influenced by: Edvard Munch, Expressionist painting tradition, symbolist figurative-painting technique, emotionally driven brushwork practice.
Edvard Munch Style（爱德华·蒙克风格）：一种 raw、情感 charge 绘画风格，以 distort 形态为 expressive、undulating 曲线的 sweeping、wave-like 笔触，通过 swirling、restless 线条携带的 anxious、unsettled 构图节奏，weighted toward oppressive 黑暗、被 isolated、searing 高光打破的 limited、heavy 色调范围，convey raw 心理 turmoil 的戏剧性拉长与扭曲，以及 visceral、unsettled、quietly overwhelming 的 haunting、deeply personal painterly 强度。受影响于：Edvard Munch、表现主义绘画传统、symbolist 人物绘画技法、情感驱动笔触实践。

Modern Klimt-Inspired Flat Collage Art: A richly ornamental illustration style built on dense, mosaic-like flat shapes assembled together in place of naturalistic modeling, a deliberately flattened sense of depth that abandons perspective entirely in favor of a single unified picture-extent, meticulous decorative patterning applied with mathematical, tessellated precision across every region, a hushed, reverent stillness carried through symmetrical, deliberate composition, and a luminous, contemporary decorative elegance that bridges early-20th-century ornamental painting with a modern collage-driven graphic sensibility. Influenced by: Gustav Klimt, contemporary flat-collage illustration technique, tessellated decorative-pattern practice, Byzantine mosaic-adjacent compositional method.
Modern Klimt-Inspired Flat Collage Art（现代克里姆特启发平涂拼贴艺术）：一种 richly ornamental 插画风格，以 assemble together 取代自然建模的 dense、mosaic-like flat 形状，完全 abandon 透视、优先单一 unified 画面范围的 deliberately flattened 纵深感，跨每个区域以数学、tessellated 精度应用的 meticulous 装饰图案，通过对称、deliberate 构图携带的轻声、reverent 静谧，以及 bridge 20 世纪初 ornamental 绘画与当代 collage 驱动图形感的 luminous、当代装饰优雅。受影响于：Gustav Klimt、当代平涂拼贴插画技法、tessellated 装饰图案实践、拜占庭 mosaic 邻接构图方法。

Henri Matisse Style: A bold, joyful painting style built on flat, confidently bounded color fields applied with expressive, almost naive simplicity, dynamic organic cut-shape composition favoring rhythmic decorative pattern over naturalistic modeling, a liberated, unmodulated brushwork that celebrates pure color relationship over blended tone, a lively, compositional energy carried through curving, simplified form, and a joyful, painterly intensity that feels free, vibrant, and unmistakably modernist. Influenced by: Henri Matisse, Fauvism painting tradition,
Henri Matisse Style（亨利·马蒂斯风格）：一种 bold、joyful 绘画风格，以 expressive、almost naive 简单应用的 flat、自信 bounded 色场，优先 rhythmic 装饰图案而非自然建模的 dynamic organic cut-shape 构图，celebrate pure 色彩关系而非 blended 色调的 liberated、unmodulated 笔触，通过 curving、simplified 形态携带的 lively、构图能量，以及 free、vibrant、毋庸置疑 modernist 的 joyful、painterly 强度。受影响于：Henri Matisse、野兽派绘画传统。

Andy Warhol Style: A bold, flat pop-art illustration style built on high-contrast silkscreen-adjacent color-block construction, a mechanical, offset-print flatness, and a bold, ironic, instantly recognizable icon intensity rooted in mid-century pop-art tradition. Influenced by: Andy Warhol, silkscreen-printing technique, pop-art movement tradition, mass-media art,
Andy Warhol Style（安迪·沃霍尔风格）：一种 bold、flat 波普艺术插画风格，以高对比 silkscreen 邻接色块构建、mechanical、offset-print flatness，以及根植中叶世纪波普艺术传统的 bold、ironic、瞬时可辨识 icon 强度。受影响于：Andy Warhol、silkscreen 印刷技法、波普艺术运动传统、大众媒体艺术。

Impasto: A richly physical painting style built on thickly applied pigment that stands proud from the working surface, ridges and peaks of dried medium catching real directional light with sculptural dimensionality, confident, decisive strokes carrying visible weight and texture across the entire composition, dramatic tactile depth replacing smooth blending or flat coverage entirely, and a raw, sculptural painterly intensity that celebrates the physical presence of pigment itself. Influenced by: Vincent van Gogh, Lucian Freud, impasto oil-painting technique, expressive thick-pigment method.
Impasto（厚涂/厚堆颜料）：一种 richly physical 绘画风格，以 stand proud from 工作表面的厚涂颜料，dried 媒介的 ridge 与 peak 以雕塑性维度 catch 真实方向光，跨整个构图携带可见重量与纹理的自信、decisive strokes，完全取代平滑混合或 flat 覆盖的戏剧性 tactile 纵深，以及赞美颜料物理存在本身的 raw、雕塑性 painterly 强度。受影响于：Vincent van Gogh、Lucian Freud、impasto 油画技法、表现性厚颜料方法。
